<file path="src/api/axios.ts">
import axios from "axios"
import { useAuthStore } from "@/store/authStore"

export const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: "http://localhost:8080",
  headers: {
    "Content-Type": "application/json",
  },
})

// Tự động gắn Bearer token vào mọi request (nếu đã đăng nhập)
api.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = useAuthStore.getState().accessToken
  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  }
  return config
})

// Nếu token hết hạn / không hợp lệ (401) -> tự đăng xuất
api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      useAuthStore.getState().logout()
      window.location.href = "/login"
    }
    return Promise.reject(error)
  }
)
</file>

import { api } from "./axios"
import type { Book, SyllabusBook, CreateSyllabusBookRequest, CreateBookRequest, Plo } from "@/types/book"

export const bookApi = {
  getAll: async (): Promise<Book[]> => {
    const res = await api.get<Book[]>("/api/books")
    return res.data
  },

  create: async (data: CreateBookRequest): Promise<Book> => {
    const res = await api.post<Book>("/api/books", data)
    return res.data
  },

  delete: async (id: number): Promise<void> => {
    await api.delete(`/api/books/${id}`)
  },
}

export const syllabusBookApi = {
  getBySyllabus: async (syllabusId: number): Promise<SyllabusBook[]> => {
    const res = await api.get<SyllabusBook[]>(`/api/syllabus-books/syllabus/${syllabusId}`)
    return res.data
  },

  create: async (data: CreateSyllabusBookRequest): Promise<SyllabusBook> => {
    const res = await api.post<SyllabusBook>("/api/syllabus-books", data)
    return res.data
  },

  delete: async (syllabusId: number, bookId: number): Promise<void> => {
    await api.delete(`/api/syllabus-books/${syllabusId}/${bookId}`)
  },
}

export const ploApi = {

  getByProgram: async (programId: number): Promise<Plo[]> => {

<file path="src/api/classSectionApi.ts">
import { api } from "./axios"

export interface ClassSectionResponse {
  id: number
  courseId: number
  courseCode: string
  courseName: string
  syllabusId: number
  syllabusVersionNumber: number
  instructorId: number
  instructorName: string
  semester: number
  academicYear: string
  groupNumber: number
  labGroup: number
  maxStudents: number
  room: string
  schedule: string
  sectionType: "THEORY" | "LAB" | "PRACTICE"
  isActive: boolean
}

export interface CreateClassSectionRequest {
  courseId: number
  syllabusId: number
  instructorId: number
  semester: number
  academicYear: string
  groupNumber: number
  labGroup?: number
  maxStudents?: number
  room?: string
  schedule?: string
  sectionType: "THEORY" | "LAB" | "PRACTICE"
  isActive?: boolean
}

export const getClassSections = async (): Promise<ClassSectionResponse[]> => {
  const { data } = await api.get("/api/class-sections")
  return data
}

export const getClassSectionById = async (id: number): Promise<ClassSectionResponse> => {
  const { data } = await api.get(`/api/class-sections/${id}`)
  return data
}

export const getSectionsByCourse = async (courseId: number): Promise<ClassSectionResponse[]> => {
  const { data } = await api.get(`/api/class-sections/course/${courseId}`)
  return data
}

export const searchClassSections = async (q: string): Promise<ClassSectionResponse[]> => {
  const { data } = await api.get(`/api/class-sections/search`, { params: { q } })
  return data
}

export const createClassSection = async (req: CreateClassSectionRequest): Promise<ClassSectionResponse> => {
  const { data } = await api.post("/api/class-sections", req)
  return data
}

export const updateClassSection = async ({
  id,
  req,
}: {
  id: number
  req: CreateClassSectionRequest
}): Promise<ClassSectionResponse> => {
  const { data } = await api.put(`/api/class-sections/${id}`, req)
  return data
}

export const deleteClassSection = async (id: number): Promise<void> => {
  await api.delete(`/api/class-sections/${id}`)
}

import { api } from './axios';

export interface DashboardDeanResponse {
  totalSyllabus: number;
  approvedSyllabus: number;
  pendingSyllabus: number;
  rejectedSyllabus: number;
  statusByMajor: { majorCode: string; total: number; approved: number }[];
  creditDistribution: { courseType: string; totalCredits: number }[];
}

export interface DashboardDeptHeadResponse {
  totalCoursesInDept: number;
  syllabusesToReview: number;
  coursesStatus: { courseCode: string; courseName: string; instructorName: string; status: string }[];
}

export interface DashboardFacultyResponse {
  assignedCourses: number;
  completedSyllabuses: number;
  pendingSyllabuses: number;
  upcomingDeadlines: { courseCode: string; courseName: string; status: string; deadline: string }[];
}

export interface DashboardAdminResponse {
  totalUsers: number;
  totalPrograms: number;
  totalCourses: number;
  totalDepartments: number;
  totalSyllabuses: number;
  facultiesNotSubmitted: { instructorName: string; email: string; missingCount: number }[];
}

export interface DashboardHeatmapResponse {
  programCode: string;
  plos: string[];
  courseCoverages: { courseCode: string; coverageLevels: (string | null)[] }[];
}

export const dashboardApi = {
  getDeanDashboard: () => api.get<DashboardDeanResponse>('/api/dashboard/dean').then(res => res.data),
  getDeptHeadDashboard: (userId: number) => api.get<DashboardDeptHeadResponse>(`/api/dashboard/dept-head/${userId}`).then(res => res.data),
  getFacultyDashboard: (userId: number) => api.get<DashboardFacultyResponse>(`/api/dashboard/faculty/${userId}`).then(res => res.data),
  getAdminDashboard: () => api.get<DashboardAdminResponse>('/api/dashboard/admin').then(res => res.data),
  getHeatmapCoverage: (programId: number) => api.get<DashboardHeatmapResponse>(`/api/dashboard/heatmap/${programId}`).then(res => res.data),
};

  },

<file path="src/api/syllabusApi.ts">
import { api } from "./axios"
import type {
  CreateSyllabusRequest,
  Syllabus,
} from "@/types/syllabus"

const normalizeSyllabusPayload = (
  data: CreateSyllabusRequest
): CreateSyllabusRequest => {
  return {
    ...data,

    courseId: Number(data.courseId),
    versionNumber: Number(data.versionNumber || 1),
    versionLabel: data.versionLabel || "v1.0",
    academicYear: data.academicYear || "K24",
    createdBy: Number(data.createdBy || 1),
    changeSummary: data.changeSummary ?? "",
    notes: data.notes ?? "",

    clos: data.clos ?? [],
    topics: data.topics ?? [],
    assessments: data.assessments ?? [],
  }
}

export const syllabusApi = {
  getAll: async (): Promise<Syllabus[]> => {
    const response = await api.get("/api/syllabuses")
    return response.data
  },

  getById: async (id: number): Promise<Syllabus> => {
    const response = await api.get(`/api/syllabuses/${id}`)
    return response.data
  },

  getByCourse: async (courseId: number): Promise<Syllabus[]> => {
    const response = await api.get(`/api/syllabuses/course/${courseId}`)
    return response.data
  },

  getByStatus: async (status: string): Promise<Syllabus[]> => {
    const response = await api.get(`/api/syllabuses/status/${status}`)
    return response.data
  },

  create: async (
    data: CreateSyllabusRequest
  ): Promise<Syllabus> => {
    const payload = normalizeSyllabusPayload(data)
    const response = await api.post("/api/syllabuses", payload)
    return response.data
  },

  update: async (
    id: number,
    data: CreateSyllabusRequest
  ): Promise<Syllabus> => {
    const payload = normalizeSyllabusPayload(data)
    const response = await api.put(`/api/syllabuses/${id}`, payload)
    return response.data
  },

  delete: async (id: number): Promise<void> => {
    await api.delete(`/api/syllabuses/${id}`)
  },

  submit: async (id: number): Promise<Syllabus> => {
    const response = await api.post(`/api/syllabuses/${id}/submit`)
    return response.data
  },

  approve: async (
    id: number,
    approverId: number
  ): Promise<Syllabus> => {
    const response = await api.post(
      `/api/syllabuses/${id}/approve`,
      null,
      {
        params: { approverId },
      }
    )
    return response.data
  },

  reject: async (
    id: number,
    approverId: number
  ): Promise<Syllabus> => {
    const response = await api.post(
      `/api/syllabuses/${id}/reject`,
      null,
      {
        params: { approverId },
      }
    )
    return response.data
  },

  getDiff: async (
    id: number,
    compareWith: number
  ): Promise<any> => {
    const response = await api.get(`/api/syllabuses/${id}/diff`, {
      params: { compareWith },
    })
    return response.data
  },

  clone: async (
    id: number,
    userId: number
  ): Promise<Syllabus> => {
    const response = await api.post(
      `/api/syllabuses/${id}/clone`,
      null,
      {
        params: { userId },
      }
    )
    return response.data
  },
}
</file>

<file path="src/api/topicApi.ts">
import { api } from "./axios"
import type { Topic, CreateTopicRequest } from "@/types/topic"

export const topicApi = {
  getBySyllabus: async (syllabusId: number): Promise<Topic[]> => {
    const res = await api.get<Topic[]>(`/api/topics/syllabus/${syllabusId}`)
    return res.data
  },

  create: async (data: CreateTopicRequest): Promise<Topic> => {
    const res = await api.post<Topic>("/api/topics", data)
    return res.data
  },

  update: async (id: number, data: CreateTopicRequest): Promise<Topic> => {
    const res = await api.put<Topic>(`/api/topics/${id}`, data)
    return res.data
  },

  delete: async (id: number): Promise<void> => {
    await api.delete(`/api/topics/${id}`)
  },
}
</file>

<file path="src/api/userApi.ts">
import { api } from "@/api/axios"
import type {
  CreateUserRequest,
  UpdateUserRequest,
  UserAccountResponse,
} from "@/types/user"

const BASE = "/api/admin/users"

export async function getUsers(): Promise<UserAccountResponse[]> {
  const { data } = await api.get<UserAccountResponse[]>(BASE)
  return data
}

export async function createUser(
  payload: CreateUserRequest
): Promise<UserAccountResponse> {
  const { data } = await api.post<UserAccountResponse>(BASE, payload)
  return data
}

export async function updateUser(
  id: number,
  payload: UpdateUserRequest
): Promise<UserAccountResponse> {
  const { data } = await api.put<UserAccountResponse>(`${BASE}/${id}`, payload)
  return data
}

export async function toggleUserActive(
  id: number
): Promise<UserAccountResponse> {
  const { data } = await api.patch<UserAccountResponse>(
    `${BASE}/${id}/toggle-active`
  )
  return data
}

export async function deleteUser(id: number): Promise<void> {
  await api.delete(`${BASE}/${id}`)
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/curriculum/CourseCard.tsx">
import React from 'react';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Edit2, Info } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export interface CourseCardProps {
  id: number;
  courseCode: string;
  courseName: string;
  courseTypeName: string;
  onEdit: () => void;
  onClick: () => void;
}

// Map course types to colors like the image
const getColorByType = (type: string) => {
  const t = type.toLowerCase();
  if (t.includes('general')) return 'bg-blue-400 text-white';
  if (t.includes('core')) return 'bg-yellow-400 text-black';
  if (t.includes('specialized') && t.includes('required')) return 'bg-green-500 text-white';
  if (t.includes('specialized') && t.includes('elective')) return 'bg-green-300 text-black';
  if (t.includes('research') || t.includes('practical')) return 'bg-orange-500 text-white';
  if (t.includes('elective')) return 'bg-green-300 text-black'; // fallback
  return 'bg-gray-200 text-black';
};

export function CourseCard({
  id,
  courseCode,
  courseName,
  courseTypeName,
  onEdit,
  onClick,
}: CourseCardProps) {
  const colorClass = getColorByType(courseTypeName);

  return (
    <Card 
      id={`course-${id}`}
      className={cn(
        "relative w-full p-3 shadow-md rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-105 group border-2 border-slate-700/20",
        colorClass
      )}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase opacity-80 mb-1">{courseCode}</div>
      <div className="text-sm font-bold leading-tight px-1">{courseName}</div>

      {/* Hover action overlay */}
      <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex space-x-1">
        <Button
          size="icon"
          variant="secondary"
          className="h-6 w-6 bg-white text-black hover:bg-gray-100 shadow"
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onEdit();
          }}
          title="Edit placement"
        >
          <Edit2 className="h-3 w-3" />
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}
</file>

import React from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { courseApi } from "@/api/courseApi";
import { syllabusApi } from "@/api/syllabusApi";

interface CourseDetailsModalProps {
  courseId: number | null;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
}

export function CourseDetailsModal({
  courseId,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: CourseDetailsModalProps) {
  // Fetch course data
  const { data: course, isLoading: isCourseLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["course", courseId],
    queryFn: () => courseApi.getById(courseId!),
    enabled: !!courseId && open,
  });

  // Fetch syllabuses for this course to find the current one
  const { data: syllabuses, isLoading: isSyllabusLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["syllabuses", "course", courseId],
    queryFn: () => syllabusApi.getByCourse(courseId!),
    enabled: !!courseId && open,
  });

  const isLoading = isCourseLoading || isSyllabusLoading;
  const currentSyllabus = syllabuses?.find((s) => s.isCurrent) || syllabuses?.[0];

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-2xl max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="text-2xl border-b pb-2">
            Course Syllabus: {course?.name || "Loading..."}
          </DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="flex justify-center p-8">Loading course details...</div>
        ) : !course ? (
          <div className="p-4 text-red-500">Course not found.</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div className="text-center">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">COURSE SYLLABUS</h2>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Course Name: {course.name}</h3>
              <p className="text-md">Course Code: <strong>{course.courseCode}</strong></p>
            </div>

            <section>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-lg mb-2">1. General information</h4>
              <table className="w-full border-collapse border border-slate-300 text-sm">
                <tbody>
                  <tr>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2 font-semibold w-1/3 bg-slate-50">
                      Course designation
                    </td>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2 italic text-slate-700">
                      {course.description || "No description provided."}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                  <tr>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2 font-semibold bg-slate-50">
                      Semester(s) in which the course is taught
                    </td>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2">
                      Depends on curriculum mapping
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                  <tr>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2 font-semibold bg-slate-50">
                      Person responsible for the course
                    </td>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2">
                      {currentSyllabus?.createdByFullName || "N/A"}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                  <tr>

                      Credit points
                    </td>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2">
                      {course.totalCredits} (Theory: {course.creditTheory}, Lab: {course.creditLab})
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                  <tr>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2 font-semibold bg-slate-50">
                      Relation to curriculum
                    </td>
                    <td className="border border-slate-300 p-2">
                      {course.departmentName}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </section>

              <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 text-right">

              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-sm text-yellow-600 bg-yellow-50 p-3 rounded">
                No syllabus document has been created for this course yet.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/curriculum/CourseNode.tsx">
import React from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export interface CourseNodeProps {
  id: string; // DOM ID for Xarrow
  courseName: string;
  credits: number;
  courseTypes: string[]; // e.g., ["General knowledge"], ["Specialized knowledge"]
  onClick?: () => void;
}

export function CourseNode({ id, courseName, credits, courseTypes, onClick }: CourseNodeProps) {
  // Determine color based on courseTypes
  let bgClass = "bg-slate-200 border-slate-400"
  
  if (courseTypes?.includes("General knowledge") || courseTypes?.includes("General")) {
    bgClass = "bg-[#00b0f0] border-sky-600 shadow-[inset_0_0_10px_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)]"
  } else if (courseTypes?.includes("Fundamental") || courseTypes?.includes("Core")) {
    bgClass = "bg-[#ffff00] border-yellow-500 shadow-[inset_0_0_10px_rgba(255,255,255,0.5)]"
  } else if (courseTypes?.includes("Specialized knowledge") || courseTypes?.includes("Specialized")) {
    // If courseName contains "Elective", make it light green, else dark green
    if (courseName.toLowerCase().includes("elective")) {
      bgClass = "bg-[#92d050] border-green-600 shadow-[inset_0_0_10px_rgba(255,255,255,0.3)]"
    } else {
      bgClass = "bg-[#00b050] border-green-700 shadow-[inset_0_0_10px_rgba(255,255,255,0.3)]"
    }
  } else if (courseTypes?.includes("Internship/Project/Thesis") || courseTypes?.includes("Research") || courseTypes?.includes("Others")) {
    bgClass = "bg-[#ffc000] border-orange-600 shadow-[inset_0_0_10px_rgba(255,255,255,0.3)]"
  }

  return (
    <div
      id={id}
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        "relative flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-3 text-center cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-105 z-10",
        "w-44 min-h-[70px] rounded-full border border-b-4 border-r-2", // Pill shape with 3D button effect
        "text-slate-900 text-xs font-semibold leading-tight",
        bgClass
      )}
      style={{
        boxShadow: "2px 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.15), inset -2px -2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)",
      }}
    >
      <div className="line-clamp-3 px-2">
        {courseName} ({credits}C)
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/layout/Header.tsx">
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"
import { LogOut, User, Bell, CheckCircle } from "lucide-react"
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { useAuthStore } from "@/store/authStore"
import { ROLE_LABEL } from "@/config/navConfig"
import { notificationApi } from "@/api/notificationApi"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"

export default function Header() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuthStore()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()

  function handleLogout() {
    logout()
    navigate("/login")
  }

  // Fetch notifications
  const { data: notifications = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["notifications", user?.userId],
    queryFn: () => notificationApi.getUserNotifications(user?.userId ?? 0),
    enabled: !!user?.userId,
    refetchInterval: 30000, // auto refetch every 30s
  })

  const markAsReadMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: number) => notificationApi.markAsRead(id),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["notifications", user?.userId] })
    },
  })

  const markAllAsReadMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => notificationApi.markAllAsRead(user?.userId ?? 0),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["notifications", user?.userId] })
    },
  })

  if (!user) return null

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.isRead).length

  return (
    <header className="h-16 border-b border-border bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-6 sticky top-0 z-10">
      <div />
      <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
        {/* Notification Bell */}
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="relative">
              <Bell className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-600" />
              {unreadCount > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-500 text-[10px] font-bold text-white">
                  {unreadCount > 9 ? "9+" : unreadCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-80">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-2">
              <DropdownMenuLabel className="font-semibold text-base p-0">Thông báo</DropdownMenuLabel>
              {unreadCount > 0 && (
                <Button 
                  variant="ghost" 
                  size="sm" 
                  className="h-auto p-0 text-xs text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700 hover:bg-transparent"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    markAllAsReadMutation.mutate();
                  }}
                  disabled={markAllAsReadMutation.isPending}
                >
                  Đánh dấu đã đọc
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            <div className="max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
              {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-4 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Không có thông báo nào.
                </div>
              ) : (
                notifications.slice(0, 10).map((n) => (
                  <DropdownMenuItem 
                    key={n.id} 
                    className={`flex flex-col items-start p-4 cursor-pointer gap-1 border-b last:border-0 ${!n.isRead ? 'bg-blue-50/50' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (!n.isRead) {
                        markAsReadMutation.mutate(n.id)
                      }
                      // Navigate logic can go here (e.g. to approvals page)
                      if (n.type === 'SYLLABUS_SUBMITTED') navigate('/dept-head/approvals');
                    }}
                  >
                    <div className="flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className={`text-sm font-medium ${!n.isRead ? 'text-slate-900' : 'text-slate-600'}`}>
                        {n.title}
                      </span>
                      {!n.isRead && <div className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-blue-600 mt-1.5 shrink-0" />}
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                      {n.message}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">
                      {new Date(n.createdAt).toLocaleString('vi-VN')}
                    </span>
                  </DropdownMenuItem>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>

        {/* User Info */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600 border-l pl-6">
          <User className="h-4 w-4" />
          <span className="font-medium text-brand-900">{user.username}</span>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">
            · {ROLE_LABEL[user.role]}
          </span>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={handleLogout} className="ml-2">
            <LogOut className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
            Đăng xuất
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { NavLink, useLocation } from "react-router-dom"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { NAV_CONFIG } from "@/config/navConfig"
import { useAuthStore } from "@/store/authStore"
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react"

export default function Sidebar() {
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const location = useLocation()
  
  // State to track which parent menu is expanded
  const [expandedMenus, setExpandedMenus] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({})

  if (!user) return null

  const items = NAV_CONFIG[user.role]

  const toggleMenu = (path: string) => {
    setExpandedMenus((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [path]: !prev[path]
    }))
  }

  // Helper to check if a parent route is active (to keep it open/highlighted)
  const isParentActive = (itemPath: string, children?: { path: string }[]) => {
    if (location.pathname === itemPath) return true
    if (children && children.some((c) => location.pathname === c.path)) return true
    return false
  }

  return (
    <aside className="w-64 shrink-0 h-screen bg-brand-900 text-white flex flex-col">
      <div className="px-5 py-5 border-b border-white/10">
        <p className="font-bold text-lg leading-tight">SCSE • IU</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-brand-100">Curriculum Management</p>
      </div>

      <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-4 px-2 space-y-1">
        {items.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon
          const hasChildren = !!item.children && item.children.length > 0
          const active = isParentActive(item.path, item.children)
          const isExpanded = expandedMenus[item.path] || active

          if (hasChildren) {
            return (
              <div key={item.path} className="space-y-1">
                <button
                  onClick={() => toggleMenu(item.path)}
                  className={cn(
                    "w-full flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors",
                    active
                      ? "bg-brand-700/50 text-white font-medium"
                      : "text-brand-100 hover:bg-white/10"
                  )}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{item.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  {isExpanded ? (
                    <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" />
                  ) : (
                    <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                  )}
                </button>
                {isExpanded && (
                  <div className="pl-9 pr-2 space-y-1 mt-1">
                    {item.children!.map((child) => (
                      <NavLink
                        key={child.path}
                        to={child.path}
                        end
                        className={({ isActive }) =>
                          cn(
                            "block rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors",
                            isActive
                              ? "bg-brand-700 text-white font-medium"
                              : "text-brand-100 hover:bg-white/10"
                          )
                        }
                      >
                        {child.label}
                      </NavLink>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          }

          return (
            <NavLink
              key={item.path}
              to={item.path}
              end={item.path === `/${item.path.split("/")[1]}`}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                cn(
                  "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors",
                  isActive
                    ? "bg-brand-700 text-white font-medium"
                    : "text-brand-100 hover:bg-white/10"
                )
              }
            >
              <Icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{item.label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          )
        })}
      </nav>
    </aside>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/syllabus/editor/tabs/Section1GeneralInfo.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

import { FileText, Info, Save } from "lucide-react"
import type { Syllabus } from "@/types/syllabus"
import { useUpdateSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useUpdateSyllabus"

const ACADEMIC_YEARS = [
  "2022-2023", "2023-2024", "2024-2025", "2025-2026", "2026-2027", "2027-2028",
]

const SEMESTERS = [
  { value: "1", label: "Học kỳ 1" },

  { value: "Bilingual", label: "Song ngữ (Bilingual)" },
]

interface Props {

export default function Section1GeneralInfo({ syllabus, readOnly = false }: Props) {

  const readOnlyClass = "bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 cursor-default"

              <div className="h-10 px-3 flex items-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-sm font-mono font-semibold text-slate-700">
                {syllabus.courseCode}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Tên môn học</Label>
              <div className="h-10 px-3 flex items-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-sm text-slate-700">
                {syllabus.courseName}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Giảng viên soạn</Label>
              <div className="h-10 px-3 flex items-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-sm text-slate-700">
                {syllabus.createdByUsername}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Trạng thái</Label>
              <div className="h-10 px-3 flex items-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
                <Badge className={`text-xs font-semibold ${
                  syllabus.status === "APPROVED" ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700" :
                  syllabus.status === "SUBMITTED" ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700" :
                  syllabus.status === "REJECTED" ? "bg-rose-100 text-rose-700" :
                  "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"
                }`}>
                  {syllabus.status === "APPROVED" ? "Đã duyệt" :
                   syllabus.status === "SUBMITTED" ? "Đã nộp" :
                   syllabus.status === "REJECTED" ? "Bị từ chối" : "Bản nháp"}
                </Badge>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Editable metadata */}
      <Card className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
          <CardTitle className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2">
            <FileText className="size-4 text-primary" />
            Thông tin phiên bản đề cương
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="pt-4 space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Năm học áp dụng <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </Label>
              {readOnly ? (
                <div className={`h-10 px-3 flex items-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm ${readOnlyClass}`}>
                  {form.academicYear}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <Select value={form.academicYear} onValueChange={(v) => setForm({ ...form, academicYear: v })}>
                  <SelectTrigger className={fieldClass}>
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn năm học..." />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {ACADEMIC_YEARS.map(y => (
                      <SelectItem key={y} value={y}>Năm học {y}</SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Nhãn phiên bản <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </Label>
              <Input
                className={`${fieldClass} ${readOnly ? readOnlyClass : ""}`}
                readOnly={readOnly}
                placeholder="v1.0, v2024.1..."
                value={form.versionLabel}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, versionLabel: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Số phiên bản
              </Label>
              <div className={`h-10 px-3 flex items-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm ${readOnlyClass}`}>
                v{syllabus.versionNumber}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Tóm tắt thay đổi so với kỳ trước
              </Label>
              <textarea
                className={`w-full min-h-[90px] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-slate-400
                  ${readOnly ? readOnlyClass + " resize-none" : "bg-white focus:border-primary/60"}`}
                readOnly={readOnly}
                placeholder="Mô tả những điểm thay đổi chính so với phiên bản trước..."
                value={form.changeSummary}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, changeSummary: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Ghi chú nội bộ
              </Label>
              <textarea
                className={`w-full min-h-[90px] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-slate-400
                  ${readOnly ? readOnlyClass + " resize-none" : "bg-white focus:border-primary/60"}`}
                readOnly={readOnly}
                placeholder="Ghi chú thêm cho trưởng bộ môn / hội đồng..."
                value={form.notes}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, notes: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {!readOnly && (
            <div className="flex justify-end pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
              <Button
                onClick={handleSave}
                disabled={updateMutation.isPending}
                className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 gap-2 h-9 text-sm shadow-sm"
              >
                <Save className="size-4" />
                {updateMutation.isPending ? "Đang lưu..." : "Lưu thông tin chung"}
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Approval info if applicable */}
      {(syllabus.approvedByUsername || syllabus.approvedAt) && (
        <Card className="border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/40 shadow-sm">
          <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-4">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <div className="size-9 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <span className="text-emerald-700 text-lg">✓</span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-800">Đề cương đã được duyệt</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-emerald-600 mt-0.5">
                  Duyệt bởi <strong>{syllabus.approvedByUsername}</strong>
                  {syllabus.approvedAt && (
                    <> · Ngày {new Date(syllabus.approvedAt).toLocaleDateString("vi-VN")}</>
                  )}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/syllabus/editor/tabs/Section3CLOs.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"
import {
  AlertCircle,
  GripVertical,
  ListChecks,
  Loader2,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
} from "lucide-react"
import { useClos, useCreateClo, useDeleteClo } from "@/hooks/useClos"
import { BLOOM_LEVELS, COMPETENCY_LEVELS } from "@/types/clo"
import type { Clo } from "@/types/clo"

interface Props {
  syllabusId: number
  readOnly?: boolean
}

interface CloFormState {
  code: string
  description: string
  descriptionVn: string
  bloomLevel: string
  competencyLevel: string
}

const emptyForm: CloFormState = {
  code: "",
  description: "",
  descriptionVn: "",
  bloomLevel: "",
  competencyLevel: "",
}

export default function Section3CLOs({ syllabusId, readOnly = false }: Props) {
  const { data: clos = [], isLoading } = useClos(syllabusId)
  const createMutation = useCreateClo()
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteClo(syllabusId)

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState<CloFormState>(emptyForm)
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Partial<CloFormState>>({})

  const nextCode = `CLO${(clos.length + 1).toString().padStart(1, "0")}`

  const validate = () => {
    const e: Partial<CloFormState> = {}
    if (!form.code.trim()) e.code = "Bắt buộc"
    if (!form.description.trim()) e.description = "Bắt buộc"
    if (!form.bloomLevel) e.bloomLevel = "Bắt buộc"
    if (!form.competencyLevel) e.competencyLevel = "Bắt buộc"
    setErrors(e)
    return Object.keys(e).length === 0
  }

  const handleAdd = () => {
    if (!validate()) return
    createMutation.mutate(
      {
        syllabusId,
        code: form.code.trim(),
        description: form.description.trim(),
        descriptionVn: form.descriptionVn.trim() || undefined,
        bloomLevel: form.bloomLevel,
        competencyLevel: form.competencyLevel,
        orderIndex: clos.length + 1,
      },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setForm(emptyForm)
          setErrors({})
          setShowForm(false)
        },
      }
    )
  }

  const handleDelete = (id: number, code: string) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Xóa ${code}? Mapping CLO-PLO liên quan cũng sẽ bị xóa.`)) return
    deleteMutation.mutate(id)
  }

  const getBloomConfig = (level: string) =>
    BLOOM_LEVELS.find(b => b.value === level)

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-48 gap-2 text-slate-400">
        <Loader2 className="size-5 animate-spin" />
        Đang tải CLO...
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header card */}
      <Card className="border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50/40 shadow-sm">
        <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <AlertCircle className="size-5 text-blue-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <div className="text-sm text-blue-700">
              <p className="font-semibold mb-0.5">Chuẩn đầu ra môn học (CLO) theo Bloom's Taxonomy</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-blue-600">
                Mỗi CLO phải khai báo mức độ nhận thức (Bloom) và loại năng lực (Knowledge/Skill/Attitude).
                CLO sẽ được mapping với PLO của ngành ở bước tiếp theo.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* CLO List */}
      <Card className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-row items-center justify-between">
          <CardTitle className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2">
            <ListChecks className="size-4 text-primary" />
            Danh sách CLO
            <Badge className="bg-primary/10 text-primary border-0 text-xs font-semibold">
              {clos.length} CLO
            </Badge>
          </CardTitle>
          {!readOnly && (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => { setShowForm(true); setForm({ ...emptyForm, code: nextCode }) }}
              className="gap-1.5 bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 h-8 text-xs shadow-sm"
            >
              <Plus className="size-3.5" />
              Thêm CLO
            </Button>
          )}
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-0">
          {clos.length === 0 && !showForm ? (
            <div className="py-12 text-center">
              <ListChecks className="size-10 text-slate-200 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm font-medium">Chưa có CLO nào</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs mt-1">Nhấn "Thêm CLO" để bắt đầu</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {clos.map((clo: Clo, idx: number) => {
                const bloom = getBloomConfig(clo.bloomLevel ?? "")
                return (
                  <div key={clo.id} className="flex items-start gap-3 p-4 hover:bg-slate-50/40 group transition-colors">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 mt-1">
                      <GripVertical className="size-4 text-slate-300" />
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 w-5 text-center">{idx + 1}</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1.5">
                        <span className="text-sm font-bold text-primary font-mono">{clo.code}</span>
                        {bloom && (
                          <span className={`text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${bloom.color}`}>
                            {bloom.label}
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {clo.competencyLevel && (
                          <span className="text-xs bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">
                            {COMPETENCY_LEVELS.find(c => c.value === clo.competencyLevel)?.label ?? clo.competencyLevel}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">{clo.description}</p>
                      {clo.descriptionVn && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 italic">{clo.descriptionVn}</p>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {!readOnly && (
                      <Button
                        size="sm"
                        variant="ghost"
                        onClick={() => handleDelete(clo.id, clo.code)}
                        className="shrink-0 h-8 w-8 p-0 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity mt-1"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Add Form */}
          {showForm && !readOnly && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50/70 border-t border-slate-200">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">Thêm CLO mới</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                    Mã CLO <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                  </Label>
                  <Input
                    className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                    placeholder="VD: CLO1"
                    value={form.code}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, code: e.target.value })}
                  />
                  {errors.code && <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{errors.code}</p>}
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                    Bloom's Level <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                  </Label>
                  <Select value={form.bloomLevel} onValueChange={(v) => setForm({ ...form, bloomLevel: v })}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-9 text-sm bg-white">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn mức Bloom..." />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {BLOOM_LEVELS.map(b => (
                        <SelectItem key={b.value} value={b.value}>
                          <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-semibold ${b.color}`}>{b.label}</span>
                        </SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                  {errors.bloomLevel && <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{errors.bloomLevel}</p>}
                </div>

                <div className="md:col-span-2 space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                    Mô tả (Tiếng Anh) <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                  </Label>
                  <textarea
                    className="w-full min-h-[70px] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary/60 placeholder:text-slate-400"
                    placeholder="Students will be able to..."
                    value={form.description}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })}
                  />
                  {errors.description && <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{errors.description}</p>}
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                    Mô tả (Tiếng Việt)
                  </Label>
                  <textarea
                    className="w-full min-h-[60px] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary/60 placeholder:text-slate-400"
                    placeholder="Sinh viên có khả năng..."
                    value={form.descriptionVn}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, descriptionVn: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
                    Loại năng lực <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                  </Label>
                  <Select value={form.competencyLevel} onValueChange={(v) => setForm({ ...form, competencyLevel: v })}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-9 text-sm bg-white">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn loại năng lực..." />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {COMPETENCY_LEVELS.map(c => (
                        <SelectItem key={c.value} value={c.value}>{c.label}</SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                  {errors.competencyLevel && <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{errors.competencyLevel}</p>}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={handleAdd}
                  disabled={createMutation.isPending}
                  className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 gap-1.5 h-8 text-xs"
                >
                  {createMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="size-3.5" />}
                  Lưu CLO
                </Button>
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={() => { setShowForm(false); setErrors({}) }}
                  className="h-8 text-xs"
                >
                  Hủy
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Summary */}
      {clos.length > 0 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {["KNOWLEDGE", "SKILL", "ATTITUDE"].map(type => {
            const count = clos.filter(c => c.competencyLevel === type).length
            return (
              <Card key={type} className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                <CardContent className="pt-3 pb-3 text-center">
                  <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800">{count}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                    {type === "KNOWLEDGE" ? "Kiến thức" : type === "SKILL" ? "Kỹ năng" : "Thái độ"}
                  </p>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/syllabus/editor/tabs/Section4PLOMapping.tsx">
import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Layers, Loader2, Save } from "lucide-react"
import { useClos, useCreateCloPloMapping, useDeleteCloPloMapping } from "@/hooks/useClos"
import { useAllPlos } from "@/hooks/useSyllabusBooks"
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { cloPloMappingApi } from "@/api/cloApi"
import { useQueries } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { Clo, CloPloMapping, ContributionLevel } from "@/types/clo"

const LEVELS: { value: ContributionLevel | ""; label: string; bg: string; text: string }[] = [
  { value: "", label: "–", bg: "bg-slate-50", text: "text-slate-400" },
  { value: "I", label: "I", bg: "bg-sky-100", text: "text-sky-700" },
  { value: "D", label: "D", bg: "bg-amber-100", text: "text-amber-700" },
  { value: "A", label: "A", bg: "bg-emerald-100", text: "text-emerald-700" },
]

interface Props {
  syllabusId: number
  readOnly?: boolean
}

export default function Section4PLOMapping({ syllabusId, readOnly = false }: Props) {
  const { data: clos = [], isLoading: loadingClos } = useClos(syllabusId)
  const { data: allPlos = [], isLoading: loadingPlos } = useAllPlos()
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<string | null>(null) // "cloId-ploId"

  // fetch mappings for all CLOs
  const mappingQueries = useQueries({
    queries: clos.map(clo => ({
      queryKey: ["clo-plo-mappings", clo.id],
      queryFn: () => cloPloMappingApi.getByClo(clo.id),
      enabled: clo.id > 0,
    })),
  })

  const allMappings = useMemo(() => {
    const result: Record<number, CloPloMapping[]> = {}
    clos.forEach((clo, idx) => {
      result[clo.id] = mappingQueries[idx]?.data ?? []
    })
    return result
  }, [clos, mappingQueries])

  const getMapping = (cloId: number, ploId: number): CloPloMapping | undefined => {
    return allMappings[cloId]?.find(m => m.ploId === ploId)
  }

  const getCurrentLevel = (cloId: number, ploId: number): ContributionLevel | "" => {
    return getMapping(cloId, ploId)?.level ?? ""
  }

  const handleCellClick = async (cloId: number, ploId: number) => {
    if (readOnly) return
    const key = `${cloId}-${ploId}`
    setSaving(key)

    const current = getCurrentLevel(cloId, ploId)
    const nextIdx = (LEVELS.findIndex(l => l.value === current) + 1) % LEVELS.length
    const nextLevel = LEVELS[nextIdx].value

    try {
      const existingMapping = getMapping(cloId, ploId)
      if (existingMapping) {
        await cloPloMappingApi.delete(existingMapping.id)
      }
      if (nextLevel !== "") {
        await cloPloMappingApi.create({ cloId, ploId, level: nextLevel })
      }
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["clo-plo-mappings", cloId] })
    } finally {
      setSaving(null)
    }
  }

  // Check coverage: each CLO must map at least 1 PLO
  const unmappedClos = clos.filter(clo => (allMappings[clo.id] ?? []).length === 0)

  const loading = loadingClos || loadingPlos || mappingQueries.some(q => q.isLoading)

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-48 gap-2 text-slate-400">
        <Loader2 className="size-5 animate-spin" />
        Đang tải ma trận...
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (clos.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Card className="border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/40 shadow-sm">
        <CardContent className="pt-6 pb-6 text-center">
          <AlertCircle className="size-10 text-amber-400 mx-auto mb-3" />
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-800">Chưa có CLO nào</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 mt-1">Vui lòng thêm CLO ở tab "Chuẩn đầu ra (CLO)" trước</p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  if (allPlos.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Card className="border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/40 shadow-sm">
        <CardContent className="pt-6 pb-6 text-center">
          <AlertCircle className="size-10 text-amber-400 mx-auto mb-3" />
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-800">Chưa có PLO nào trong hệ thống</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 mt-1">Admin hoặc Program Coordinator cần tạo PLO trước</p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Legend */}
      <Card className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <CardContent className="pt-3 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-6 flex-wrap">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Mức độ liên kết:</p>
            {LEVELS.slice(1).map(l => (
              <div key={l.value} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-lg ${l.bg} ${l.text} flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold border border-current/20`}>
                  {l.label}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">
                  {l.value === "I" ? "Introduce – Giới thiệu" : l.value === "D" ? "Develop – Phát triển" : "Achieve – Đạt được"}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
            {!readOnly && (
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic ml-auto">Nhấn vào ô để chuyển mức: Trống → I → D → A → Trống</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Warning for unmapped CLOs */}
      {unmappedClos.length > 0 && (
        <Card className="border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/40 shadow-sm">
          <CardContent className="pt-3 pb-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="size-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
              <p className="text-xs text-rose-700">
                <span className="font-semibold">Cảnh báo: </span>
                {unmappedClos.map(c => c.code).join(", ")} chưa được mapping với PLO nào.
                Đề cương sẽ không thể nộp cho đến khi hoàn thành mapping.
              </p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Matrix table */}
      <Card className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
          <CardTitle className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Layers className="size-4 text-primary" />
            Ma trận CLO – PLO
            {unmappedClos.length === 0 && clos.length > 0 && (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-600 font-semibold">
                <CheckCircle2 className="size-3.5" />
                Đã mapping đầy đủ
              </span>
            )}
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="p-0 overflow-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm border-collapse min-w-max">
            <thead>
              <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider sticky left-0 bg-slate-50 border-r border-slate-200 min-w-[140px] z-10">
                  CLO \ PLO
                </th>
                {allPlos.map(plo => (
                  <th key={plo.id} className="px-2 py-3 text-center min-w-[80px]">
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">{plo.code}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-normal max-w-[70px] truncate" title={plo.descriptionVn || plo.description}>
                        {(plo.descriptionVn || plo.description || "").slice(0, 20)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </th>
                ))}
                <th className="px-3 py-3 text-center text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider min-w-[70px]">
                  Tổng
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {clos.map((clo: Clo) => {
                const rowMappings = allMappings[clo.id] ?? []
                const mappedCount = rowMappings.length
                return (
                  <tr key={clo.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/30 transition-colors">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 sticky left-0 bg-white border-r border-slate-200 z-10">
                      <div className="flex flex-col">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary font-mono">{clo.code}</span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-[120px] truncate" title={clo.description}>
                          {clo.description.slice(0, 35)}...
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    {allPlos.map(plo => {
                      const level = getCurrentLevel(clo.id, plo.id)
                      const key = `${clo.id}-${plo.id}`
                      const isSaving = saving === key
                      const levelCfg = LEVELS.find(l => l.value === level) ?? LEVELS[0]
                      return (
                        <td key={plo.id} className="px-2 py-3 text-center">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleCellClick(clo.id, plo.id)}
                            disabled={isSaving || readOnly}
                            className={`
                              w-9 h-9 rounded-lg border text-sm font-bold transition-all duration-150
                              ${levelCfg.bg} ${levelCfg.text} border-current/20
                              ${!readOnly ? "hover:scale-110 hover:shadow-sm cursor-pointer active:scale-95" : "cursor-default"}
                              ${isSaving ? "opacity-50 animate-pulse" : ""}
                            `}
                            title={`${clo.code} × ${plo.code}: ${level || "Không có"}`}
                          >
                            {isSaving ? "…" : levelCfg.label}
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      )
                    })}
                    <td className="px-3 py-3 text-center">
                      <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                        mappedCount === 0 ? "bg-rose-100 text-rose-700" :
                        mappedCount >= 2 ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700" :
                        "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"
                      }`}>
                        {mappedCount}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )
              })}
            </tbody>
            {/* PLO column totals */}
            <tfoot>
              <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-t-2 border-slate-200">
                <td className="px-4 py-2 sticky left-0 bg-slate-50 border-r border-slate-200 z-10">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">Tổng CLO/PLO</span>
                </td>
                {allPlos.map(plo => {
                  const colCount = clos.filter(clo => getCurrentLevel(clo.id, plo.id) !== "").length
                  return (
                    <td key={plo.id} className="px-2 py-2 text-center">
                      <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                        colCount === 0 ? "bg-rose-100 text-rose-700" :
                        "bg-slate-200 text-slate-700"
                      }`}>
                        {colCount}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                  )
                })}
                <td />
              </tr>
            </tfoot>
          </table>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/syllabus/editor/tabs/Section5Topics.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"
import { BookOpen, GripVertical, Loader2, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react"
import { useTopics, useCreateTopic, useUpdateTopic, useDeleteTopic } from "@/hooks/useTopics"
import { TOPIC_TYPES } from "@/types/topic"
import type { Topic, CreateTopicRequest } from "@/types/topic"

interface Props {
  syllabusId: number
  readOnly?: boolean
}

const emptyRow = (): Omit<CreateTopicRequest, "syllabusId"> => ({
  weekNumber: 1,
  orderInWeek: 1,
  name: "",
  nameVn: "",
  teachingHours: 2,
  labHours: 0,
  selfStudyHours: 0,
  topicType: "LECTURE",
  teachingMethod: "",
  learningActivity: "",
  notes: "",
})

export default function Section5Topics({ syllabusId, readOnly = false }: Props) {
  const { data: topics = [], isLoading } = useTopics(syllabusId)
  const createMutation = useCreateTopic()
  const updateMutation = useUpdateTopic(syllabusId)
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteTopic(syllabusId)

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyRow())
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<Partial<Topic>>({})

  // Group topics by week
  const byWeek = topics.reduce<Record<number, Topic[]>>((acc, t) => {
    const w = t.weekNumber
    if (!acc[w]) acc[w] = []
    acc[w].push(t)
    return acc
  }, {})

  const weeks = Object.keys(byWeek).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b)

  const nextWeek = topics.length > 0
    ? Math.max(...topics.map(t => t.weekNumber))
    : 1

  const handleAdd = () => {
    if (!form.name.trim()) { alert("Vui lòng nhập tên nội dung"); return }
    createMutation.mutate(
      { ...form, syllabusId },
      { onSuccess: () => { setForm(emptyRow()); setShowForm(false) } }
    )
  }

  const handleDelete = (id: number) => {
    if (!window.confirm("Xóa nội dung này?")) return
    deleteMutation.mutate(id)
  }

  const handleEdit = (topic: Topic) => {
    setEditingId(topic.id)
    setEditForm({ ...topic })
  }

  const handleSaveEdit = () => {
    if (!editingId || !editForm.name?.trim()) return
    updateMutation.mutate(
      {
        id: editingId,
        data: {
          syllabusId,
          weekNumber: editForm.weekNumber ?? 1,
          orderInWeek: editForm.orderInWeek,
          name: editForm.name ?? "",
          nameVn: editForm.nameVn,
          teachingHours: editForm.teachingHours,
          labHours: editForm.labHours,
          selfStudyHours: editForm.selfStudyHours,
          topicType: editForm.topicType as string,
          teachingMethod: editForm.teachingMethod,
          learningActivity: editForm.learningActivity,
          notes: editForm.notes,
        },
      },
      { onSuccess: () => { setEditingId(null); setEditForm({}) } }
    )
  }

  // Totals
  const totalTeaching = topics.reduce((s, t) => s + (t.teachingHours ?? 0), 0)
  const totalLab = topics.reduce((s, t) => s + (t.labHours ?? 0), 0)
  const totalSelf = topics.reduce((s, t) => s + (t.selfStudyHours ?? 0), 0)

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-48 gap-2 text-slate-400">
        <Loader2 className="size-5 animate-spin" />
        Đang tải nội dung...
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Summary stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        {[
          { label: "Tổng chủ đề", value: topics.length, color: "text-primary" },
          { label: "Tiết LT", value: totalTeaching, color: "text-blue-600" },
          { label: "Tiết TH", value: totalLab, color: "text-emerald-600" },
          { label: "Tiết tự học", value: totalSelf, color: "text-amber-600" },
        ].map(s => (
          <Card key={s.label} className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
            <CardContent className="pt-3 pb-3 text-center">
              <p className={`text-2xl font-bold ${s.color}`}>{s.value}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{s.label}</p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Topics by week */}
      <Card className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-row items-center justify-between">
          <CardTitle className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2">
            <BookOpen className="size-4 text-primary" />
            Nội dung giảng dạy theo tuần
          </CardTitle>
          {!readOnly && (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => { setShowForm(true); setForm({ ...emptyRow(), weekNumber: nextWeek }) }}
              className="gap-1.5 bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 h-8 text-xs shadow-sm"
            >
              <Plus className="size-3.5" />
              Thêm nội dung
            </Button>
          )}
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-0">
          {topics.length === 0 && !showForm ? (
            <div className="py-12 text-center">
              <BookOpen className="size-10 text-slate-200 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm font-medium">Chưa có nội dung giảng dạy</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs mt-1">Nhấn "Thêm nội dung" để bắt đầu</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div>
              {weeks.map(week => (
                <div key={week}>
                  <div className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-50/80 border-y border-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Tuần {week}
                    </span>
                    <Badge className="bg-primary/10 text-primary border-0 text-xs">
                      {byWeek[week].length} nội dung
                    </Badge>
                  </div>
                  {byWeek[week]
                    .sort((a, b) => (a.orderInWeek ?? 0) - (b.orderInWeek ?? 0))
                    .map((topic: Topic) => (
                      <div key={topic.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-b-0">
                        {editingId === topic.id && !readOnly ? (
                          <div className="p-4 bg-blue-50/30">
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                              <div className="space-y-1">
                                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tên nội dung (EN) *</Label>
                                <Input
                                  className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                                  value={editForm.name ?? ""}
                                  onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, name: e.target.value })}
                                />
                              </div>
                              <div className="space-y-1">
                                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tên nội dung (VN)</Label>
                                <Input
                                  className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                                  value={editForm.nameVn ?? ""}
                                  onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, nameVn: e.target.value })}
                                />
                              </div>
                              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                                <div className="space-y-1">
                                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">LT (tiết)</Label>
                                  <Input type="number" min={0} className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                                    value={editForm.teachingHours ?? 0}
                                    onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, teachingHours: Number(e.target.value) })} />
                                </div>
                                <div className="space-y-1">
                                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">TH (tiết)</Label>
                                  <Input type="number" min={0} className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                                    value={editForm.labHours ?? 0}
                                    onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, labHours: Number(e.target.value) })} />
                                </div>
                                <div className="space-y-1">
                                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tự học</Label>
                                  <Input type="number" min={0} className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                                    value={editForm.selfStudyHours ?? 0}
                                    onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, selfStudyHours: Number(e.target.value) })} />
                                </div>
                              </div>
                              <div className="space-y-1">
                                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Loại</Label>
                                <Select value={editForm.topicType as string ?? "LECTURE"}
                                  onValueChange={(v) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, topicType: v as Topic["topicType"] })}>
                                  <SelectTrigger className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                                  <SelectContent>
                                    {TOPIC_TYPES.map(t => <SelectItem key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</SelectItem>)}
                                  </SelectContent>
                                </Select>
                              </div>
                              <div className="space-y-1">
                                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Phương pháp giảng dạy</Label>
                                <Input className="h-9 text-sm bg-white" value={editForm.teachingMethod ?? ""}
                                  onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, teachingMethod: e.target.value })} />
                              </div>
                              <div className="space-y-1">
                                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Hoạt động học</Label>
                                <Input className="h-9 text-sm bg-white" value={editForm.learningActivity ?? ""}
                                  onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, learningActivity: e.target.value })} />
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
                              <Button size="sm" onClick={handleSaveEdit} disabled={updateMutation.isPending}
                                className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 h-8 text-xs gap-1.5">
                                {updateMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : null}
                                Lưu
                              </Button>
                              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setEditingId(null)} className="h-8 text-xs">
                                Hủy
                              </Button>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <div
                            className={`flex items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-slate-50/40 group transition-colors ${!readOnly ? "cursor-pointer" : ""}`}
                            onClick={() => !readOnly && handleEdit(topic)}
                          >
                            <GripVertical className="size-4 text-slate-300 mt-1 shrink-0" />
                            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1">
                                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{topic.name}</span>
                                {topic.nameVn && <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">/ {topic.nameVn}</span>}
                                <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium ${
                                  topic.topicType === "LECTURE" ? "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700" :
                                  topic.topicType === "LAB" ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700" :
                                  "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"
                                }`}>
                                  {TOPIC_TYPES.find(t => t.value === topic.topicType)?.label.split("(")[0].trim() ?? topic.topicType}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                {(topic.teachingHours ?? 0) > 0 && <span>LT: {topic.teachingHours} tiết</span>}
                                {(topic.labHours ?? 0) > 0 && <span>TH: {topic.labHours} tiết</span>}
                                {(topic.selfStudyHours ?? 0) > 0 && <span>Tự học: {topic.selfStudyHours} tiết</span>}
                                {topic.teachingMethod && <span>PP: {topic.teachingMethod}</span>}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            {!readOnly && (
                              <Button
                                size="sm" variant="ghost"
                                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDelete(topic.id) }}
                                className="shrink-0 h-8 w-8 p-0 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
                              >
                                <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                              </Button>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Add form */}
          {showForm && !readOnly && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50/70 border-t border-slate-200">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">Thêm nội dung mới</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tuần số *</Label>
                  <Input type="number" min={1} max={16} className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                    value={form.weekNumber}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, weekNumber: Number(e.target.value) })} />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Loại nội dung</Label>
                  <Select value={form.topicType ?? "LECTURE"} onValueChange={(v) => setForm({ ...form, topicType: v })}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {TOPIC_TYPES.map(t => <SelectItem key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</SelectItem>)}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>
                <div className="md:col-span-2 space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tên nội dung (Tiếng Anh) *</Label>
                  <Input className="h-9 text-sm bg-white" placeholder="Introduction to..."
                    value={form.name}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} />
                </div>
                <div className="md:col-span-2 space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tên nội dung (Tiếng Việt)</Label>
                  <Input className="h-9 text-sm bg-white" placeholder="Giới thiệu về..."
                    value={form.nameVn ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, nameVn: e.target.value })} />
                </div>
