                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">LT (tiết)</Label>
                    <Input type="number" min={0} className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                      value={form.teachingHours ?? 0}
                      onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, teachingHours: Number(e.target.value) })} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">TH (tiết)</Label>
                    <Input type="number" min={0} className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                      value={form.labHours ?? 0}
                      onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, labHours: Number(e.target.value) })} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tự học</Label>
                    <Input type="number" min={0} className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                      value={form.selfStudyHours ?? 0}
                      onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, selfStudyHours: Number(e.target.value) })} />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Phương pháp giảng dạy</Label>
                  <Input className="h-9 text-sm bg-white" placeholder="Lecture, Discussion..."
                    value={form.teachingMethod ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, teachingMethod: e.target.value })} />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Hoạt động học tập</Label>
                  <Input className="h-9 text-sm bg-white" placeholder="Individual work, Group discussion..."
                    value={form.learningActivity ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, learningActivity: e.target.value })} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
                <Button size="sm" onClick={handleAdd} disabled={createMutation.isPending}
                  className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 gap-1.5 h-8 text-xs">
                  {createMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="size-3.5" />}
                  Lưu nội dung
                </Button>
                <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setShowForm(false)} className="h-8 text-xs">
                  Hủy
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/syllabus/editor/tabs/Section6Assessment.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, GripVertical, Loader2, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react"
import { useAssessments, useCreateAssessment, useDeleteAssessment } from "@/hooks/useAssessments"
import { ASSESSMENT_TYPES } from "@/types/assessment"
import type { AssessmentComponent, CreateAssessmentComponentRequest } from "@/types/assessment"

interface Props {
  syllabusId: number
  readOnly?: boolean
}

const emptyForm: Omit<CreateAssessmentComponentRequest, "syllabusId"> = {
  name: "",
  nameVn: "",
  assessmentType: "OTHER",
  weightPercent: 0,
  minScore: 0,
  maxScore: 10,
}

export default function Section6Assessment({ syllabusId, readOnly = false }: Props) {
  const { data: assessments = [], isLoading } = useAssessments(syllabusId)
  const createMutation = useCreateAssessment()
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteAssessment(syllabusId)

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})

  const totalWeight = assessments.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.weightPercent ?? 0), 0)
  const isTotalValid = totalWeight === 100

  const validate = () => {
    const e: Record<string, string> = {}
    if (!form.name.trim()) e.name = "Bắt buộc"
    if (!form.assessmentType) e.assessmentType = "Bắt buộc"
    if ((form.weightPercent ?? 0) <= 0) e.weightPercent = "Phải > 0"
    if (totalWeight + (form.weightPercent ?? 0) > 100) e.weightPercent = "Vượt quá 100%"
    setErrors(e)
    return Object.keys(e).length === 0
  }

  const handleAdd = () => {
    if (!validate()) return
    createMutation.mutate(
      {
        ...form,
        syllabusId,
        orderIndex: assessments.length + 1,
      },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setForm(emptyForm)
          setShowForm(false)
          setErrors({})
        },
      }
    )
  }

  const handleDelete = (id: number) => {
    if (!window.confirm("Xóa thành phần đánh giá này?")) return
    deleteMutation.mutate(id)
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-48 gap-2 text-slate-400">
        <Loader2 className="size-5 animate-spin" />
        Đang tải thành phần đánh giá...
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Warning/Success Banner */}
      <Card className={`border shadow-sm ${
        isTotalValid ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/40" : "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/40"
      }`}>
        <CardContent className="pt-4 pb-3 flex items-start gap-3">
          {isTotalValid ? (
            <CheckCircle2 className="size-5 text-emerald-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          ) : (
            <AlertCircle className="size-5 text-amber-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          )}
          <div>
            <p className={`font-semibold text-sm ${isTotalValid ? "text-emerald-800" : "text-amber-800"}`}>
              Tổng trọng số: {totalWeight}% / 100%
            </p>
            <p className={`text-xs mt-0.5 ${isTotalValid ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-amber-600"}`}>
              {isTotalValid
                ? "Tổng trọng số đã hợp lệ. Đề cương có thể được nộp."
                : "Tổng trọng số các thành phần đánh giá phải đúng bằng 100%."}
            </p>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <CardHeader className="pb-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-row items-center justify-between">
          <CardTitle className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="size-4 text-primary" />
            Các thành phần đánh giá
          </CardTitle>
          {!readOnly && totalWeight < 100 && (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => { setShowForm(true); setForm({ ...emptyForm, weightPercent: 100 - totalWeight }) }}
              className="gap-1.5 bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 h-8 text-xs shadow-sm"
            >
              <Plus className="size-3.5" />
              Thêm thành phần
            </Button>
          )}
        </CardHeader>

        <CardContent className="p-0">
          {assessments.length === 0 && !showForm ? (
            <div className="py-12 text-center">
              <CheckCircle2 className="size-10 text-slate-200 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm font-medium">Chưa có thành phần đánh giá</p>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs mt-1">Nhấn "Thêm thành phần" để bắt đầu</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {assessments.map((a: AssessmentComponent) => (
                <div key={a.id} className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 hover:bg-slate-50/40 group transition-colors">
                  <GripVertical className="size-4 text-slate-300 shrink-0" />
                  
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">{a.name}</span>
                      {a.nameVn && <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">/ {a.nameVn}</span>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      <span className="bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-medium text-slate-600">
                        {ASSESSMENT_TYPES.find(t => t.value === a.assessmentType)?.label ?? a.assessmentType}
                      </span>
                      <span>Min: {a.minScore}</span>
                      <span>Max: {a.maxScore}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="shrink-0 text-right mr-4">
                    <span className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">{a.weightPercent}%</span>
                  </div>

                  {!readOnly && (
                    <Button
                      size="sm"
                      variant="ghost"
                      onClick={() => handleDelete(a.id)}
                      className="shrink-0 h-8 w-8 p-0 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                    </Button>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Add form */}
          {showForm && !readOnly && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50/70 border-t border-slate-200">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">Thêm đánh giá mới</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tên thành phần (EN) *</Label>
                  <Input
                    className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                    placeholder="Midterm Exam..."
                    value={form.name}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
                  />
                  {errors.name && <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{errors.name}</p>}
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Tên thành phần (VN)</Label>
                  <Input
                    className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                    placeholder="Thi giữa kỳ..."
                    value={form.nameVn ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, nameVn: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Loại đánh giá *</Label>
                  <Select value={form.assessmentType ?? ""} onValueChange={(v) => setForm({ ...form, assessmentType: v })}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="h-9 text-sm bg-white">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      {ASSESSMENT_TYPES.map(t => (
                        <SelectItem key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Trọng số (%) *</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="number"
                    min={1}
                    max={100}
                    className="h-9 text-sm bg-white font-bold text-primary"
                    value={form.weightPercent}
                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, weightPercent: Number(e.target.value) })}
                  />
                  {errors.weightPercent && <p className="text-xs text-rose-500">{errors.weightPercent}</p>}
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Điểm tối thiểu</Label>
                    <Input
                      type="number"
                      className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                      value={form.minScore}
                      onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, minScore: Number(e.target.value) })}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Điểm tối đa</Label>
                    <Input
                      type="number"
                      className="h-9 text-sm bg-white"
                      value={form.maxScore}
                      onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, maxScore: Number(e.target.value) })}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-4">
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={handleAdd}
                  disabled={createMutation.isPending}
                  className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 gap-1.5 h-8 text-xs"
                >
                  {createMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="size-3.5" />}
                  Lưu
                </Button>
                <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setShowForm(false)} className="h-8 text-xs">
                  Hủy
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/syllabus/editor/SyllabusEditorShell.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  BookOpen,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronRight,
  ClipboardList,
  FileText,
  GraduationCap,
  Layers,
  LibraryBig,
  ListChecks,
  Send,
} from "lucide-react"
import type { Syllabus } from "@/types/syllabus"
import { useSubmitSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useSubmitSyllabus"

// Section imports
import Section1GeneralInfo from "./tabs/Section1GeneralInfo"
import Section3CLOs from "./tabs/Section3CLOs"
import Section4PLOMapping from "./tabs/Section4PLOMapping"
import Section5Topics from "./tabs/Section5Topics"
import Section6Assessment from "./tabs/Section6Assessment"
import Section7ReadingList from "./tabs/Section7ReadingList"

interface Tab {
  id: number
  label: string
  shortLabel: string
  icon: React.ElementType
  description: string
}

const TABS: Tab[] = [
  { id: 1, label: "Thông tin chung", shortLabel: "Chung", icon: FileText, description: "Thông tin cơ bản của môn học" },
  { id: 3, label: "Chuẩn đầu ra (CLO)", shortLabel: "CLO", icon: ListChecks, description: "Course Learning Outcomes theo Bloom's Taxonomy" },
  { id: 4, label: "Mapping CLO–PLO", shortLabel: "CLO–PLO", icon: Layers, description: "Ma trận liên kết CLO với PLO của ngành" },
  { id: 5, label: "Nội dung giảng dạy", shortLabel: "Nội dung", icon: ClipboardList, description: "Phân bổ nội dung theo tuần học" },
  { id: 6, label: "Phương pháp đánh giá", shortLabel: "Đánh giá", icon: CheckCircle2, description: "Thành phần điểm và trọng số" },
  { id: 7, label: "Tài liệu tham khảo", shortLabel: "Tài liệu", icon: LibraryBig, description: "Sách giáo khoa và tài liệu bổ sung" },
]

const STATUS_CONFIG: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {
  DRAFT: { label: "Bản nháp", className: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200" },
  SUBMITTED: { label: "Đã nộp", className: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border-blue-200" },
  APPROVED: { label: "Đã duyệt", className: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200" },
  REJECTED: { label: "Bị từ chối", className: "bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border-rose-200" },
}

interface Props {
  syllabus: Syllabus
  readOnly?: boolean
}

export default function SyllabusEditorShell({ syllabus, readOnly = false }: Props) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(1)
  const submitMutation = useSubmitSyllabus()

  const statusCfg = STATUS_CONFIG[syllabus.status] ?? STATUS_CONFIG.DRAFT
  const canSubmit = syllabus.status === "DRAFT" && !readOnly

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    if (!window.confirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn nộp đề cương này để duyệt?\nSau khi nộp, bạn không thể chỉnh sửa cho đến khi bị từ chối.")) return
    submitMutation.mutate(syllabus.id, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        alert("Đề cương đã được nộp thành công!")
        navigate(-1)
      },
    })
  }

  const renderSection = () => {
    switch (activeTab) {
      case 1: return <Section1GeneralInfo syllabus={syllabus} readOnly={readOnly} />
      case 3: return <Section3CLOs syllabusId={syllabus.id} readOnly={readOnly} />
      case 4: return <Section4PLOMapping syllabusId={syllabus.id} readOnly={readOnly} />
      case 5: return <Section5Topics syllabusId={syllabus.id} readOnly={readOnly} />
      case 6: return <Section6Assessment syllabusId={syllabus.id} readOnly={readOnly} />
      case 7: return <Section7ReadingList syllabusId={syllabus.id} readOnly={readOnly} />
      default: return null
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen bg-slate-50/60">
      {/* ── Sticky Top Header ── */}
      <div className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <div className="px-6 py-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          {/* Left: Back + Title */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
              className="shrink-0 h-8 w-8 p-0 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="size-5" />
            </Button>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
              <div className="bg-primary/10 p-1.5 rounded-lg shrink-0">
                <GraduationCap className="size-5 text-primary" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 font-heading truncate">
                    {syllabus.courseCode} — {syllabus.courseName}
                  </h1>
                  <Badge className={`text-xs font-semibold border ${statusCfg.className} shrink-0`}>
                    {statusCfg.label}
                  </Badge>
                  {syllabus.isCurrent && (
                    <Badge className="text-xs font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 shrink-0">
                      ● Hiện hành
                    </Badge>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Năm học {syllabus.academicYear} · Phiên bản {syllabus.versionLabel} · Tạo bởi {syllabus.createdByUsername}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right: Actions */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            {readOnly && syllabus.status === "DRAFT" && (
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => navigate(`/dept-head/syllabus/${syllabus.id}/edit`)}
                className="gap-1.5 text-sm"
              >
                <BookOpen className="size-4" />
                Chỉnh sửa
              </Button>
            )}
            {canSubmit && (
              <Button
                size="sm"
                onClick={handleSubmit}
                disabled={submitMutation.isPending}
                className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 gap-1.5 text-sm shadow-sm"
              >
                <Send className="size-4" />
                {submitMutation.isPending ? "Đang nộp..." : "Nộp đề cương"}
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ── Tab Navigation ── */}
        <div className="px-6 flex items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto pb-0 scrollbar-none">
          {TABS.map((tab, idx) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon
            const isActive = activeTab === tab.id
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                className={`
                  flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap
                  border-b-2 transition-all duration-200 rounded-t-md
                  ${isActive
                    ? "border-primary text-primary bg-primary/5"
                    : "border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:border-slate-300"
                  }
                `}
              >
                <span className={`flex items-center justify-center size-5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold shrink-0 ${
                  isActive ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-slate-200 text-slate-500"
                }`}>
                  {idx + 1}
                </span>
                <Icon className="size-4 shrink-0" />
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">{tab.shortLabel}</span>
                {idx < TABS.length - 1 && (
                  <ChevronRight className="size-3.5 text-slate-300 ml-1 hidden sm:block" />
                )}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Section Description Bar ── */}
      <div className="bg-white border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-2.5">
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
            {TABS.find(t => t.id === activeTab)?.label}
          </span>
          {" "}—{" "}
          {TABS.find(t => t.id === activeTab)?.description}
          {readOnly && (
            <span className="ml-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-amber-600 font-medium bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
              👁 Chế độ xem
            </span>
          )}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* ── Section Content ── */}
      <div className="flex-1 px-6 py-6 max-w-6xl mx-auto w-full">
        {renderSection()}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/syllabus/SyllabusForm.tsx">
import { useState, useEffect } from "react"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Plus, X, Save, Trash2 } from "lucide-react"
import { useAuthStore } from "@/store/authStore"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogFooter,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { MAJORS } from "@/lib/syllabusHelper"

import type { CreateSyllabusRequest } from "@/types/syllabus"
import { courseApi } from "@/api/courseApi"
import { departmentApi } from "@/api/departmentApi" // <-- THÊM IMPORT API DEPARTMENT
import type { Course } from "@/types/course"

interface Props {
  initialData?: CreateSyllabusRequest
  onSubmit: (values: CreateSyllabusRequest) => void
  loading?: boolean
}

const parseJsonArray = <T,>(value: unknown, fallback: T[] = []): T[] => {
  if (!value) return fallback
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value as T[]
  if (typeof value !== "string") return fallback

  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(value)
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : fallback
  } catch {
    return fallback
  }
}

const normalizeCloLevel = (levels: string[]) => {
  if (levels.includes("Skill")) return "SKILL"
  if (levels.includes("Attitude")) return "ATTITUDE"
  return "KNOWLEDGE"
}

export default function SyllabusForm({
  initialData,
  onSubmit,
  loading,
}: Props) {
  const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user)

  const notesData = (() => {
    if (!initialData?.notes) return {} as Record<string, any>

    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(initialData.notes)
      return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" ? parsed : {}
    } catch {
      return {} as Record<string, any>
    }
  })()

  const [courses, setCourses] = useState<Course[]>([])
  
  // <-- THÊM STATE VÀ EFFECT ĐỂ LẤY DANH SÁCH DEPARTMENT
  const [departments, setDepartments] = useState<any[]>([])
  useEffect(() => {
    departmentApi.getAll().then(data => {
      setDepartments(data)
    }).catch(console.error)
  }, [])
  // ----------------------------------------------------

  useEffect(() => {
    courseApi.getAll().then(data => {
      setCourses(data)
      if (initialData?.courseId) {
        const course = data.find(c => c.id === initialData.courseId)
        if (course) {
          setCourseNameEn(course.name)
          setCourseNameVn(course.nameVn)
          setCourseCode(course.courseCode)
          setCreditsTheory(course.creditTheory?.toString() || "0")
          setCreditsPractice(course.creditLab?.toString() || "0")
        }
      }
    }).catch(console.error)
  }, [initialData?.courseId])

  useEffect(() => {
  if (initialData?.notes) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(initialData.notes)

      if (parsed.instructor) setInstructor(parsed.instructor)
      if (parsed.creditsTheory) setCreditsTheory(parsed.creditsTheory)
      if (parsed.creditsPractice) setCreditsPractice(parsed.creditsPractice)
      if (parsed.ects) setEcts(parsed.ects)
      if (parsed.periodsTheory) setPeriodsTheory(parsed.periodsTheory)
      if (parsed.periodsPractice) setPeriodsPractice(parsed.periodsPractice)

      // Tạm thời giữ vì backend chưa có API riêng
      if (parsed.references) setReferences(parsed.references)
      if (parsed.loMatrix) setLoMatrix(parsed.loMatrix)
      if (parsed.activities) setActivities(parsed.activities)

      // ❌ XÓA DÒNG NÀY
      // if (parsed.assessmentMatrix) setAssessments(parsed.assessmentMatrix)

    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to parse notes", e)
    }
  }
}, [initialData?.notes])

  const [form] = useState<CreateSyllabusRequest>(
    initialData ?? {
      courseId: 1,
      versionNumber: 1,
      versionLabel: "v1.0",
      academicYear: "K24",
      createdBy: user?.userId ?? 1,
      changeSummary: "",
      notes: "",
    }
  )

  const [versionLabel, setVersionLabel] = useState(initialData?.versionLabel || "v1.0")
  const [academicYear, setAcademicYear] = useState(initialData?.academicYear || "K24")
  const [major, setMajor] = useState(initialData?.major || "")


  const [customCohorts, setCustomCohorts] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [isCohortDialogOpen, setIsCohortDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [newCohortValue, setNewCohortValue] = useState("")
  
  // Combine base cohorts with any custom ones added during this session, and the initial academicYear if it's not in the base list
  const baseCohorts = ["K20", "K21", "K22", "K23", "K24", "K25"]
  const allCohorts = Array.from(new Set([...baseCohorts, ...customCohorts, academicYear])).filter(Boolean).sort()

  // 01. General Info
  // The course metadata would ideally come from the selected Course. For now we use the form inputs.
  const [courseNameEn, setCourseNameEn] = useState("")
  const [courseNameVn, setCourseNameVn] = useState("")
  const [courseCode, setCourseCode] = useState("")
  const [instructor, setInstructor] = useState("")
  const [creditsTheory, setCreditsTheory] = useState("0")
  const [creditsPractice, setCreditsPractice] = useState("0")
  const [ects, setEcts] = useState("6.64")
  const [periodsTheory, setPeriodsTheory] = useState("45")
  const [periodsPractice, setPeriodsPractice] = useState("30")

  const [courseDesignation, setCourseDesignation] = useState(initialData?.courseDesignation || "")
  const courseTypeOptions = ["General knowledge", "Fundamental", "Specialized knowledge", "Internship/Project/Thesis", "Others"]
  const [courseTypes, setCourseTypes] = useState<string[]>(parseJsonArray<string>(initialData?.courseTypes, []))
  const [semester, setSemester] = useState(initialData?.semester || "")
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState((initialData?.language as string) || "")
  const [relation, setRelation] = useState((initialData?.relation as string) || "")
  const [teachingMethods, setTeachingMethods] = useState((initialData?.teachingMethods as string) || "")
  const [workloadTotal, setWorkloadTotal] = useState((initialData?.workloadTotal as string) || "")
  const [workloadContact, setWorkloadContact] = useState((initialData?.workloadContact as string) || "")
  const [workloadPrivate, setWorkloadPrivate] = useState((initialData?.workloadPrivate as string) || "")
  const [prerequisites, setPrerequisites] = useState((initialData?.prerequisites as string) || "")
  const [objectives, setObjectives] = useState((initialData?.objectives as string) || "")

  // 02. Course learning outcomes
  const [clos, setClos] = useState(
    initialData?.clos?.map(c => ({ id: c.code, content: c.description, levels: [c.competencyLevel, c.bloomLevel].filter(Boolean) as string[] })) || []
  )

  const toggleCloLevel = (cloIdx: number, level: string) => {
    const newClos = [...clos]
    const cloLevels = newClos[cloIdx].levels
    if (cloLevels.includes(level)) {
      newClos[cloIdx].levels = cloLevels.filter(l => l !== level)
    } else {
      newClos[cloIdx].levels.push(level)
    }
    setClos(newClos)
  }

  // 03. Content
  const [topics, setTopics] = useState(
    initialData?.topics?.map(t => ({ name: t.name, weight: String(t.teachingHours || 0), level: t.notes || "I" })) || []
  )

  // 04. Examination forms & requirements
  const [examForms, setExamForms] = useState((initialData?.examForms as string) || "")
  const [examReqs, setExamReqs] = useState((initialData?.examRequirements as string) || "")

  // 05. References
  const [references, setReferences] = useState<string[]>([]) // Can be extracted from notes if stored there, or dedicated

  // 06. LO Matrix (CLO -> SLO mapping)
  const [loMatrix, setLoMatrix] = useState<Record<string, number[]>>({})

  const toggleLoMap = (cloId: string, sloNum: number) => {
    setLoMatrix(prev => {
      const existing = prev[cloId] || []
      if (existing.includes(sloNum)) {
        return { ...prev, [cloId]: existing.filter(x => x !== sloNum) }
      } else {
        return { ...prev, [cloId]: [...existing, sloNum] }
      }
    })
  }

  // 07. Planned learning activities
  const [activities, setActivities] = useState<any[]>([])

  // 08. Assessment Plan
  const [assessments, setAssessments] = useState(
    initialData?.assessments?.map(a => ({ type: a.name, clo1: "", clo2: "", clo3: "", weight: a.weightPercent })) || []
  )

  // 09. Rubrics
  const [rubrics, setRubrics] = useState((initialData?.rubrics as string) || "")

  // Validation Errors state
  const [validationErrors, setValidationErrors] = useState<{id: string, name: string}[]>([])

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()

    const errors: {id: string, name: string}[] = [];

    if (!academicYear.trim()) errors.push({ id: "academicYear", name: "Khóa (Academic Year)" });
    if (!major.trim()) errors.push({ id: "major", name: "Chuyên ngành (Major)" });
    if (!courseNameEn.trim()) errors.push({ id: "courseNameEn", name: "Course Name (English)" });
    if (!courseNameVn.trim()) errors.push({ id: "courseNameVn", name: "Course Name (Vietnamese)" });
    if (!courseCode.trim()) errors.push({ id: "courseCode", name: "Course Code" });
    if (!instructor.trim()) errors.push({ id: "instructor", name: "Instructor" });
    if (!creditsTheory.trim()) errors.push({ id: "creditsTheory", name: "Credits (Theory)" });
    if (!creditsPractice.trim()) errors.push({ id: "creditsPractice", name: "Credits (Practice)" });
    if (!ects.trim()) errors.push({ id: "ects", name: "ECTS" });
    if (!periodsTheory.trim()) errors.push({ id: "periodsTheory", name: "Periods (Theory)" });
    if (!periodsPractice.trim()) errors.push({ id: "periodsPractice", name: "Periods (Practice)" });
    if (!courseDesignation.trim()) errors.push({ id: "courseDesignation", name: "Course Designation" });
    if (!semester.trim()) errors.push({ id: "semester", name: "Semester" });
    if (!language.trim()) errors.push({ id: "language", name: "Language" });
    if (!relation.trim()) errors.push({ id: "relation", name: "Relation (Previous courses)" });
    if (!teachingMethods.trim()) errors.push({ id: "teachingMethods", name: "Teaching methods" });
    if (!workloadTotal.trim()) errors.push({ id: "workloadTotal", name: "Total Workload" });
    if (!workloadContact.trim()) errors.push({ id: "workloadContact", name: "Contact hours" });
    if (!workloadPrivate.trim()) errors.push({ id: "workloadPrivate", name: "Private study" });
    if (!prerequisites.trim()) errors.push({ id: "prerequisites", name: "Prerequisites" });
    if (!objectives.trim()) errors.push({ id: "objectives", name: "Course Objectives" });
    if (!examForms.trim()) errors.push({ id: "examForms", name: "Examination forms" });
    if (!examReqs.trim()) errors.push({ id: "examReqs", name: "Examination requirements" });
    // rubrics is optional

    if (!courseTypes || courseTypes.length === 0) {
      errors.push({ id: "courseTypes", name: "Course Type (Cơ sở, Chuyên ngành...)" });
    }

    const isClosFilled = clos && clos.length > 0 && clos.every(c => c.content && c.content.trim() !== "");
    if (!isClosFilled) errors.push({ id: "clos", name: "Course Learning Outcomes (CLOs)" });

    const isTopicsFilled = topics && topics.length > 0 && topics.every(t => t.name && t.name.trim() !== "");
    if (!isTopicsFilled) errors.push({ id: "topics", name: "Content (Topics) - Phải có ít nhất 1 chủ đề" });

    const isAssessmentsFilled = assessments && assessments.length > 0 && assessments.every(a => a.type && a.type.trim() !== "" && Number(a.weight) > 0);
    if (!isAssessmentsFilled) errors.push({ id: "assessments", name: "Assessment Plan - Phải có ít nhất 1 đánh giá với Trọng số > 0" });

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      setValidationErrors(errors);
      const fieldList = errors.map(e => "• " + e.name).join("\n");
      alert("Vui lòng điền đầy đủ " + errors.length + " trường còn thiếu:\n\n" + fieldList);
      setTimeout(() => {
        const firstErrorEl = document.getElementById(errors[0].id);
        if (firstErrorEl) {
          firstErrorEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
          firstErrorEl.focus();
        }
      }, 100);
      return;
    }

    // Clear errors on success
    setValidationErrors([]);

    let finalCourseId = 0;
    const existingCourse = courses.find(c =>
      c.courseCode && courseCode && c.courseCode.toLowerCase() === courseCode.toLowerCase().trim()
    );

    // <-- ĐOẠN SỬA LỖI TẠO MÔN HỌC BỊ HARDCODE DEPARTMENT ID -->
    if (existingCourse) {
      finalCourseId = existingCourse.id;
    } else {
      try {
        // Tránh lỗi hardcode 404 bằng cách lấy ID hợp lệ đầu tiên từ database
        const fallbackDeptId = departments.length > 0 ? departments[0].id : 1; 

        const newCourse = await courseApi.create({
          courseCode: courseCode || "NEW_CODE",
          name: courseNameEn || "New Course",
          nameVn: courseNameVn || "Môn học mới",
          departmentId: fallbackDeptId, // Đã thay thế số 1 bằng fallbackDeptId
          creditTheory: parseInt(creditsTheory) || 0,
          creditLab: parseInt(creditsPractice) || 0,
          courseLevel: "UNDERGRADUATE"
        });
        finalCourseId = newCourse.id;
        setCourses([...courses, newCourse]);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to create new course inline", err);
        alert("Không thể tự động tạo môn học mới. Vui lòng tạo môn học trong mục Quản lý môn học trước.");
        return;
      }
    }
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const payload: CreateSyllabusRequest = {
      ...form,
      courseId: finalCourseId,
      versionLabel,
      academicYear,
      
      courseDesignation,
  courseTypes: JSON.stringify(courseTypes),

  semester,
  language,

  relation,

  teachingMethods,

  workloadTotal,
  workloadContact,
  workloadPrivate,

  prerequisites,

  objectives,

  examForms,

  examRequirements: examReqs,

  rubrics,

  major,
      createdBy: user?.userId ?? 1,
      clos: clos.map((c, i) => ({
        code: `CLO${i + 1}`,
        description: c.content,
        orderIndex: i,
        competencyLevel: normalizeCloLevel(c.levels),
        bloomLevel: "APPLY"
      })),
      topics: topics.map((t, i) => ({
        name: t.name,
        weekNumber: i + 1,
        orderInWeek: 1,

        teachingHours: Number(t.weight) || 0,
        labHours: 0,
        selfStudyHours: 0,

        topicType: "LECTURE",
        teachingMethod: "",
        learningActivity: "",

        notes: t.level
      })),
      assessments: assessments.map((a, i) => {
        let astType = "ASSIGNMENT";
        const n = (a.type || "").toLowerCase();
        if (n.includes("mid") || n.includes("giữa kỳ") || n.includes("giữa kì")) astType = "MIDTERM_EXAM";
        else if (n.includes("final") || n.includes("cuối kỳ") || n.includes("cuối kì")) astType = "FINAL_EXAM";
        else if (n.includes("quiz") || n.includes("trắc nghiệm")) astType = "QUIZ";
        else if (n.includes("lab") || n.includes("thực hành") || n.includes("báo cáo")) astType = "LAB_REPORT";
        else if (n.includes("project") || n.includes("đồ án") || n.includes("tiểu luận")) astType = "PROJECT";
        else if (n.includes("presentation") || n.includes("thuyết trình")) astType = "PRESENTATION";
        else if (n.includes("participation") || n.includes("chuyên cần") || n.includes("phát biểu")) astType = "PARTICIPATION";
        
        return {
          name: a.type,
          assessmentType: astType,
          weightPercent: Number(a.weight) || 0,

          minScore: 0,
          maxScore: 100,

          orderIndex: i
        };
      }),
      notes: JSON.stringify({
        instructor,
        creditsTheory,
        creditsPractice,
        ects,
        periodsTheory,
        periodsPractice,
        references,
        loMatrix,
        activities,
        assessmentMatrix: assessments.map(a => ({
          type: a.type,
          clo1: a.clo1,
          clo2: a.clo2,
          clo3: a.clo3,
          weight: a.weight,
        }))
      })
    }

    onSubmit(payload)
  }

  const addClo = () => {
    const uniqueId = `CLO_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`;
    setClos([...clos, { id: uniqueId, content: "", levels: [] }]);
  }
  const removeClo = (idx: number) => setClos(clos.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))
  const addTopic = () => setTopics([...topics, { name: "", weight: "", level: "" }])
  const removeTopic = (idx: number) => setTopics(topics.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))
  const addReference = () => setReferences([...references, ""])
  const addActivity = () => setActivities([...activities, { week: "", topic: "", clo: "", assessments: "", activities: "", resources: "" }])
  const removeActivity = (idx: number) => setActivities(activities.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))
  const addAssessment = () => setAssessments([...assessments, { type: "", clo1: "", clo2: "", clo3: "", weight: 0 }])

  const cardClasses = "bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-t-[3px] border-t-amber-400 shadow-[0_2px_10px_-3px_rgba(6,81,237,0.05)]"
  const inputClasses = "h-10 bg-amber-50/20 border-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-amber-400/20 rounded-lg text-slate-800 font-medium"
  const labelClasses = "block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5"

  const getErrorClass = (id: string) => {
    return validationErrors.some(e => e.id === id) ? "border-red-500 ring-red-500 bg-red-50/50" : "";
  }
  
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-12">
        {/* 01. THÔNG TIN CHUNG */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">01</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">General Information</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Mã môn, tên tiếng Anh, tín chỉ và thông tin cơ bản.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className={`${cardClasses} p-6 md:p-8 space-y-6`}>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-x-8 gap-y-6 pb-6 border-b border-slate-100">
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Khóa áp dụng (Cohort)</label>
              <Select 
                value={academicYear} 
                onValueChange={(val) => {
                  if (val === "ADD_NEW") {
                    setIsCohortDialogOpen(true);
                  } else {
                    setAcademicYear(val);
                  }
                }}
              >
                <SelectTrigger className={inputClasses}>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn khóa" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {allCohorts.map(c => (
                    <SelectItem key={c} value={c}>{c}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                  <SelectItem value="ADD_NEW" className="font-bold text-brand-700 bg-brand-50 mt-1 cursor-pointer">
                    + Thêm khóa mới...
                  </SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Chuyên ngành (Major)</label>
              <Select value={major} onValueChange={setMajor}>
                <SelectTrigger className={inputClasses}>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn chuyên ngành" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {MAJORS.map(m => (
                    <SelectItem key={m} value={m}>{m}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Phiên bản (Version)</label>
              <Input className={inputClasses} value={versionLabel} onChange={(e) => setVersionLabel(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-x-8 gap-y-6">
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Course Name (English)</label>
              <Input id="courseNameEn" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("courseNameEn"))} value={courseNameEn} onChange={(e) => setCourseNameEn(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "courseNameEn") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Course Name (Vietnamese)</label>
              <Input id="courseNameVn" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("courseNameVn"))} value={courseNameVn} onChange={(e) => setCourseNameVn(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "courseNameVn") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Course Code</label>
              <Input id="courseCode" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("courseCode"))} value={courseCode} onChange={(e) => {
                const newCode = e.target.value;
                setCourseCode(newCode);
                // Optionally auto-fill if they type an existing course code
                const existing = courses.find(c => c.courseCode && c.courseCode.toLowerCase() === newCode.toLowerCase().trim());
                if (existing) {
                  setCourseNameEn(existing.name);
                  setCourseNameVn(existing.nameVn);
                  setCreditsTheory(existing.creditTheory?.toString() || "0");
                  setCreditsPractice(existing.creditLab?.toString() || "0");
                }
              }} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "courseCode") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Instructor</label>
              <Input id="instructor" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("instructor"))} value={instructor} onChange={(e) => setInstructor(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "instructor") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Credits Theory</label>
              <Input id="creditsTheory" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("creditsTheory"))} value={creditsTheory} onChange={(e) => setCreditsTheory(e.target.value)} type="number" />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "creditsTheory") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Credits Practice/Lab</label>
              <Input id="creditsPractice" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("creditsPractice"))} value={creditsPractice} onChange={(e) => setCreditsPractice(e.target.value)} type="number" />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "creditsPractice") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={labelClasses}>Course designation</label>
            <textarea id="courseDesignation"
              className={cn("w-full border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm focus:border-amber-400 outline-none resize-y min-h-[60px] text-slate-700 bg-amber-50/20", getErrorClass("courseDesignation"))}
              value={courseDesignation} onChange={(e) => setCourseDesignation(e.target.value)}
            />
            {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "courseDesignation") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-x-8 gap-y-6">
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Course type</label>
              <div className="space-y-2 mt-2">
                {courseTypeOptions.map(opt => (
                  <label key={opt} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
                    <input 
                      type="checkbox" 
                      className="rounded border-slate-300 text-amber-500 focus:ring-amber-400"
                      checked={courseTypes.includes(opt)}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        if (e.target.checked) setCourseTypes([...courseTypes, opt])
                        else setCourseTypes(courseTypes.filter(x => x !== opt))
                      }}
                    />
                    {opt}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "courseTypes") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng chọn ít nhất 1 loại môn học (Please check at least one)</span>}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div>
                <label className={labelClasses}>Semester(s) in which the course is taught</label>
                <Select value={semester} onValueChange={setSemester}>
  <SelectTrigger id="semester" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("semester"))}>
    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn học kỳ" />
  </SelectTrigger>
  <SelectContent>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 1">Semester 1</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 2">Semester 2</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 3">Semester 3</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 4">Semester 4</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 5">Semester 5</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 6">Semester 6</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 7">Semester 7</SelectItem>
    <SelectItem value="Semester 8">Semester 8</SelectItem>
  </SelectContent>
</Select>
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "semester") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className={labelClasses}>Language</label>
                <Input id="language" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("language"))} value={language} onChange={(e) => setLanguage(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "language") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={labelClasses}>Relation to curriculum</label>
            <textarea id="relation"
              className={cn("w-full border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm focus:border-amber-400 outline-none resize-y min-h-[60px] text-slate-700 bg-amber-50/20", getErrorClass("relation"))}
              value={relation} onChange={(e) => setRelation(e.target.value)}
            />
            {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "relation") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={labelClasses}>Teaching methods</label>
            <Input id="teachingMethods" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("teachingMethods"))} value={teachingMethods} onChange={(e) => setTeachingMethods(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "teachingMethods") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
          </div>

          <div className="border border-slate-100 rounded-lg p-4 bg-slate-50/50 space-y-4">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">Workload</h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className={labelClasses}>Total Workload</label>
                <Input id="workloadTotal" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("workloadTotal"))} value={workloadTotal} onChange={(e) => setWorkloadTotal(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "workloadTotal") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className={labelClasses}>Contact hours</label>
                <Input id="workloadContact" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("workloadContact"))} value={workloadContact} onChange={(e) => setWorkloadContact(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "workloadContact") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className={labelClasses}>Private study</label>
                <Input id="workloadPrivate" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("workloadPrivate"))} value={workloadPrivate} onChange={(e) => setWorkloadPrivate(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "workloadPrivate") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Credits (Theory)</label>
              <Input id="creditsTheory" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("creditsTheory"))} value={creditsTheory} onChange={(e) => setCreditsTheory(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "creditsTheory") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>Credits (Practice)</label>
              <Input id="creditsPractice" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("creditsPractice"))} value={creditsPractice} onChange={(e) => setCreditsPractice(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "creditsPractice") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClasses}>ECTS</label>
              <Input id="ects" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("ects"))} value={ects} onChange={(e) => setEcts(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "ects") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
            </div>
            <div className="col-span-1">
              <label className={labelClasses}>Periods (T/P)</label>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <Input id="periodsTheory" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("periodsTheory"))} placeholder="Theory" value={periodsTheory} onChange={(e) => setPeriodsTheory(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "periodsTheory") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
                <Input id="periodsPractice" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("periodsPractice"))} placeholder="Practice" value={periodsPractice} onChange={(e) => setPeriodsPractice(e.target.value)} />
              {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "periodsPractice") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={labelClasses}>Required and recommended prerequisites</label>
            <Input id="prerequisites" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("prerequisites"))} value={prerequisites} onChange={(e) => setPrerequisites(e.target.value)} />
            {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "prerequisites") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={labelClasses}>Course objectives</label>
            <textarea id="objectives"
              className={cn("w-full border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm focus:border-amber-400 outline-none resize-y min-h-[100px] text-slate-700 bg-amber-50/20", getErrorClass("objectives"))}
              value={objectives} onChange={(e) => setObjectives(e.target.value)}
            />
            {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "objectives") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 02. COURSE LEARNING OUTCOMES */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">02</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Course Learning Outcomes</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Chuẩn đầu ra môn học và Mức độ năng lực (Competency level).</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={`${cardClasses} overflow-hidden`}>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm text-left whitespace-nowrap min-w-[600px]">
              <thead className="bg-amber-50/50 border-b border-amber-100/50 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-6 py-4 w-16">CLO</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-4 w-full min-w-[300px]">Nội dung</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center w-24">Knowledge</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center w-24">Skill</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center w-24">Attitude</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4 w-12"></th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {clos.map((clo, idx) => (
                  <tr key={idx} className="hover:bg-slate-50/50 transition-colors group">
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-slate-800 align-top">CLO {idx + 1}</td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-3">
                      <textarea 
                        className="w-full resize-none border border-transparent group-hover:border-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 bg-transparent p-2 rounded-lg outline-none transition-colors text-slate-700 whitespace-normal" 
                        rows={2} 
                        value={clo.content} 
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          const newClos = [...clos]
                          newClos[idx].content = e.target.value
                          setClos(newClos)
                        }}
                      />
                    </td>
                    {['Knowledge', 'Skill', 'Attitude'].map(level => (
                      <td key={level} className="px-6 py-4 text-center align-top pt-5">
                        <input 
                          type="checkbox" 
                          className="size-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-amber-500 focus:ring-amber-400 cursor-pointer"
                          checked={clo.levels.includes(level)}
                          onChange={() => toggleCloLevel(idx, level)}
                        />
                      </td>
                    ))}
                    <td className="px-4 py-4 align-top pt-4">
                      <Button variant="ghost" type="button" onClick={() => removeClo(idx)} className="h-8 w-8 p-0 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50">
                        <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                      </Button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/30">
            <button type="button" onClick={addClo} className="flex items-center text-sm font-semibold text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-700 transition-colors">
              <Plus className="mr-1.5 size-4" /> Thêm CLO
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 03. CONTENT */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">03</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Content</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Weight: lecture session (3 hours) · Level: I = Introduce, T = Teach, U = Utilize.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={`${cardClasses} overflow-hidden`}>
          <table id="topics" className={cn("w-full text-sm text-left transition-all", getErrorClass("topics") && "ring-2 ring-red-500")}>
            <thead className="bg-amber-50/50 border-b border-amber-100/50 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-6 py-4">Topic</th>
                <th className="px-6 py-4 w-32 text-center">Weight</th>
                <th className="px-6 py-4 w-32 text-center">Level</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-4 w-12"></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {topics.map((topic, idx) => (
                <tr key={idx} className="hover:bg-slate-50/50 transition-colors group">
                  <td className="px-6 py-3">
                    <Input 
                      className="h-10 border-transparent group-hover:border-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 shadow-none bg-transparent text-slate-800 font-medium px-2" 
                      value={topic.name} 
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const newTopics = [...topics]
                        newTopics[idx].name = e.target.value
                        setTopics(newTopics)
                      }}
                    />
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-3">
                    <Input 
                      className="h-10 font-mono text-sm text-center border-transparent group-hover:border-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 shadow-none bg-transparent text-slate-800 font-bold" 
                      value={topic.weight} 
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const newTopics = [...topics]
                        newTopics[idx].weight = e.target.value
                        setTopics(newTopics)
                      }}
                    />
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-3 text-center">
                    <Input 
                      className="h-10 text-center border-transparent group-hover:border-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 shadow-none bg-transparent text-amber-800 font-bold uppercase" 
                      value={topic.level} 
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const newTopics = [...topics]
                        newTopics[idx].level = e.target.value
                        setTopics(newTopics)
                      }}
                    />
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <Button variant="ghost" type="button" onClick={() => removeTopic(idx)} className="h-8 w-8 p-0 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50">
                      <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                    </Button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/30">
            <button type="button" onClick={addTopic} className="flex items-center text-sm font-semibold text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-700 transition-colors">
              <Plus className="mr-1.5 size-4" /> Thêm Topic
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 04. EXAMINATION & REQUIREMENTS */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">04</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Examination & Requirements</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Hình thức kiểm tra, yêu cầu khóa học và tài liệu tham khảo.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className={`${cardClasses} p-6 space-y-6`}>
          <div>
            <label className={labelClasses}>Examination forms</label>
            <Input id="examForms" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("examForms"))} value={examForms} onChange={(e) => setExamForms(e.target.value)} />
            {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "examForms") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={labelClasses}>Study and examination requirements</label>
            <textarea id="examReqs"
              className={cn("w-full min-h-[100px] border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400/20 outline-none resize-y text-slate-700 bg-amber-50/10", getErrorClass("examReqs"))}
              value={examReqs}
              onChange={(e) => setExamReqs(e.target.value)}
            />
            {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "examReqs") && <span className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-1.5 block font-medium">* Vui lòng điền thông tin trường này (Please fill out this field)</span>}
          </div>

          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-6 mt-6">
            <label className={labelClasses}>Reading list</label>
            <div className="space-y-3 mt-2">
              {references.map((ref, idx) => (
                <div key={idx} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-sm w-6 text-right">{idx + 1}.</span>
                  <Input 
                    className="flex-1 h-12 bg-white border-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-amber-400/20 text-slate-700" 
                    value={ref}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const newRefs = [...references]
                      newRefs[idx] = e.target.value
                      setReferences(newRefs)
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <button type="button" onClick={addReference} className="mt-4 flex items-center text-sm font-semibold text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-700 transition-colors">
              <Plus className="mr-1.5 size-4" /> Thêm tài liệu
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 05. LO MATRIX */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">05</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Learning Outcomes Matrix</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Relationship between CLOs and Student Learning Outcomes (SLO).</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={`${cardClasses} p-6 overflow-x-auto`}>
          <table className="w-auto border-collapse">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase"></th>
                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(slo => (
                  <th key={slo} className="border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase w-12 text-center">
                    SLO<br/>{slo}
                  </th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {clos.map((clo, idx) => (
                <tr key={clo.id}>
                  <td className="border border-slate-200 px-6 py-3 font-bold text-slate-700 text-sm bg-slate-50">
                    CLO {idx + 1}
                  </td>
                  {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(slo => {
                    const isChecked = (loMatrix[clo.id] || []).includes(slo)
                    return (
                      <td key={slo} className="border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 text-center cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-50" onClick={() => toggleLoMap(clo.id, slo)}>
                        {isChecked && <span className="font-bold text-amber-600">x</span>}
                      </td>
                    )
                  })}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 06. PLANNED LEARNING ACTIVITIES */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">06</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Planned Learning Activities</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Lịch trình giảng dạy chi tiết theo tuần.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={`${cardClasses} overflow-hidden`}>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm text-left min-w-[800px]">
              <thead className="bg-amber-50/50 border-b border-amber-100/50 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4 w-16">Week</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4 w-64">Topic</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4 w-24">CLO</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4 w-40">Assessments</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4 w-40">Learning Activities</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4">Resources</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-4 w-12"></th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {activities.map((act, idx) => (
                  <tr key={idx} className="hover:bg-slate-50/50 transition-colors group">
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <Input className="h-9 border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 bg-transparent text-center font-bold px-1" value={act.week} onChange={(e) => { const a = [...activities]; a[idx].week = e.target.value; setActivities(a) }} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <textarea rows={2} className="w-full h-full resize-none border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 bg-transparent p-2 text-sm outline-none" value={act.topic} onChange={(e) => { const a = [...activities]; a[idx].topic = e.target.value; setActivities(a) }} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <Input className="h-9 border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 bg-transparent text-center px-1" value={act.clo} onChange={(e) => { const a = [...activities]; a[idx].clo = e.target.value; setActivities(a) }} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <textarea rows={2} className="w-full h-full resize-none border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 bg-transparent p-2 text-sm outline-none" value={act.assessments} onChange={(e) => { const a = [...activities]; a[idx].assessments = e.target.value; setActivities(a) }} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <textarea rows={2} className="w-full h-full resize-none border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 bg-transparent p-2 text-sm outline-none" value={act.activities} onChange={(e) => { const a = [...activities]; a[idx].activities = e.target.value; setActivities(a) }} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <textarea rows={2} className="w-full h-full resize-none border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 bg-transparent p-2 text-sm outline-none" value={act.resources} onChange={(e) => { const a = [...activities]; a[idx].resources = e.target.value; setActivities(a) }} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-center">
                      <Button variant="ghost" type="button" onClick={() => removeActivity(idx)} className="h-8 w-8 p-0 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50">
                        <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                      </Button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/30">
            <button type="button" onClick={addActivity} className="flex items-center text-sm font-semibold text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-700 transition-colors">
              <Plus className="mr-1.5 size-4" /> Thêm Tuần học
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 07. ASSESSMENT PLAN */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">07</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Assessment Plan</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Phân bổ trọng số của các hình thức đánh giá cho từng CLO.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="assessments" className={`${cardClasses} p-6 overflow-x-auto`}>
          <table className="w-full border-collapse min-w-[600px]">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-amber-50/50 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 text-left">Assessment Type</th>
                <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase w-24 text-center">CLO 1</th>
                <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase w-24 text-center">CLO 2</th>
                <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase w-24 text-center">CLO 3</th>
                <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-amber-100/60 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-amber-700 uppercase w-28 text-center">Weight (%)</th>
                <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-3 w-10"></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {assessments.map((ast, idx) => (
                <tr key={idx}>
                  <td className="border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2">
                    <Input className="h-9 border-transparent hover:border-slate-200 focus:border-amber-400 shadow-none text-sm font-medium w-full" value={ast.type} onChange={e => { const a = [...assessments]; a[idx].type = e.target.value; setAssessments(a) }} />
                  </td>
                  <td className="border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2">
                    <Input className="h-9 text-center border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 w-full" value={ast.clo1} onChange={e => { const a = [...assessments]; a[idx].clo1 = e.target.value; setAssessments(a) }} />
                  </td>
                  <td className="border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2">
                    <Input className="h-9 text-center border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 w-full" value={ast.clo2} onChange={e => { const a = [...assessments]; a[idx].clo2 = e.target.value; setAssessments(a) }} />
                  </td>
                  <td className="border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2">
                    <Input className="h-9 text-center border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 w-full" value={ast.clo3} onChange={e => { const a = [...assessments]; a[idx].clo3 = e.target.value; setAssessments(a) }} />
                  </td>
                  <td className="border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2">
                    <Input
                      type="number" min="0" max="100"
                      className="h-9 text-center border-transparent focus:border-amber-400 w-full font-bold text-amber-700"
                      value={ast.weight === 0 ? "" : ast.weight}
                      placeholder="0"
                      onChange={e => { const a = [...assessments]; a[idx].weight = Number(e.target.value); setAssessments(a) }}
                    />
                  </td>
                  <td className="border border-slate-200 px-2 py-2 text-center">
                    <Button variant="ghost" type="button" size="sm" onClick={() => setAssessments(assessments.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))} className="h-7 w-7 p-0 text-slate-300 hover:text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50">
                      <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                    </Button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          {validationErrors.some(e => e.id === "assessments") && (
            <p className="text-red-500 text-[11px] mt-2 font-medium">* Phải có ít nhất 1 đánh giá với Hình thức và Trọng số &gt; 0</p>
          )}
          <button type="button" onClick={addAssessment} className="mt-4 flex items-center text-sm font-semibold text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-700 transition-colors">
            <Plus className="mr-1.5 size-4" /> Thêm Assessment
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 08. RUBRICS */}
      <section>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
          <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold text-xl mt-0.5">08</span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">Rubrics</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Holistic and Analytic rubrics for evaluating tasks and exams.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {/* 5.1 Grading Checklist */}
          <div className={`${cardClasses} p-6 overflow-x-auto`}>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 mb-4 text-sm">5.1. Grading checklist (Written Reports)</h3>
            <textarea
              className="w-full min-h-[100px] border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400/20 outline-none resize-y text-slate-700 bg-amber-50/10"
              placeholder="Grading checklist details..."
              value={rubrics}
              onChange={(e) => setRubrics(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          {/* 5.2 Holistic Rubric */}
          <div className={`${cardClasses} p-6 overflow-x-auto`}>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 mb-4 text-sm">5.2. Holistic rubric</h3>
            <table className="w-full border-collapse min-w-[600px] text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-amber-50/50 px-4 py-2 w-20 text-center font-bold">Score</th>
                  <th className="border border-slate-200 bg-amber-50/50 px-4 py-2 font-bold text-left">Description</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="bg-white">
                {[
                  { score: "5", desc: "Demonstrates complete understanding of the problem. All requirements of task are included in response" },
                  { score: "4", desc: "Demonstrates considerable understanding of the problem. All requirements of task are included." },
                  { score: "3", desc: "Demonstrates partial understanding of the problem. Most requirements of task are included." },
                  { score: "2", desc: "Demonstrates little understanding of the problem. Many requirements of task are missing." },
