<file path="src/pages/admin/InstructorManagementPage.tsx">
import { useState, useMemo } from "react"
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import {
  Plus,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Download,
  Search,
  Eye,
  Calendar,
  Edit3,
  Power,
  Users,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
  ShieldCheck,
  Clock,
  Filter,
  RefreshCw
} from "lucide-react"

import { instructorApi } from "@/api/instructorApi"
import { departmentApi } from "@/api/departmentApi"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select"
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table"
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog"
import type { UserRole } from "@/types/auth"
import type { Instructor } from "@/types/instructor"

const ROLE_LABELS: Record<UserRole, string> = {
  ADMIN: "Admin (Thư ký khoa)",
  DEAN: "Trưởng khoa",
  DEPT_HEAD: "Trưởng bộ môn",
  INSTRUCTOR: "Giảng viên",
  PROGRAM_COORDINATOR: "Điều phối viên",
  STUDENT: "Sinh viên"
}

// Initial avatar background colors based on initials
const AVATAR_COLORS = [
  "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200",
  "bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border-purple-200",
  "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200",
  "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-200",
  "bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border-rose-200",
  "bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 border-indigo-200",
  "bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border-cyan-200"
]

export default function InstructorManagementPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<"list" | "schedule">("list")

  // Filters State
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("")
  const [filterDept, setFilterDept] = useState("all")
  const [filterRole, setFilterRole] = useState("all")
  const [filterDegree, setFilterDegree] = useState("all")
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState("all")

  // Dialog State
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [dialogMode, setDialogMode] = useState<"create" | "edit" | "view" | "assign">("create")
  const [selectedInstructor, setSelectedInstructor] = useState<Instructor | null>(null)

  // Form State
  const [staffCode, setStaffCode] = useState("")
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState("")
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("")
  const [degree, setDegree] = useState("PhD")
  const [academicRank, setAcademicRank] = useState("Lecturer")
  const [departmentId, setDepartmentId] = useState("")
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(true)
  const [role, setRole] = useState<UserRole>("INSTRUCTOR")

  // React Query Fetching
  const { data: instructors, isLoading, isError } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["instructors"],
    queryFn: instructorApi.getAll
  })

  const { data: departments } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["departments"],
    queryFn: departmentApi.getAll
  })

  // Mutations
  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: instructorApi.create,
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["instructors"] })
      setOpen(false)
      resetForm()
    },
    onError: (err: any) => {
      alert(err?.response?.data?.message || "Lỗi khi thêm giảng viên")
    }
  })

  const updateMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, data }: { id: number; data: any }) => instructorApi.update(id, data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["instructors"] })
      setOpen(false)
      resetForm()
    },
    onError: (err: any) => {
      alert(err?.response?.data?.message || "Lỗi khi cập nhật thông tin")
    }
  })

  const toggleStatusMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, data }: { id: number; data: any }) => instructorApi.update(id, data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["instructors"] })
    },
    onError: (err: any) => {
      alert(err?.response?.data?.message || "Lỗi khi cập nhật trạng thái")
    }
  })

  // Reset form helper
  const resetForm = () => {
    setStaffCode("")
    setFullName("")
    setEmail("")
    setDegree("PhD")
    setAcademicRank("Lecturer")
    setDepartmentId("")
    setIsActive(true)
    setRole("INSTRUCTOR")
    setSelectedInstructor(null)
  }

  // Handle open dialog for create/edit/view
  const handleOpenDialog = (mode: "create" | "edit" | "view" | "assign", instructor?: Instructor) => {
    setDialogMode(mode)
    if (instructor) {
      setSelectedInstructor(instructor)
      setStaffCode(instructor.staffCode)
      setFullName(instructor.fullName)
      setEmail(instructor.email)
      setDegree(instructor.degree || "PhD")
      setAcademicRank(instructor.academicRank || "Lecturer")
      setDepartmentId(instructor.departmentId.toString())
      setIsActive(instructor.isActive)
      setRole(instructor.role || "INSTRUCTOR")
    } else {
      resetForm()
    }
    setOpen(true)
  }

  // Handle Form Submit
  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!staffCode || !fullName || !email || !departmentId) {
      alert("Vui lòng điền đầy đủ các trường thông tin bắt buộc")
      return
    }

    const payload = {
      staffCode,
      fullName,
      email,
      degree,
      academicRank,
      departmentId: parseInt(departmentId),
      isActive,
      role
    }

    if (dialogMode === "create") {
      createMutation.mutate(payload)
    } else if (dialogMode === "edit" && selectedInstructor) {
      updateMutation.mutate({ id: selectedInstructor.id, data: payload })
    }
  }

  // Toggle active status
  const handleToggleStatus = (instructor: Instructor) => {
    const confirmation = window.confirm(
      `Bạn có chắc chắn muốn ${instructor.isActive ? "vô hiệu hóa" : "kích hoạt"} tài khoản của giảng viên ${instructor.fullName}?`
    )
    if (confirmation) {
      toggleStatusMutation.mutate({
        id: instructor.id,
        data: {
          staffCode: instructor.staffCode,
          fullName: instructor.fullName,
          email: instructor.email,
          degree: instructor.degree,
          academicRank: instructor.academicRank,
          departmentId: instructor.departmentId,
          isActive: !instructor.isActive,
          role: instructor.role
        }
      })
    }
  }

  // Computed statistics cards
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    if (!instructors) return { total: 0, phd: 0, msc: 0, active: 0, inactive: 0, leads: 0 }
    
    let phd = 0
    let msc = 0
    let active = 0
    let inactive = 0
    let leads = 0

    instructors.forEach((inst) => {
      // Degree check
      const d = inst.degree?.toUpperCase() || ""
      if (d.includes("PHD") || d.includes("TS") || d.includes("TIẾN SĨ")) {
        phd++
      } else if (d.includes("MSC") || d.includes("THS") || d.includes("THẠC SĨ")) {
        msc++
      }
      
      // Status check
      if (inst.isActive) {
        active++
      } else {
        inactive++
      }

      // Role check (Dean or Department Head)
      if (inst.role === "DEAN" || inst.role === "DEPT_HEAD") {
        leads++
      }
    })

    return {
      total: instructors.length,
      phd,
      msc,
      active,
      inactive,
      leads
    }
  }, [instructors])

  // Filtered Instructor List
  const filteredInstructors = useMemo(() => {
    if (!instructors) return []
    return instructors.filter((inst) => {
      // Search filter
      const searchLower = searchQuery.toLowerCase()
      const matchesSearch =
        inst.staffCode.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower) ||
        inst.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower) ||
        inst.email.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower)

      // Department filter
      const matchesDept = filterDept === "all" || inst.departmentId.toString() === filterDept

      // Role filter
      const matchesRole = filterRole === "all" || inst.role === filterRole

      // Degree filter
      const matchesDegree =
        filterDegree === "all" ||
        (filterDegree === "PHD" && (inst.degree?.toUpperCase().includes("PHD") || inst.degree?.toUpperCase().includes("TS"))) ||
        (filterDegree === "MSC" && (inst.degree?.toUpperCase().includes("MSC") || inst.degree?.toUpperCase().includes("THS")))

      // Status filter
      const matchesStatus =
        filterStatus === "all" ||
        (filterStatus === "active" && inst.isActive) ||
        (filterStatus === "inactive" && !inst.isActive)

      return matchesSearch && matchesDept && matchesRole && matchesDegree && matchesStatus
    })
  }, [instructors, searchQuery, filterDept, filterRole, filterDegree, filterStatus])

  // Get initials for Avatar
  const getInitials = (name: string) => {
    if (!name) return ""
    const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/)
    if (parts.length >= 2) {
      return (parts[parts.length - 2][0] + parts[parts.length - 1][0]).toUpperCase()
    }
    return name[0]?.toUpperCase() || ""
  }

  // Get Avatar color class based on instructor ID
  const getAvatarColor = (id: number) => {
    return AVATAR_COLORS[id % AVATAR_COLORS.length]
  }

  // Reset all filters
  const handleClearFilters = () => {
    setSearchQuery("")
    setFilterDept("all")
    setFilterRole("all")
    setFilterDegree("all")
    setFilterStatus("all")
  }

  // Mock excel actions
  const handleImportExcel = () => {
    alert("Tính năng Nhập Excel: Vui lòng chọn file mẫu .xlsx để cập nhật danh sách.")
  }

  const handleExportExcel = () => {
    alert("Đang xuất danh sách giảng viên ra Excel...")
    // Simple CSV export simulation
    const headers = "Mã GV,Họ và Tên,Email,Bộ môn,Vai trò,Học vị,Số môn,Trạng thái\n"
    const rows = filteredInstructors
      .map(
        (inst) =>
          `"${inst.staffCode}","${inst.fullName}","${inst.email}","${inst.departmentNameVn}","${
            inst.role ? ROLE_LABELS[inst.role] : "Giảng viên"
          }","${inst.degree}","${inst.courseCount || 0}","${inst.isActive ? "Hoạt động" : "Ngừng hoạt động"}"`
      )
      .join("\n")
    
    const blob = new Blob([headers + rows], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" })
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
    const link = document.createElement("a")
    link.setAttribute("href", url)
    link.setAttribute("download", "Danh_sach_giang_vien_SCSE.csv")
    document.body.appendChild(link)
    link.click()
    document.body.removeChild(link)
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* 4 Stats Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
        {/* Stat 1: Tổng giảng viên */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Tổng giảng viên</p>
              <h3 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-2 font-heading">{isLoading ? "..." : stats.total}</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">{stats.phd} tiến sĩ - {stats.msc} thạc sĩ</p>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-blue-50 p-3 text-blue-600 border border-blue-100">
              <Users className="size-5" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Stat 2: Đang hoạt động */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Đang hoạt động</p>
              <h3 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-2 font-heading">{isLoading ? "..." : stats.active}</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">{stats.inactive} ngừng / nghỉ</p>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 p-3 text-emerald-600 border border-emerald-100">
              <CheckCircle2 className="size-5" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Stat 3: Trưởng khoa / BM */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Trưởng khoa / BM</p>
              <h3 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-2 font-heading">{isLoading ? "..." : stats.leads}</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Phân theo 4 bộ môn của SCSE</p>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-purple-50 p-3 text-purple-600 border border-purple-100">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-5" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Stat 4: Phân công chờ xác nhận */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Phân công chờ xác nhận</p>
              <h3 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-2 font-heading">2</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Cần GV xác nhận trong tuần</p>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-50 p-3 text-amber-600 border border-amber-100">
              <Clock className="size-5" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Title block */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-4 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-heading">Quản lý giảng viên</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Danh sách giảng viên, vai trò và phân công giảng dạy của SCSE
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={handleImportExcel}>
            <FileSpreadsheet className="size-4 text-emerald-600" />
            Nhập Excel
          </Button>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={handleExportExcel}>
            <Download className="size-4 text-blue-600" />
            Xuất danh sách
          </Button>
          <Button className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/95 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm" onClick={() => handleOpenDialog("create")}>
            <Plus className="size-4" />
            Thêm giảng viên
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Tabs */}
      <div className="flex border-b border-slate-200">
        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab("list")}
          className={`px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold border-b-2 transition-colors ${
            activeTab === "list"
              ? "border-primary text-primary"
              : "border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700"
          }`}
        >
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Users className="size-4" /> Danh sách giảng viên
          </span>
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab("schedule")}
          className={`px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold border-b-2 transition-colors ${
            activeTab === "schedule"
              ? "border-primary text-primary"
              : "border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700"
          }`}
        >
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Calendar className="size-4" /> Phân công giảng dạy
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {activeTab === "list" && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Filters Row */}
          <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              {/* Department filter */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase">Bộ môn</Label>
                <Select value={filterDept} onValueChange={setFilterDept}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="w-full bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-sm">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Tất cả bộ môn" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                    <SelectItem value="all">Tất cả bộ môn</SelectItem>
                    {departments?.map((d) => (
                      <SelectItem key={d.id} value={d.id.toString()}>
                        {d.nameVn}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              {/* Role filter */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase">Vai trò</Label>
                <Select value={filterRole} onValueChange={setFilterRole}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="w-full bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-sm">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Tất cả vai trò" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                    <SelectItem value="all">Tất cả vai trò</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="DEAN">Trưởng khoa</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="DEPT_HEAD">Trưởng bộ môn</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="PROGRAM_COORDINATOR">Điều phối viên</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="INSTRUCTOR">Giảng viên</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              {/* Degree filter */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase">Học vị</Label>
                <Select value={filterDegree} onValueChange={setFilterDegree}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="w-full bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-sm">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Tất cả học vị" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                    <SelectItem value="all">Tất cả học vị</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="PHD">PhD (Tiến sĩ)</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="MSC">MSc (Thạc sĩ)</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              {/* Status filter */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase">Trạng thái</Label>
                <Select value={filterStatus} onValueChange={setFilterStatus}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="w-full bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-sm">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Tất cả trạng thái" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                    <SelectItem value="all">Tất cả trạng thái</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="active">Hoạt động</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="inactive">Ngừng hoạt động</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
              {/* Search text filter */}
              <div className="relative w-full md:w-96">
                <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                <Input
                  placeholder="Tìm theo mã, họ tên hoặc email..."
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-9 bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 placeholder-slate-400 text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Action filter buttons */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto justify-end">
                <Button onClick={handleClearFilters} variant="outline" size="sm" className="border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-1">
                  Bỏ lọc
                </Button>
                <Button className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/95 size-sm text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Filter className="size-3.5" /> Lọc
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            {/* Display counts */}
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium pt-1">
              Hiển thị {filteredInstructors.length} / {instructors?.length || 0} giảng viên
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Main Instructors Table */}
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
            <Table>
              <TableHeader>
                <TableRow className="bg-slate-50/50">
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[100px]">MÃ GV</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">HỌ VÀ TÊN</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">BỘ MÔN</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">VAI TRÒ</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">HỌC VỊ</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[100px]">SỐ MÔN</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[150px]">TRẠNG THÁI</TableHead>
                  <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 text-right pr-6 w-[300px]">HÀNH ĐỘNG</TableHead>
                </TableRow>
              </TableHeader>
              <TableBody>
                {isLoading ? (
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12 text-slate-400">
                      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2">
                        <RefreshCw className="size-6 animate-spin text-primary" />
                        <span>Đang tải danh sách giảng viên...</span>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                ) : isError ? (
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12 text-rose-500 font-semibold">
                      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1">
                        <XCircle className="size-6" />
                        <span>Đã xảy ra lỗi khi tải dữ liệu.</span>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                ) : filteredInstructors.length === 0 ? (
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12 text-slate-400">
                      Không tìm thấy giảng viên nào khớp với bộ lọc.
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                ) : (
                  filteredInstructors.map((inst) => (
                    <TableRow key={inst.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/30">
                      {/* Mã GV */}
                      <TableCell className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                        {inst.staffCode}
                      </TableCell>
                      
                      {/* Họ và tên */}
                      <TableCell>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <div className={`size-9 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0 shadow-sm ${getAvatarColor(inst.id)}`}>
                            {getInitials(inst.fullName)}
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <span className="block font-semibold text-slate-900 leading-tight">
                              {inst.fullName}
                            </span>
                            <span className="block text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium mt-0.5">
                              {inst.email}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </TableCell>

                      {/* Bộ môn */}
                      <TableCell className="font-medium text-slate-700">
                        {inst.departmentNameVn || "Chưa xác định"}
                      </TableCell>

                      {/* Vai trò */}
                      <TableCell>
                        {inst.role === "DEAN" ? (
                          <Badge className="bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 hover:bg-amber-50">
                            {ROLE_LABELS.DEAN}
                          </Badge>
                        ) : inst.role === "DEPT_HEAD" ? (
                          <Badge className="bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 hover:bg-blue-50">
                            {ROLE_LABELS.DEPT_HEAD}
                          </Badge>
                        ) : inst.role === "PROGRAM_COORDINATOR" ? (
                          <Badge className="bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200 hover:bg-purple-50">
                            {ROLE_LABELS.PROGRAM_COORDINATOR}
                          </Badge>
                        ) : (
                          <Badge className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100">
                            {ROLE_LABELS.INSTRUCTOR}
                          </Badge>
                        )}
                      </TableCell>

                      {/* Học vị */}
                      <TableCell className="text-slate-600 font-medium">
                        {inst.degree}
                      </TableCell>

                      {/* Số môn */}
                      <TableCell className="font-semibold text-slate-700 pl-4">
                        {inst.courseCount || 0}
                      </TableCell>

                      {/* Trạng thái */}
                      <TableCell>
                        {inst.isActive ? (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-emerald-700 font-semibold">
                            <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 inline-block animate-pulse" />
                            <span>Hoạt động</span>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 font-semibold">
                            <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-slate-300 inline-block" />
                            <span>Ngừng hoạt động</span>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </TableCell>

                      {/* Hành động */}
                      <TableCell className="text-right pr-6">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="sm"
                            className="h-8 px-2 text-slate-600 hover:text-primary hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs"
                            onClick={() => handleOpenDialog("view", inst)}
                          >
                            <Eye className="size-3.5" />
                            Xem
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="sm"
                            className="h-8 px-2 text-slate-600 hover:text-primary hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs"
                            onClick={() => handleOpenDialog("assign", inst)}
                          >
                            <Calendar className="size-3.5" />
                            Phân công
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="sm"
                            className="h-8 px-2 text-slate-600 hover:text-primary hover:bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs"
                            onClick={() => handleOpenDialog("edit", inst)}
                          >
                            <Edit3 className="size-3.5" />
                            Sửa
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="sm"
                            className={`h-8 px-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs ${
                              inst.isActive
                                ? "text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50"
                                : "text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50"
                            }`}
                            onClick={() => handleToggleStatus(inst)}
                          >
                            <Power className="size-3.5" />
                            {inst.isActive ? "Vô hiệu" : "Kích hoạt"}
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))
                )}
              </TableBody>
            </Table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activeTab === "schedule" && (
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 font-heading">Bảng phân công giảng dạy học phần</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Xem danh sách phân công đứng lớp học phần cho giảng viên học kỳ này</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow className="bg-slate-50/50">
                <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Mã Lớp HP</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Học Phần</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Giảng Viên Phụ Trách</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Học Kỳ</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Năm Học</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Sĩ Số</TableHead>
                <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Lịch Học</TableHead>
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {filteredInstructors.slice(0, 8).map((inst, index) => {
                // Generate a realistic course assignment for demonstration
                const mockCourses = [
                  { code: "IT093IU", name: "Cơ sở dữ liệu", sem: 1, year: "2025-2026", max: 50, sched: "Thứ 2 (Tiết 1-3) - A1.203" },
                  { code: "CS102IU", name: "Cấu trúc dữ liệu và giải thuật", sem: 1, year: "2025-2026", max: 45, sched: "Thứ 3 (Tiết 4-6) - A2.405" },
                  { code: "DS201IU", name: "Nhập môn Khoa học dữ liệu", sem: 1, year: "2025-2026", max: 60, sched: "Thứ 4 (Tiết 7-9) - LA.302" },
                  { code: "CE302IU", name: "Kiến trúc máy tính", sem: 1, year: "2025-2026", max: 40, sched: "Thứ 5 (Tiết 1-3) - A1.104" }
                ]
                const course = mockCourses[index % mockCourses.length]
                const classCode = `${course.code}_Group${(index % 2) + 1}`

                return (
                  <TableRow key={inst.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/30">
                    <TableCell className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{classCode}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <div>
                        <span className="block font-semibold text-slate-900">{course.name}</span>
                        <span className="block text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">{course.code}</span>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <div className={`size-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[9px] shrink-0 ${getAvatarColor(inst.id)}`}>
                          {getInitials(inst.fullName)}
                        </div>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700 text-sm">{inst.fullName}</span>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="font-medium text-slate-600">Học kỳ {course.sem}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="font-medium text-slate-600">{course.year}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="font-semibold text-slate-700">{course.max} SV</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-slate-500 text-sm">{course.sched}</TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                )
              })}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* dialog Form for Create/Edit/View/Assign */}
      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg bg-white">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-primary font-heading flex items-center gap-2">
              {dialogMode === "create" ? (
                <>
                  <Users className="size-5 text-primary" />
                  <span>Thêm giảng viên mới</span>
                </>
              ) : dialogMode === "edit" ? (
                <>
                  <Edit3 className="size-5 text-primary" />
                  <span>Sửa thông tin giảng viên</span>
                </>
              ) : dialogMode === "view" ? (
                <>
                  <Eye className="size-5 text-primary" />
                  <span>Chi tiết hồ sơ giảng viên</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Calendar className="size-5 text-primary" />
                  <span>Phân công giảng dạy</span>
                </>
              )}
            </DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              {dialogMode === "create" && "Nhập mã và hồ sơ cá nhân để khởi tạo thông tin và tài khoản giảng viên mới."}
              {dialogMode === "edit" && "Cập nhật thông tin chi tiết hoặc phân quyền vai trò cho giảng viên."}
              {dialogMode === "view" && "Xem toàn bộ thông tin chi tiết và lịch sử giảng dạy của giảng viên."}
              {dialogMode === "assign" && `Phân công lớp học phần giảng dạy học kỳ này cho giảng viên ${fullName}`}
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>

          {dialogMode === "assign" ? (
            <div className="space-y-4 py-2 text-slate-700">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <Label className="font-semibold">Chọn học phần giảng dạy</Label>
                <Select defaultValue="1">
                  <SelectTrigger className="w-full bg-slate-50/50">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn học phần" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                    <SelectItem value="1">Cơ sở dữ liệu (IT093IU)</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="2">Cấu trúc dữ liệu và giải thuật (CS102IU)</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="3">Mạng máy tính (IT091IU)</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="4">Lập trình hướng đối tượng (IT095IU)</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="font-semibold">Nhóm lớp</Label>
                  <Input type="number" defaultValue={1} min={1} />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="font-semibold">Sĩ số tối đa</Label>
                  <Input type="number" defaultValue={50} min={10} />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1">
                <Label className="font-semibold">Lịch học chi tiết</Label>
                <Input placeholder="VD: Thứ 3 (Tiết 4-6) - Phòng A2.405" />
              </div>

              <DialogFooter className="pt-2">
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
                  Hủy
                </Button>
                <Button onClick={() => { alert("Phân công học phần thành công!"); setOpen(false) }} className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/95">
                  Xác nhận phân công
                </Button>
              </DialogFooter>
            </div>
          ) : dialogMode === "view" ? (
            <div className="space-y-4 py-2 text-slate-700">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                <div className={`size-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg ${selectedInstructor ? getAvatarColor(selectedInstructor.id) : ""}`}>
                  {selectedInstructor ? getInitials(selectedInstructor.fullName) : ""}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-lg leading-tight">{fullName}</h4>
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">{email}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-sm">
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">Mã giảng viên:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{staffCode}</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">Bộ môn công tác:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
                    {selectedInstructor?.departmentNameVn}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">Học vị / Học hàm:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{degree} / {academicRank}</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">Vai trò hệ thống:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
                    {ROLE_LABELS[role]}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">Môn học phụ trách:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{selectedInstructor?.courseCount || 0} môn</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">Tài khoản đăng nhập:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
                    {email.split("@")[0]} / admin123
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <DialogFooter className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" className="w-full" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
                  Đóng
                </Button>
              </DialogFooter>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4 pt-2">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  <Label htmlFor="inst-code">Mã giảng viên (Staff Code) *</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="inst-code"
                    placeholder="VD: GV014"
                    value={staffCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setStaffCode(e.target.value)}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  <Label htmlFor="inst-name">Họ và tên *</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="inst-name"
                    placeholder="VD: Nguyễn Văn B"
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="inst-email">Email công tác *</Label>
                <Input
                  id="inst-email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="VD: nvb@hcmiu.edu.vn"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  <Label htmlFor="inst-degree">Học vị</Label>
                  <Select value={degree} onValueChange={setDegree}>
                    <SelectTrigger id="inst-degree" className="bg-slate-50/50">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn học vị" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                      <SelectItem value="PhD">PhD (Tiến sĩ)</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="MSc">MSc (Thạc sĩ)</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="BSc">BSc (Cử nhân)</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  <Label htmlFor="inst-rank">Học hàm / Chức danh</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="inst-rank"
                    placeholder="VD: GS, PGS, Giảng viên..."
                    value={academicRank}
                    onChange={(e) => setAcademicRank(e.target.value)}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  <Label htmlFor="inst-dept">Bộ môn công tác *</Label>
                  <Select value={departmentId} onValueChange={setDepartmentId}>
                    <SelectTrigger id="inst-dept" className="bg-slate-50/50">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn bộ môn" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                      {departments?.map((d) => (
                        <SelectItem key={d.id} value={d.id.toString()}>
                          {d.nameVn}
                        </SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  <Label htmlFor="inst-role">Vai trò hệ thống *</Label>
                  <Select value={role} onValueChange={(val) => setRole(val as UserRole)}>
                    <SelectTrigger id="inst-role" className="bg-slate-50/50">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn vai trò" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent className="bg-white">
                      <SelectItem value="DEAN">Trưởng khoa</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="DEPT_HEAD">Trưởng bộ môn</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="PROGRAM_COORDINATOR">Điều phối viên</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="INSTRUCTOR">Giảng viên</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  id="inst-active"
                  checked={isActive}
                  onChange={(e) => setIsActive(e.target.checked)}
                  className="rounded border-slate-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary h-4 w-4"
                />
                <Label htmlFor="inst-active" className="cursor-pointer font-semibold text-slate-700 text-sm">
                  Kích hoạt trạng thái hoạt động và tài khoản đăng nhập
                </Label>
              </div>

              <DialogFooter className="pt-2">
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
                  Hủy
                </Button>
                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/95"
                  disabled={createMutation.isPending || updateMutation.isPending}
                >
                  {createMutation.isPending || updateMutation.isPending ? "Đang lưu..." : dialogMode === "create" ? "Thêm mới" : "Lưu thay đổi"}
                </Button>
              </DialogFooter>
            </form>
          )}
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/admin/ProgramManagementPage.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Plus } from "lucide-react"

import { departmentApi } from "@/api/departmentApi"
import { programApi as progApi } from "@/api/programApi"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog"

export default function ProgramManagementPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)

  // Form Fields
  const [code, setCode] = useState("")
  const [name, setName] = useState("")
  const [nameVn, setNameVn] = useState("")
  const [majorId, setMajorId] = useState("")
  const [programTypeId, setProgramTypeId] = useState("")
  const [departmentId, setDepartmentId] = useState("")
  const [accreditationBody, setAccreditationBody] = useState("")
  const [totalCredits, setTotalCredits] = useState("135")
  const [durationYears, setDurationYears] = useState("4")
  const [validFrom, setValidFrom] = useState("2021-09-01")

  // Query programs
  const { data: programs, isLoading, isError } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["programs"],
    queryFn: progApi.getAll,
  })

  // Dropdown Queries
  const { data: departments } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["departments"], queryFn: departmentApi.getAll })
  const { data: majors } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["majors"], queryFn: progApi.getMajors })
  const { data: programTypes } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["program-types"], queryFn: progApi.getProgramTypes })

  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: progApi.create,
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["programs"] })
      setOpen(false)
      // Reset
      setCode("")
      setName("")
      setNameVn("")
      setMajorId("")
      setProgramTypeId("")
      setDepartmentId("")
      setAccreditationBody("")
      setTotalCredits("135")
      setDurationYears("4")
      setValidFrom("2021-09-01")
    },
    onError: (err: any) => {
      const msg = err?.response?.data?.message || "Đã xảy ra lỗi khi tạo chương trình"
      alert(msg)
    }
  })

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (
      !code ||
      !name ||
      !nameVn ||
      !majorId ||
      !programTypeId ||
      !departmentId ||
      !totalCredits ||
      !durationYears ||
      !validFrom
    ) {
      alert("Vui lòng điền đầy đủ các thông tin bắt buộc")
      return
    }

    progApi.create({
      code,
      name,
      nameVn,
      majorId: Number(majorId),
      programTypeId: Number(programTypeId),
      departmentId: Number(departmentId),
      accreditationBody: accreditationBody || "ASIIN",
      totalCredits: Number(totalCredits),
      durationYears: Number(durationYears),
      validFrom,
    })
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary font-heading">
            Quản lý Chương trình đào tạo (CTĐT)
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
            Xem và cấu trúc khung chương trình đào tạo theo ngành học và chuẩn kiểm định
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90 flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
          <Plus className="size-4" />
          Thêm chương trình đào tạo
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* Main Table */}
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-white overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow className="bg-slate-50/50">
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[120px]">Mã CTĐT</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Tên chương trình</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Ngành học</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[100px]">Bộ môn</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[100px]">Kiểm định</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[100px] text-center">Tín chỉ</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[100px] text-center">Thời gian</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="font-semibold text-slate-700 w-[120px]">Trạng thái</TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {isLoading ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-8 text-slate-400">
                  Đang tải...
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : isError ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-8 text-rose-500">
                  Lỗi khi tải dữ liệu chương trình đào tạo.
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : !programs || programs.length === 0 ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={8} className="text-center py-8 text-slate-400">
                  Chưa có chương trình nào trong hệ thống.
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : (
              programs.map((prog) => (
                <TableRow key={prog.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/30">
                  <TableCell className="font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    {prog.code}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <div className="flex flex-col">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{prog.nameVn}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{prog.name}</span>
                    </div>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-slate-600 font-medium text-sm">
                    {prog.majorCode}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-slate-600 text-sm">
                    {prog.departmentCode}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    {prog.accreditationBody ? (
                      <Badge variant="outline" className="text-indigo-600 border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50/30">
                        {prog.accreditationBody}
                      </Badge>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-slate-400 text-xs">Chưa rõ</span>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-center font-medium">{prog.totalCredits} TC</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-center text-slate-600">{prog.durationYears} năm</TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    {prog.isActive ? (
                      <Badge className="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
                        Đang áp dụng
                      </Badge>
                    ) : (
                      <Badge className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border border-slate-200">
                        Lưu trữ
                      </Badge>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))
            )}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </div>

      {/* Dialog creation */}
      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-xl bg-white max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-primary font-heading">Thêm Chương trình đào tạo mới</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              Tạo khung chương trình đào tạo mới (Cử nhân/Kỹ sư) cho các ngành học của bộ môn.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4 pt-2">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="prog-code">Mã chương trình</Label>
                <Input
                  id="prog-code"
                  placeholder="VD: CS-2021, IT-2021..."
                  value={code}
                  onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="prog-year">Ngày hiệu lực</Label>
                <Input
                  id="prog-year"
                  type="date"
                  value={validFrom}
                  onChange={(e) => setValidFrom(e.target.value)}
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="prog-name-vn">Tên chương trình đào tạo (Tiếng Việt)</Label>
              <Input
                id="prog-name-vn"
                placeholder="VD: Cử nhân Khoa học Máy tính..."
                value={nameVn}
                onChange={(e) => setNameVn(e.target.value)}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="prog-name-en">Tên tiếng Anh (English Name)</Label>
              <Input
                id="prog-name-en"
                placeholder="VD: Bachelor of Science in Computer Science..."
                value={name}
                onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label>Ngành học</Label>
                <Select value={majorId} onValueChange={setMajorId}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="bg-white border-slate-200">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn ngành..." />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {majors?.map((m) => (
                      <SelectItem key={m.id} value={String(m.id)}>
                        {m.code} - {m.nameVn}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label>Hệ đào tạo</Label>
                <Select value={programTypeId} onValueChange={setProgramTypeId}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="bg-white border-slate-200">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn hệ..." />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {programTypes?.map((pt) => (
                      <SelectItem key={pt.id} value={String(pt.id)}>
                        {pt.code} - {pt.name}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label>Khoa phụ trách</Label>
                <Select value={departmentId} onValueChange={setDepartmentId}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="bg-white border-slate-200">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn khoa..." />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {departments?.map((d) => (
                      <SelectItem key={d.id} value={String(d.id)}>
                        {d.code} - {d.nameVn}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="prog-credits">Tổng số tín chỉ</Label>
                <Input
                  id="prog-credits"
                  type="number"
                  value={totalCredits}
                  onChange={(e) => setTotalCredits(e.target.value)}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="prog-duration">Thời gian (năm)</Label>
                <Input
                  id="prog-duration"
                  type="number"
                  value={durationYears}
                  onChange={(e) => setDurationYears(e.target.value)}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="prog-accreditation">Chuẩn kiểm định</Label>
                <Input
                  id="prog-accreditation"
                  placeholder="VD: ASIIN, ABET..."
                  value={accreditationBody}
                  onChange={(e) => setAccreditationBody(e.target.value)}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <DialogFooter className="pt-2">
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
                Hủy
              </Button>
              <Button type="submit" className="bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary/90" disabled={createMutation.isPending}>
                {createMutation.isPending ? "Đang lưu..." : "Thêm mới"}
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </form>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/admin/ReportManagementPage.tsx">
import { useState } from "react";
import { Card, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Download, FileText, Table } from "lucide-react";
import { reportApi } from "@/api/reportApi";

export default function ReportManagementPage() {
  const [programId, setProgramId] = useState("1");
  const [semester, setSemester] = useState("HK1 2024-2025");
  const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false);

  const handleExport = async (type: "CLO_PLO" | "SYLLABUS", format: "EXCEL" | "PDF") => {
    try {
      setIsExporting(true);
      let response;
      let filename = "";

      if (type === "CLO_PLO") {
        if (format === "EXCEL") {
          response = await reportApi.exportCloPloMatrixExcel(Number(programId));
          filename = `CLO_PLO_Matrix_Program_${programId}.xlsx`;
        } else {
          response = await reportApi.exportCloPloMatrixPdf(Number(programId));
          filename = `CLO_PLO_Matrix_Program_${programId}.pdf`;
        }
      } else if (type === "SYLLABUS") {
        if (format === "EXCEL") {
          response = await reportApi.exportSyllabusListExcel(semester);
          filename = `Syllabus_List_${semester}.xlsx`;
        } else {
          response = await reportApi.exportSyllabusListPdf(semester);
          filename = `Syllabus_List_${semester}.pdf`;
        }
      }

      if (response && response.data) {
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([response.data]));
        const link = document.createElement("a");
        link.href = url;
        link.setAttribute("download", filename);
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        link.parentNode?.removeChild(link);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Export failed", error);
      alert("Đã có lỗi xảy ra khi xuất báo cáo.");
    } finally {
      setIsExporting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary font-heading">
          Xuất Báo Cáo & Dữ Liệu
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
          Tạo và tải xuống các báo cáo kiểm định chất lượng đào tạo (Excel / PDF).
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* CLO-PLO Matrix Report */}
        <Card className="border border-slate-100 shadow-sm">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
              <Table className="size-5 text-indigo-600" />
              Ma trận CLO-PLO
            </CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Xuất báo cáo mức độ bao phủ của môn học với chuẩn đầu ra chương trình (PLO).</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label>Chương trình đào tạo</Label>
              <Select value={programId} onValueChange={setProgramId}>
                <SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn chương trình" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="1">Khoa học Máy tính (CS-2021)</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="2">Công nghệ Thông tin (IT-2021)</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="3">Khoa học Dữ liệu (DS-2022)</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
              <Button 
                variant="outline" 
                className="flex-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100 hover:text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200"
                onClick={() => handleExport("CLO_PLO", "EXCEL")}
                disabled={isExporting}
              >
                <Download className="mr-2 size-4" />
                Excel (.xlsx)
              </Button>
              <Button 
                variant="outline" 
                className="flex-1 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100 hover:text-rose-800 border-rose-200"
                onClick={() => handleExport("CLO_PLO", "PDF")}
                disabled={isExporting}
              >
                <FileText className="mr-2 size-4" />
                PDF (.pdf)
              </Button>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {/* Syllabus List Report */}
        <Card className="border border-slate-100 shadow-sm">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
              <FileText className="size-5 text-amber-600" />
              Danh sách Syllabus theo Học kỳ
            </CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Xuất thống kê tình trạng phê duyệt Syllabus của tất cả môn học.</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label>Học kỳ</Label>
              <Input 
                value={semester} 
                onChange={(e) => setSemester(e.target.value)} 
                placeholder="VD: HK1 2024-2025" 
              />
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
              <Button 
                variant="outline" 
                className="flex-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100 hover:text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200"
                onClick={() => handleExport("SYLLABUS", "EXCEL")}
                disabled={isExporting}
              >
                <Download className="mr-2 size-4" />
                Excel (.xlsx)
              </Button>
              <Button 
                variant="outline" 
                className="flex-1 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100 hover:text-rose-800 border-rose-200"
                onClick={() => handleExport("SYLLABUS", "PDF")}
                disabled={isExporting}
              >
                <FileText className="mr-2 size-4" />
                PDF (.pdf)
              </Button>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
