<file path="src/pages/admin/SystemSettingsPage.tsx">
import { useState, useEffect } from "react"
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
import { api } from "@/api/axios"

export default function SystemSettingsPage() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [deadline, setDeadline] = useState("")

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchConfigs()
  }, [])

  const fetchConfigs = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get("/api/configs")
      const dlConfig = res.data.find((c: any) => c.key === "SYLLABUS_DEADLINE")
      if (dlConfig) {
        setDeadline(dlConfig.value)
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error fetching configs:", error)
    }
  }

  async function onSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!deadline) {
      alert("Vui lòng nhập deadline hợp lệ!")
      return
    }
    
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      await api.post("/api/configs", {
        key: "SYLLABUS_DEADLINE",
        value: deadline,
        description: "Deadline nộp Syllabus (YYYY-MM-DD)",
      })
      alert("Cập nhật cấu hình thành công!")
      fetchConfigs()
    } catch (error) {
      alert("Lỗi cập nhật cấu hình!")
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Cấu hình hệ thống</h1>
      
      <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Deadline nộp Syllabus</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Thiết lập hạn chót cho giảng viên nộp đề cương</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="deadline">Ngày (YYYY-MM-DD)</Label>
                <Input 
                  id="deadline"
                  type="date" 
                  value={deadline}
                  onChange={(e) => setDeadline(e.target.value)}
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <Button type="submit" disabled={loading}>
                {loading ? "Đang lưu..." : "Lưu thay đổi"}
              </Button>
            </form>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/admin/UserManagementPage.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { MoreHorizontal, Plus } from "lucide-react"

import { getUsers, toggleUserActive } from "@/api/userApi"
import type { UserAccountResponse } from "@/types/user"
import { ROLE_LABEL } from "@/config/navConfig"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table"
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu"

import UserFormDialog from "@/components/UserFormDialog"
import DeleteUserDialog from "@/components/DeleteUserDialog"

export default function UserManagementPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()

  const { data: users, isLoading, isError } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["users"],
    queryFn: getUsers,
  })

  const toggleMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: toggleUserActive,
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["users"] }),
  })

  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false)
  const [editingUser, setEditingUser] = useState<UserAccountResponse | null>(
    null
  )

  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = useState(false)
  const [deletingUser, setDeletingUser] =
    useState<UserAccountResponse | null>(null)

  function openCreate() {
    setEditingUser(null)
    setFormOpen(true)
  }

  function openEdit(user: UserAccountResponse) {
    setEditingUser(user)
    setFormOpen(true)
  }

  function openDelete(user: UserAccountResponse) {
    setDeletingUser(user)
    setDeleteOpen(true)
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-700">
            Quản lý người dùng
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Tạo, chỉnh sửa, kích hoạt/khoá tài khoản trong hệ thống
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button className="bg-brand-700 hover:bg-brand-800" onClick={openCreate}>
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
          Thêm người dùng
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-lg border bg-white">
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow>
              <TableHead>Username</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Email</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Vai trò</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Trạng thái</TableHead>
              <TableHead>Đăng nhập gần nhất</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="text-right">Thao tác</TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {isLoading && (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={6} className="text-center py-6 text-muted-foreground">
                  Đang tải...
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            )}

            {isError && (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={6} className="text-center py-6 text-destructive">
                  Không thể tải danh sách người dùng.
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            )}

            {!isLoading && users?.length === 0 && (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={6} className="text-center py-6 text-muted-foreground">
                  Chưa có người dùng nào.
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            )}

            {users?.map((user) => (
              <TableRow key={user.id}>
                <TableCell className="font-medium">{user.username}</TableCell>
                <TableCell>{user.email}</TableCell>
                <TableCell>
                  <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-brand-50 text-brand-700">
                    {ROLE_LABEL[user.role]}
                  </Badge>
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell>
                  {user.isActive ? (
                    <Badge className="bg-status-approved-bg text-status-approved-text border border-status-approved-border">
                      Đang hoạt động
                    </Badge>
                  ) : (
                    <Badge className="bg-status-rejected-bg text-status-rejected-text border border-status-rejected-border">
                      Đã khoá
                    </Badge>
                  )}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  {user.lastLogin
                    ? new Date(user.lastLogin).toLocaleString("vi-VN")
                    : "Chưa đăng nhập"}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell className="text-right">
                  <DropdownMenu>
                    <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                      <Button variant="ghost" size="icon">
                        <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </Button>
                    </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                    <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
                      <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => openEdit(user)}>
                        Chỉnh sửa
                      </DropdownMenuItem>
                      <DropdownMenuItem
                        onClick={() => toggleMutation.mutate(user.id)}
                      >
                        {user.isActive ? "Khoá tài khoản" : "Kích hoạt lại"}
                      </DropdownMenuItem>
                      <DropdownMenuItem
                        className="text-destructive"
                        onClick={() => openDelete(user)}
                      >
                        Xoá
                      </DropdownMenuItem>
                    </DropdownMenuContent>
                  </DropdownMenu>
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ))}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </div>

      <UserFormDialog
        open={formOpen}
        onOpenChange={setFormOpen}
        editingUser={editingUser}
      />
      <DeleteUserDialog
        open={deleteOpen}
        onOpenChange={setDeleteOpen}
        user={deletingUser}
      />
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/program/CurriculumMapPage.tsx">
import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom"; // Import useSearchParams
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { syllabusApi } from "@/api/syllabusApi";
import type { Syllabus } from "@/types/syllabus";
import { CourseNode } from "@/components/curriculum/CourseNode";
import Xarrow, { Xwrapper } from "react-xarrows";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import SyllabusToolbar from "@/components/syllabus/SyllabusToolbar"; // Import Toolbar đồng bộ

export default function CurriculumMapPage() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  
  // Đọc params từ URL (đồng bộ với Toolbar)
  const searchVal = searchParams.get("q") || "";
  const selectedMajor = searchParams.get("major") || "all";
  const selectedCohort = searchParams.get("year") || "all";
  const selectedSemester = searchParams.get("semester") || "all";

  // Data fetching
  const { data: syllabuses, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["syllabuses-all"],
    queryFn: syllabusApi.getAll,
  });

  // Extract unique cohorts for dropdown
  const cohorts = useMemo(() => {
    const baseCohorts = ["K20", "K21", "K22", "K23", "K24", "K25"];
    if (!syllabuses) return baseCohorts;
    const all = new Set(baseCohorts);
    syllabuses.forEach((s) => {
      if (s.academicYear) all.add(s.academicYear.trim());
    });
    return Array.from(all).sort();
  }, [syllabuses]);

  // Filter syllabuses based on URL Params
  const filteredSyllabuses = useMemo(() => {
    if (!syllabuses) return [];
    return syllabuses.filter((s) => {
      // Lọc theo Major (Sử dụng metadata để map)
      const majorMatch = selectedMajor === "all" || (s.major === selectedMajor);
      
      // Lọc theo Khóa
      const matchCohort = selectedCohort === "all" || (s.academicYear || "").trim() === selectedCohort;
      
      // Lọc theo Học kỳ
      const matchSemester = selectedSemester === "all" || String(s.semester) === selectedSemester;
      
      // Lọc theo Tìm kiếm
      const searchLower = searchVal.toLowerCase();
      const matchSearch =
        !searchVal ||
        s.courseCode?.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower) ||
        s.courseName?.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);

      return majorMatch && matchCohort && matchSemester && matchSearch;
    });
  }, [syllabuses, selectedMajor, selectedCohort, selectedSemester, searchVal]);

  // Organize courses by semester (1 to 8)
  const semesters = useMemo(() => {
    const s: Record<number, Syllabus[]> = {};
    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) s[i] = [];
    filteredSyllabuses.forEach((cp) => {
      const sem = parseInt(cp.semester) || 1;
      if (!s[sem]) s[sem] = [];
      s[sem].push(cp);
    });
    return s;
  }, [filteredSyllabuses]);

  // Total credits calculation
  const totalCredits = useMemo(() => {
    const totals: Record<number, { compulsory: number; elective: number; total: number }> = {};
    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) totals[i] = { compulsory: 0, elective: 0, total: 0 };
    
    filteredSyllabuses.forEach((cp) => {
      const sem = parseInt(cp.semester) || 1;
      // Giả định dữ liệu credit trả về từ syllabus hoặc course tương ứng
      const credits = 3; // Placeholder nếu không có data credit cụ thể
      const isElective = cp.courseTypes?.toLowerCase().includes("elective");
      
      if (totals[sem]) {
        totals[sem].total += credits;
        if (isElective) totals[sem].elective += credits;
        else totals[sem].compulsory += credits;
      }
    });
    return totals;
  }, [filteredSyllabuses]);

  const arrows = useMemo(() => {
    if (!filteredSyllabuses) return [];
    const renderedCourses = filteredSyllabuses.filter(s => s.courseCode);
    const validArrows: Array<{ start: string; end: string; dashness: boolean; color: string }> = [];
    
    renderedCourses.forEach((dep) => {
      const depCode = dep.courseCode;
      const preStr = dep.prerequisites?.toLowerCase() || "";
      renderedCourses.forEach((pre) => {
        if (pre.courseCode === depCode) return;
        if (preStr.includes(pre.courseCode.toLowerCase())) {
          validArrows.push({ start: `node-${pre.courseCode}`, end: `node-${depCode}`, dashness: false, color: "#1e293b" });
        }
      });
    });
    return validArrows;
  }, [filteredSyllabuses]);

  return (
    <div className="p-6 h-full flex flex-col bg-[#FDFDF9]">
      {/* Header Area */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
        <div>
          <div className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">SCSE / BẢN ĐỒ ĐÀO TẠO</div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-[#1e293b]">Curriculum Mapping</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Sơ đồ phân bổ học phần theo học kỳ, tự động sinh từ dữ liệu đề cương.</p>
        </div>
        <Button className="bg-[#1e293b] hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-md flex items-center gap-2">
          <Plus className="size-4" /> Soạn đề cương mới
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* Thay thế bộ lọc cũ bằng Toolbar đồng bộ */}
      <SyllabusToolbar />

      {/* Main Card */}
      <Card className="flex-1 flex flex-col bg-white border-slate-200 shadow-sm rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-slate-100 flex justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#1e293b]">Sơ đồ Phân bổ Học phần</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-3">
             <Button variant="outline" className="text-xs">XUẤT PDF</Button>
             <Button className="bg-[#0f172a] text-white text-xs">GENERATE MAPPING</Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center py-20">Đang tải map...</div>
        ) : filteredSyllabuses.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center py-20 text-slate-400">Không có dữ liệu khớp với bộ lọc.</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-x-auto p-6 min-h-[500px]">
            <Xwrapper>
              <div className="flex gap-16 min-w-max pb-32">
                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map((sem) => (
                  <div key={sem} className="flex-1 min-w-[200px] flex flex-col gap-6">
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-center border-b border-slate-200 pb-3 uppercase text-sm">Semester {sem}</h3>
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 items-center">
                      {semesters[sem]?.map((s) => (
                        <CourseNode
                          key={s.id}
                          id={`node-${s.courseCode}`}
                          courseName={s.courseName}
                          credits={3} // Giá trị giả định
                          courseTypes={s.courseTypes ? JSON.parse(s.courseTypes) : []}
                        />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              {arrows.map((arr, idx) => (
                <Xarrow key={idx} start={arr.start} end={arr.end} color={arr.color} strokeWidth={1.5} path="grid" dashness={arr.dashness} headSize={4} curveness={0.3} />
              ))}
            </Xwrapper>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/program/ProgramDiffPage.tsx">
import { useParams, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { useProgramDiff } from "@/hooks/useProgramDiff";

export default function ProgramDiffPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const oldCohortId = searchParams.get("oldCohortId");
  const newCohortId = searchParams.get("newCohortId");

  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useProgramDiff(
    Number(id),
    Number(oldCohortId),
    Number(newCohortId)
  );

  if (!oldCohortId || !newCohortId) {
    return <div className="p-6">Vui lòng chọn 2 khóa để so sánh.</div>;
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="p-6">Đang tải dữ liệu so sánh...</div>;
  }

  if (isError || !data) {
    return <div className="p-6">Lỗi tải dữ liệu so sánh.</div>;
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">So sánh Chương trình đào tạo (CTĐT)</h1>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <p><strong>Mã CTĐT:</strong> {data.programCode}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-4 mb-6">
        <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 border rounded-lg">
          <span className="font-semibold block mb-1">Khóa cũ:</span>
          {data.oldCohortYear}
        </div>
        <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 border rounded-lg">
          <span className="font-semibold block mb-1">Khóa mới:</span>
          {data.newCohortYear}
        </div>
      </div>

      {data.courseDiff && (
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2">Thay đổi Môn học (Courses)</h2>
          
          {data.courseDiff.added?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-6 bg-green-50/50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-green-200">
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-green-700 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-sm">New</span>
                Thêm mới môn học
              </h3>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {data.courseDiff.added.map((c: { courseId: number; courseName: string; courseCode: string }) => (
                  <div key={c.courseId} className="bg-white p-3 rounded shadow-sm border border-green-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="font-medium text-slate-800">{c.courseName}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-green-600 bg-green-50 px-2 py-1 rounded">{c.courseCode}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {data.courseDiff.removed?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-6 bg-red-50/50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-red-200">
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-red-700 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="bg-red-100 text-red-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-sm">Removed</span>
                Đã loại bỏ môn học
              </h3>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {data.courseDiff.removed.map((c: { courseId: number; courseName: string; courseCode: string }) => (
                  <div key={c.courseId} className="bg-white p-3 rounded shadow-sm border border-red-100 flex items-center justify-between opacity-80">
                    <span className="font-medium line-through text-slate-600">{c.courseName}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-red-600 bg-red-50 px-2 py-1 rounded">{c.courseCode}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {data.courseDiff.modified?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-6 bg-blue-50/50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-blue-200">
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-blue-700 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-sm">Modified</span>
                Cập nhật môn học
              </h3>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                {data.courseDiff.modified.map((c: { courseId: number; courseName: string; courseCode: string; changes: Record<string, { oldValue: unknown; newValue: unknown }> }) => (
                  <div key={c.courseId} className="bg-white p-4 rounded shadow-sm border border-blue-100">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 border-b pb-2">
                      <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">{c.courseName}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1 rounded">{c.courseCode}</span>
                    </div>
                    <ul className="space-y-2 text-sm">
                      {Object.entries(c.changes).map(([key, diff]) => (
                        <li key={key} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="font-semibold capitalize text-slate-600 w-32">{key}:</span>
                          <span className="line-through text-red-500 bg-red-50 px-1.5 rounded">{diff.oldValue}</span> 
                          <span className="text-slate-400">➔</span> 
                          <span className="text-green-600 font-medium bg-green-50 px-1.5 rounded">{diff.newValue}</span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

    </Card>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/program/SyllabusCurriculumMapPage.tsx">
import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Search } from "lucide-react";





import { CourseDetailsModal } from "@/components/curriculum/CourseDetailsModal";

type CurriculumCourse = {
  courseId: number;
  courseCode: string;
  courseName: string;
  courseNameVn?: string | null;
  creditTheory?: number | null;
  creditLab?: number | null;
  semester: string;
  major?: string | null;
  courseTypes?: string | null;
  syllabusVersion?: string | null;
};

type CurriculumSemester = {
  semester: string;
  courses: CurriculumCourse[];
};

type CurriculumRelation = {
  fromCourseId: number;
  fromCourseCode: string;
  fromCourseName: string;
  toCourseId: number;
  toCourseCode: string;
  toCourseName: string;
  relationType: string;
};

type CurriculumMapResponse = {
  academicYear: string | null;
  major: string | null;
  semesters: CurriculumSemester[];
  relations: CurriculumRelation[];
};

type ArrowLine = {
  x1: number;
  y1: number;
  x2: number;
  y2: number;
  relationType: string;
};
// Helper component for the new Card design in the map
const MapCourseCard = ({
  course,
  onClick,
}: {
  course: CurriculumCourse;
  onClick: () => void;
}) => {
  let borderColor = "border-slate-300";
  let bgColor = "bg-white";

  if (course.courseCode.startsWith("IT") && !course.courseCode.includes("072")) {
    borderColor = "border-emerald-400";
    bgColor = "bg-emerald-50";
  } else if (course.courseCode.startsWith("CS")) {
    borderColor = "border-blue-400";
    bgColor = "bg-blue-50";
  } else if (course.courseCode === "IT072IU") {
    borderColor = "border-amber-400";
    bgColor = "bg-amber-50";
  }

  return (
    <div
      id={`course-${course.courseId}`}
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`p-3 rounded-md border-2 ${borderColor} ${bgColor} cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition-shadow relative z-20`}
    >
      <div className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-700">{course.courseCode}</div>
      <div className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900 leading-tight mt-1 mb-2">{course.courseName}</div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
        {course.creditTheory ?? 0},{course.creditLab ?? 0} TC
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default function SyllabusCurriculumMapPage() {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const initialAcademicYear = searchParams.get("academicYear") || "all";
  const initialMajor = searchParams.get("major") || "all";

  const [activeCohort, setActiveCohort] = useState(initialAcademicYear);
  const [searchVal, setSearchVal] = useState("");
  const [majorVal, setMajorVal] = useState(initialMajor);

  const [detailsCourseId, setDetailsCourseId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const mapAreaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [arrowLines, setArrowLines] = useState<ArrowLine[]>([]);
  const updateMapFilter = (key: string, value: string) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);

    if (value && value !== "all") {
      params.set(key, value);
    } else {
      params.delete(key);
    }

    setSearchParams(params);
  };

  const { data: curriculumMap, isLoading: isMapLoading } = useQuery<CurriculumMapResponse>({
    queryKey: ["syllabus-curriculum-map", activeCohort, majorVal],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();

      if (activeCohort !== "all") {
        params.set("academicYear", activeCohort);
      }

      if (majorVal !== "all") {
        params.set("major", majorVal);
      }

      const query = params.toString();
      const url = `http://localhost:8080/api/curriculum-map${query ? `?${query}` : ""}`;

      const res = await fetch(url);

      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error("Không thể tải bản đồ đào tạo");
      }

      return res.json();
    },
  });

  const cohorts = ["K20", "K21", "K22", "K23", "K24", "K25"];

  // Organize courses by semester
  const semesters = useMemo(() => {
    const s: Record<number, CurriculumCourse[]> = {};

    for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) {
      s[i] = [];
    }

    const query = searchVal.toLowerCase().trim();

    curriculumMap?.semesters?.forEach((group) => {
      const match = group.semester.match(/\d+/);
      const semNumber = match ? Number(match[0]) : 0;

      if (semNumber >= 1 && semNumber <= 8) {
        const filteredCourses = group.courses.filter((course) => {
          if (!query) return true;

          const codeMatch = course.courseCode?.toLowerCase().includes(query);
          const nameMatch = course.courseName?.toLowerCase().includes(query);
          const nameVnMatch = course.courseNameVn?.toLowerCase().includes(query);

          return codeMatch || nameMatch || nameVnMatch;
        });

        s[semNumber] = filteredCourses;
      }
    });

    return s;
  }, [curriculumMap, searchVal]);

  // Determine arrows
  useEffect(() => {
    const calculateArrows = () => {
      if (!curriculumMap?.relations || !mapAreaRef.current) {
        setArrowLines([]);
        return;
      }

      const containerRect = mapAreaRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();

      const lines: ArrowLine[] = [];

      curriculumMap.relations.forEach((rel) => {
        if (rel.relationType !== "PREREQUISITE") return;
        const fromEl = document.getElementById(`course-${rel.fromCourseId}`);
        const toEl = document.getElementById(`course-${rel.toCourseId}`);

        if (!fromEl || !toEl) return;

        const fromRect = fromEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        const toRect = toEl.getBoundingClientRect();

        lines.push({
          x1: fromRect.right - containerRect.left,
          y1: fromRect.top + fromRect.height / 2 - containerRect.top,
          x2: toRect.left - containerRect.left,
          y2: toRect.top + toRect.height / 2 - containerRect.top,
          relationType: rel.relationType,
        });
      });

      setArrowLines(lines);
    };

    const timer = window.setTimeout(calculateArrows, 300);

    window.addEventListener("resize", calculateArrows);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", calculateArrows);
    };
  }, [curriculumMap, semesters, searchVal]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-brand-600 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
            Sơ đồ đào tạo
          </p>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            Curriculum Mapping
          </h1>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters Bar */}
      {/* Filters Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/60 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-600 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
          <Search className="size-4" /> BỘ LỌC
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-4 items-center">
          <div className="relative w-full lg:flex-1">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 size-4 text-slate-400" />

            <Input
              className="pl-9 bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200/80 h-10 rounded-lg"
              placeholder="Tìm theo mã môn hoặc tên môn..."
              value={searchVal}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchVal(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex w-full lg:w-auto items-center gap-3">
            <Select
              value={activeCohort}
              onValueChange={(value) => {
                setActiveCohort(value);
                updateMapFilter("academicYear", value);
              }}
            >
              <SelectTrigger className="w-full lg:w-[160px] h-10 rounded-lg">
                <SelectValue placeholder="Khóa" />
              </SelectTrigger>

              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="all">Khóa: Tất cả</SelectItem>
                {cohorts.map((cohort) => (
                  <SelectItem key={cohort} value={cohort}>
                    {cohort}
                  </SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>

            <Select
              value={majorVal}
              onValueChange={(value) => {
                setMajorVal(value);
                updateMapFilter("major", value);
              }}
            >
              <SelectTrigger className="w-full lg:w-[220px] h-10 rounded-lg">
                <SelectValue placeholder="Chuyên ngành" />
              </SelectTrigger>

              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="all">Tất cả chuyên ngành</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="Khoa học Máy tính">Khoa học Máy tính</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="Kỹ thuật phần mềm">Kỹ thuật phần mềm</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="Khoa học Dữ liệu">Khoa học Dữ liệu</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-end text-xs text-slate-500 gap-6 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
        <span>
          {Object.values(semesters).reduce(
            (total, courses) => total + courses.length,
            0
          )}{" "}
          KẾT QUẢ • {activeCohort === "all" ? "TẤT CẢ KHÓA" : activeCohort}
        </span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><div className="size-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500"></div> DUYỆT</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><div className="size-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500"></div> XEM XÉT</span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><div className="size-2 rounded-full bg-slate-300"></div> NHÁP</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Map Content */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-8">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
              KHÓA {activeCohort === "all" ? "TẤT CẢ" : activeCohort}
              {majorVal !== "all" ? ` • ${majorVal}` : ""} • TỰ SINH TỪ DỮ LIỆU ĐỀ CƯƠNG
            </p>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Sơ đồ Phân bổ Học phần theo Học kỳ</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm max-w-2xl">
              Hệ thống dựng sơ đồ từ trường semester và prereqs của từng đề cương đã chỉnh sửa. Mọi thay đổi trong form sẽ phản ánh ở đây khi bấm Generate.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <Button variant="outline" className="border-slate-300 text-slate-700 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">
              XUẤT PDF
            </Button>
            <Button className="bg-slate-900 text-white hover:bg-slate-800 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">
              GENERATE MAPPING
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
          <div className="flex gap-4">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><div className="size-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500"></div> CS</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><div className="size-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500"></div> DS</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><div className="size-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500"></div> IT</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><div className="size-2 rounded-full bg-slate-300"></div> ĐẠI CƯƠNG</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-block w-8 border-t-2 border-slate-300 border-dashed"></span>
            RÀNG BUỘC TIÊN QUYẾT
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Map Grid */}
        {isMapLoading ? (
          <div className="py-20 text-center text-slate-500">Loading map...</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto pb-10">
            <div ref={mapAreaRef} className="relative min-w-max">
              <svg
  className="absolute left-0 top-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none z-0 overflow-visible"
                style={{ minHeight: "100%" }}
              >
                <defs>
                  <marker
                    id="arrow-head"
                    markerWidth="10"
                    markerHeight="10"
                    refX="8"
                    refY="3"
                    orient="auto"
                    markerUnits="strokeWidth"
                  >
                    <path d="M0,0 L0,6 L9,3 z" fill="#64748b" />
                  </marker>

                  <marker
                    id="arrow-head-orange"
                    markerWidth="10"
                    markerHeight="10"
                    refX="8"
                    refY="3"
                    orient="auto"
                    markerUnits="strokeWidth"
                  >
                    <path d="M0,0 L0,6 L9,3 z" fill="#f59e0b" />
                  </marker>
                </defs>

                {arrowLines.map((line, index) => {
                  const isRecommended = line.relationType === "RECOMMENDED";
                  const isCorequisite = line.relationType === "COREQUISITE";

                  const strokeColor = isCorequisite ? "#f59e0b" : "#64748b";
                  const markerId = isCorequisite
                    ? "url(#arrow-head-orange)"
                    : "url(#arrow-head)";

                  const midX = (line.x1 + line.x2) / 2;

                  return (
                    <path
                      key={index}
                      d={`M ${line.x1} ${line.y1} L ${midX} ${line.y1} L ${midX} ${line.y2} L ${line.x2} ${line.y2}`}
                      fill="none"
                      stroke={strokeColor}
                      strokeWidth={1.5}
                      strokeDasharray={isRecommended ? "6 5" : undefined}
                      markerEnd={markerId}
                      opacity={0.65}
                    />
                  );
                })}
              </svg>

              <div className="relative z-10 flex gap-12 min-w-max">
                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map((sem) => (
                  <div key={sem} className="w-[200px] flex flex-col gap-4">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b-2 border-slate-900 pb-2 mb-2">
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
                        HK {sem}
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900">
                        {semesters[sem]?.length || 0}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 relative">
                      {semesters[sem]?.map((course) => (
                        <MapCourseCard
                          key={course.courseId}
                          course={course}
                          onClick={() => setDetailsCourseId(course.courseId)}
                        />
                      ))}

                      {(!semesters[sem] || semesters[sem].length === 0) && (
                        <div className="h-24 border border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-slate-300 text-2xl font-light">
                          -
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <CourseDetailsModal
        courseId={detailsCourseId}
        open={!!detailsCourseId}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setDetailsCourseId(null)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
