<file path="src/pages/syllabus/CohortSyllabusDiffPage.tsx">
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

// Dummy data for syllabus
const MOCK_SYLLABUS_DIFF = {
  credits: { old: "3 Tín chỉ", new: "4 Tín chỉ", status: "changed" },
  clos: [
    { code: "CLO1", content: "Understand the fundamentals of regression...", status: "unchanged", oldContent: "Understand the fundamentals of regression..." },
    { code: "CLO2", content: "Apply multiple linear regression models...", status: "unchanged", oldContent: "Apply multiple linear regression models..." },
    { code: "CLO3", content: "Perform logistic regression and interpret...", status: "added", oldContent: null },
    { code: "CLO4", content: "Evaluate model assumptions and fix violations.", status: "removed", oldContent: "Evaluate model assumptions and fix violations." },
    { code: "CLO5", content: "Use R or Python for regression analysis on big data.", status: "changed", oldContent: "Use R for regression analysis." },
  ],
  topics: [
    { name: "Introduction to Linear Regression", status: "unchanged" },
    { name: "Multiple Linear Regression", status: "unchanged" },
    { name: "Model Selection and Regularization", status: "added" },
    { name: "Logistic Regression", status: "changed" },
    { name: "Advanced Topics in Regression", status: "removed" },
  ]
};

// Dummy data for curriculum
const MOCK_CURRICULUM_DIFF = [
  { courseCode: "IT136IU", name: "Regression Analysis", credits: 4, type: "Core", status: "changed", oldCredits: 3 },
  { courseCode: "IT072IU", name: "Machine Learning", credits: 4, type: "Core", status: "unchanged", oldCredits: 4 },
  { courseCode: "IT100IU", name: "Deep Learning", credits: 3, type: "Elective", status: "added", oldCredits: 0 },
  { courseCode: "IT050IU", name: "Introduction to Computing", credits: 3, type: "Core", status: "removed", oldCredits: 3 },
];

export default function CohortSyllabusDiffPage() {
  const [compareType, setCompareType] = useState("syllabus");
  const [selectedCourse, setSelectedCourse] = useState("IT136IU");
  const [selectedMajor, setSelectedMajor] = useState("IT");
  const [baseCohort, setBaseCohort] = useState("K21");
  const [targetCohort, setTargetCohort] = useState("K22");
  const [isComparing, setIsComparing] = useState(false);

  const handleCompare = () => {
    setIsComparing(true);
  };

  // Reset comparison when changing type
  const handleTypeChange = (val: string) => {
    setCompareType(val);
    setIsComparing(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-brand-600 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
            So sánh phiên bản
          </p>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            So sánh giữa các Khóa
          </h1>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-5 gap-4 items-end mb-6">
          <div className="md:col-span-1">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2">Loại so sánh</label>
            <Select value={compareType} onValueChange={handleTypeChange}>
              <SelectTrigger className="bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 font-medium">
                <SelectValue placeholder="Loại..." />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="syllabus">Đề cương học phần</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="curriculum">Bản đồ đào tạo (Curriculum)</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>

          {compareType === "syllabus" ? (
            <div className="md:col-span-1">
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2">Học phần</label>
              <Select value={selectedCourse} onValueChange={setSelectedCourse}>
                <SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn học phần..." />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="IT136IU">IT136IU - Regression Analysis</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="IT072IU">IT072IU - Machine Learning</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="md:col-span-1">
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2">Chuyên ngành</label>
              <Select value={selectedMajor} onValueChange={setSelectedMajor}>
                <SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn chuyên ngành..." />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="IT">Công nghệ thông tin (IT)</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="DS">Khoa học dữ liệu (DS)</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="md:col-span-1">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2">Khóa gốc</label>
            <Select value={baseCohort} onValueChange={setBaseCohort}>
              <SelectTrigger>
                <SelectValue placeholder="Khóa cũ" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="K20">K20</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="K21">K21</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="K22">K22</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-1">
            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2">Khóa đích</label>
            <Select value={targetCohort} onValueChange={setTargetCohort}>
              <SelectTrigger>
                <SelectValue placeholder="Khóa mới" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="K20">K20</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="K21">K21</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="K22">K22</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-1">
            <Button onClick={handleCompare} className="w-full bg-brand-700 hover:bg-brand-800 text-white font-bold h-10">
              SO SÁNH
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isComparing && (
          <div className="mt-8 border-t border-slate-200 pt-8 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-500">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-8 mb-8 bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-1">{baseCohort} (Gốc)</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                  {compareType === "syllabus" ? `Đề cương môn học được áp dụng cho khóa ${baseCohort}` : `Khung chương trình đào tạo áp dụng cho khóa ${baseCohort}`}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-1">{targetCohort} (Đích)</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                  {compareType === "syllabus" ? `Đề cương môn học dự kiến cho khóa ${targetCohort}` : `Khung chương trình đào tạo dự kiến cho khóa ${targetCohort}`}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {compareType === "syllabus" ? (
              /* SYLLABUS DIFF CONTENT */
              <div className="space-y-8">
                {/* General Info */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-8">
                  <div className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">Thông tin chung</h4>
                    <div className="line-through text-red-500 decoration-red-500/50 decoration-2 font-medium">
                      {MOCK_SYLLABUS_DIFF.credits.old}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-400 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">Thông tin chung</h4>
                    <div className="text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1.5 inline-block rounded-md font-bold border border-emerald-200">
                      {MOCK_SYLLABUS_DIFF.credits.new}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* CLOs */}
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 mb-4 uppercase tracking-widest text-sm border-b border-slate-200 pb-2">Course Learning Outcomes (CLOs)</h4>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-8">
                    {/* Base Side */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {MOCK_SYLLABUS_DIFF.clos.map((clo, idx) => {
                        if (clo.status === "added") return <div key={idx} className="p-4 opacity-0 pointer-events-none">Placeholder</div>;
                        const isRemoved = clo.status === "removed";
                        const isChanged = clo.status === "changed";
                        return (
                          <div key={idx} className={`p-4 rounded-xl border ${isRemoved ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-700 line-through decoration-red-500/50 decoration-2' : isChanged ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 text-amber-800' : 'bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                            <strong className="font-bold">{clo.code}:</strong> {clo.oldContent}
                          </div>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                    {/* Target Side */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {MOCK_SYLLABUS_DIFF.clos.map((clo, idx) => {
                        if (clo.status === "removed") return <div key={idx} className="p-4 opacity-0 pointer-events-none">Placeholder</div>;
                        const isAdded = clo.status === "added";
                        const isChanged = clo.status === "changed";
                        return (
                          <div key={idx} className={`p-4 rounded-xl border ${isAdded ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 font-medium' : isChanged ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 font-medium' : 'bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                            <strong className="font-bold">{clo.code}:</strong> {clo.content}
                          </div>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Topics */}
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 mb-4 uppercase tracking-widest text-sm border-b border-slate-200 pb-2">Topics & Lectures</h4>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-8">
                    {/* Base Side */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {MOCK_SYLLABUS_DIFF.topics.map((topic, idx) => {
                        if (topic.status === "added") return <div key={idx} className="p-4 opacity-0 pointer-events-none">Placeholder</div>;
                        const isRemoved = topic.status === "removed";
                        const isChanged = topic.status === "changed";
                        return (
                          <div key={idx} className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl border flex items-center gap-3 ${isRemoved ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-700 line-through decoration-red-500/50 decoration-2' : isChanged ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 text-amber-800' : 'bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                            <div className={`size-2.5 rounded-full ${isRemoved ? 'bg-red-500' : isChanged ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-slate-300'}`}></div>
                            {topic.name}
                          </div>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                    {/* Target Side */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {MOCK_SYLLABUS_DIFF.topics.map((topic, idx) => {
                        if (topic.status === "removed") return <div key={idx} className="p-4 opacity-0 pointer-events-none">Placeholder</div>;
                        const isAdded = topic.status === "added";
                        const isChanged = topic.status === "changed";
                        return (
                          <div key={idx} className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl border flex items-center gap-3 ${isAdded ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 font-medium' : isChanged ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 font-medium' : 'bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                            <div className={`size-2.5 rounded-full ${isAdded ? 'bg-emerald-500' : isChanged ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-slate-300'}`}></div>
                            {topic.name}
                          </div>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              /* CURRICULUM DIFF CONTENT */
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 mb-4 uppercase tracking-widest text-sm border-b border-slate-200 pb-2">Thay đổi trong khung chương trình</h4>
                
                <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-white shadow-sm">
                  <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                    <thead className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
                      <tr>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Mã môn</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Tên môn</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4">Tín chỉ (Cũ &rarr; Mới)</th>
                        <th className="px-6 py-4 text-right">Trạng thái</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                      {MOCK_CURRICULUM_DIFF.map((course, idx) => {
                        const isAdded = course.status === "added";
                        const isRemoved = course.status === "removed";
                        const isChanged = course.status === "changed";

                        return (
                          <tr key={idx} className={`
                            ${isAdded ? 'bg-emerald-50/50' : ''}
                            ${isRemoved ? 'bg-red-50/50' : ''}
                            ${isChanged ? 'bg-amber-50/50' : ''}
                          `}>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-slate-800 font-mono text-xs">{course.courseCode}</td>
                            <td className={`px-6 py-4 font-medium ${isRemoved ? 'line-through text-red-500' : 'text-slate-800'}`}>
                              {course.name}
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-slate-600">
                              {isChanged ? (
                                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                  <span className="line-through text-red-400">{course.oldCredits}</span>
                                  <span>&rarr;</span>
                                  <span className="font-bold text-emerald-600">{course.credits}</span>
                                </span>
                              ) : isAdded ? (
                                <span className="font-bold text-emerald-600">+{course.credits}</span>
                              ) : isRemoved ? (
                                <span className="line-through text-red-400">{course.oldCredits}</span>
                              ) : (
                                course.credits
                              )}
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">
                              {isAdded && <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border border-emerald-200">Thêm mới</span>}
                              {isRemoved && <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-red-100 text-red-700 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border border-red-200">Xóa bỏ</span>}
                              {isChanged && <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border border-amber-200">Thay đổi</span>}
                              {!isAdded && !isRemoved && !isChanged && <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Giữ nguyên</span>}
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        )
                      })}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/syllabus/SyllabusCreatePage.tsx">
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"
import { useState } from "react"
import {
  Card,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
  CardDescription,
  CardContent,
} from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"

import SyllabusForm from "@/components/syllabus/SyllabusForm"
import { useCreateSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useCreateSyllabus"
import { useSyllabuses } from "@/hooks/useSyllabuses"
import { useSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useSyllabus"
import type { CreateSyllabusRequest } from "@/types/syllabus"

export default function SyllabusCreatePage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const createMutation = useCreateSyllabus()

  const [selectedSourceId, setSelectedSourceId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  const { data: allSyllabuses } = useSyllabuses()

  const { data: sourceSyllabus, isFetching: isFetchingSource } = useSyllabus(
    selectedSourceId
  )

  const normalizeCreatePayload = (
    values: CreateSyllabusRequest
  ): CreateSyllabusRequest => {
    return {
      ...values,

      courseId: Number(values.courseId),
      versionNumber: Number(values.versionNumber || 1),
      versionLabel: values.versionLabel || "v1.0",
      academicYear: values.academicYear || "K24",
      createdBy: Number(values.createdBy || 1),
      changeSummary: values.changeSummary ?? "",
      notes: values.notes ?? "",

      clos: values.clos ?? [],
      topics: values.topics ?? [],
      assessments: values.assessments ?? [],
    }
  }

  const handleCreate = (values: CreateSyllabusRequest) => {
    const payload = normalizeCreatePayload(values)

    createMutation.mutate(payload, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        alert("Tạo đề cương thành công!")
        navigate("/dept-head/syllabus")
      },
      onError: (error: any) => {
        console.error("Create syllabus error:", error)
        console.error("Create syllabus response:", error?.response?.data)
        alert(
          "Lỗi khi lưu đề cương: " +
            (error?.response?.data?.message || error.message)
        )
      },
    })
  }

  let initialData: CreateSyllabusRequest | undefined = undefined

  if (sourceSyllabus) {
    initialData = {
      courseId: sourceSyllabus.courseId,
      versionNumber: sourceSyllabus.versionNumber,
      versionLabel: sourceSyllabus.versionLabel,
      academicYear: sourceSyllabus.academicYear,
      createdBy: sourceSyllabus.createdById,
      changeSummary: sourceSyllabus.changeSummary ?? "",
      notes: sourceSyllabus.notes ?? "",
      clos: sourceSyllabus.clos ?? [],
      topics: sourceSyllabus.topics ?? [],
      assessments: sourceSyllabus.assessments ?? [],
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card className="shadow-md border border-slate-100 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900/50">
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
        <div>
          <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary font-heading flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
              className="h-8 w-8 p-0"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="size-5" />
            </Button>
            Tạo đề cương môn học mới
          </CardTitle>

          <CardDescription className="text-slate-500 pl-10">
            Soạn thảo đề cương hoặc sao chép cấu trúc từ các phiên bản cũ đã được duyệt
          </CardDescription>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 w-1/3">
          <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
            Import từ đề cương cũ (Tùy chọn)
          </label>

          <Select
            value={selectedSourceId ? String(selectedSourceId) : undefined}
            onValueChange={(val) => setSelectedSourceId(Number(val))}
          >
            <SelectTrigger className="w-full bg-slate-50 border-slate-200">
              <SelectValue placeholder="-- Chọn đề cương để sao chép --" />
            </SelectTrigger>

            <SelectContent>
              {allSyllabuses?.map((s) => (
                <SelectItem key={s.id} value={String(s.id)}>
                  {s.courseCode} - {s.courseName} (Khóa:{" "}
                  {s.academicYear || "N/A"}, Ver: {s.versionLabel || "N/A"})
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          {isFetchingSource && (
            <span className="text-xs text-amber-500 font-medium mt-1">
              Đang tải dữ liệu...
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="pt-6">
        <SyllabusForm
          key={selectedSourceId || "new"}
          initialData={initialData}
          onSubmit={handleCreate}
          loading={createMutation.isPending}
        />
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/syllabus/SyllabusDetailPage.tsx">
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom"

import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

import { useSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useSyllabus"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { syllabusApi } from "@/api/syllabusApi"

export default function SyllabusDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams()

  const syllabusId = Number(id)

  const {
    data: syllabus,
    isLoading,
    isError,
  } = useSyllabus(syllabusId)

  const { data: otherVersions } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["syllabuses-course", syllabus?.courseId],
    queryFn: () => syllabusApi.getByCourse(syllabus!.courseId),
    enabled: !!syllabus?.courseId,
  })

  const previousVersion = otherVersions
    ?.filter((v) => v.id !== syllabus?.id)
    .sort((a, b) => b.versionNumber - a.versionNumber)[0]

  if (!id || Number.isNaN(syllabusId)) {
    return <div>Invalid syllabus ID</div>
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div>Loading...</div>
  }

  if (isError || !syllabus) {
    return <div>Syllabus not found</div>
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Syllabus Detail</h1>

        {previousVersion && (
          <Link
            to={`/dept-head/syllabus/${syllabus.id}/diff?compareWith=${previousVersion.id}`}
          >
            <Button variant="outline">
              So sánh với V{previousVersion.versionNumber}
            </Button>
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        <p>
          <strong>Course:</strong> {syllabus.courseCode} -{" "}
          {syllabus.courseName}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Version Number:</strong> {syllabus.versionNumber}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Version Label:</strong> {syllabus.versionLabel}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Academic Year:</strong> {syllabus.academicYear}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Status:</strong> {syllabus.status}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Current Version:</strong>{" "}
          {syllabus.isCurrent ? "Yes" : "No"}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Created By:</strong> {syllabus.createdByUsername}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Approved By:</strong>{" "}
          {syllabus.approvedByUsername ?? "-"}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Change Summary:</strong>{" "}
          {syllabus.changeSummary ?? "-"}
        </p>

        <p>
          <strong>Notes:</strong> {syllabus.notes ?? "-"}
        </p>
      </div>
    </Card>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/syllabus/SyllabusDiffPage.tsx">
import { useParams, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { useSyllabusDiff } from "@/hooks/useSyllabusDiff";
import SyllabusToolbar from "@/components/syllabus/SyllabusToolbar"; // Import Toolbar để đồng bộ

export default function SyllabusDiffPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const compareWith = searchParams.get("compareWith");

  // Dữ liệu so sánh được fetch dựa trên ID đề cương chính và ID đề cương cần so sánh
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useSyllabusDiff(Number(id), Number(compareWith));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Giữ lại Toolbar để người dùng có thể điều chỉnh filter mà không làm mất trạng thái so sánh */}
      <SyllabusToolbar />

      {!compareWith ? (
        <Card className="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">
          Vui lòng chọn phiên bản đề cương khác để so sánh.
        </Card>
      ) : isLoading ? (
        <Card className="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">
          Đang tải dữ liệu so sánh...
        </Card>
      ) : isError || !data ? (
        <Card className="p-6 text-center text-rose-500">
          Lỗi tải dữ liệu so sánh.
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <Card className="p-6 shadow-sm border-slate-200">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4 text-slate-800">So sánh đề cương</h1>
          
          <div className="flex gap-4 mb-8">
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-lg flex-1">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-500 block mb-1 text-xs uppercase">Phiên bản cũ:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{data.oldVersionLabel}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-lg flex-1">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-500 block mb-1 text-xs uppercase">Phiên bản mới:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{data.newVersionLabel}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Thay đổi thông tin chung */}
          {Object.keys(data.generalInfoDiff || {}).length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-8">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-3 border-b pb-2">Thay đổi thông tin chung</h2>
              <ul className="space-y-2 pl-2">
                {Object.entries(data.generalInfoDiff).map(([key, diff]: any) => (
                  <li key={key} className="text-sm">
                    <strong className="capitalize">{key}:</strong> 
                    <span className="line-through text-red-500 mx-2">{diff.oldValue}</span> 
                    {" -> "} 
                    <span className="text-green-600 font-bold ml-2">{diff.newValue}</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Thay đổi CLO */}
          {data.cloDiff && (
            <div className="mb-8">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-3 border-b pb-2">Thay đổi CLO</h2>
              
              {data.cloDiff.added?.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4">
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-green-600 mb-2 text-sm flex items-center">✓ Thêm mới:</h3>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 pl-5">
                    {data.cloDiff.added.map((clo: any) => (
                      <li key={clo.code} className="text-sm list-disc"><strong>{clo.code}</strong>: {clo.description}</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}

              {data.cloDiff.removed?.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4">
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-red-500 mb-2 text-sm flex items-center">✗ Đã xóa:</h3>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 pl-5">
                    {data.cloDiff.removed.map((clo: any) => (
                      <li key={clo.code} className="text-sm list-disc"><strong>{clo.code}</strong>: {clo.description}</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}

              {data.cloDiff.modified?.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-blue-600 mb-2 text-sm flex items-center">✎ Đã sửa:</h3>
                  <ul className="space-y-3 pl-5">
                    {data.cloDiff.modified.map((clo: any) => (
                      <li key={clo.code} className="text-sm">
                        <strong className="block mb-1">{clo.code}</strong>
                        <ul className="pl-4 border-l-2 border-slate-200 space-y-1">
                          {Object.entries(clo.changes).map(([key, diff]: any) => (
                            <li key={key}>
                              <em className="text-slate-500">{key}</em>: 
                              <span className="line-through text-red-500 mx-2">{diff.oldValue}</span> 
                              {" -> "} 
                              <span className="text-green-600 font-bold ml-2">{diff.newValue}</span>
                            </li>
                          ))}
                        </ul>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Thay đổi Topic */}
          {data.topicDiff && (
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-3 border-b pb-2">Thay đổi Topic</h2>
              {data.topicDiff.added?.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4">
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-green-600 mb-2 text-sm">✓ Thêm mới:</h3>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 pl-5">
                    {data.topicDiff.added.map((topic: any) => (
                      <li key={topic.name} className="text-sm list-disc"><strong>{topic.name}</strong> (Tuần {topic.weekNumber})</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}
              {data.topicDiff.modified?.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-blue-600 mb-2 text-sm">✎ Đã sửa:</h3>
                  <ul className="space-y-3 pl-5">
                    {data.topicDiff.modified.map((topic: any) => (
                      <li key={topic.name} className="text-sm">
                        <strong className="block mb-1">{topic.name}</strong>
                        <ul className="pl-4 border-l-2 border-slate-200 space-y-1">
                          {Object.entries(topic.changes).map(([key, diff]: any) => (
                            <li key={key}>
                              <em className="text-slate-500">{key}</em>: 
                              <span className="line-through text-red-500 mx-2">{diff.oldValue}</span> 
                              {" -> "} 
                              <span className="text-green-600 font-bold ml-2">{diff.newValue}</span>
                            </li>
                          ))}
                        </ul>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/syllabus/SyllabusEditPage.tsx">
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom"
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"

import SyllabusForm from "@/components/syllabus/SyllabusForm"

import { useSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useSyllabus"
import { useUpdateSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useUpdateSyllabus"

import type { CreateSyllabusRequest } from "@/types/syllabus"

export default function SyllabusEditPage() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const syllabusId = Number(id)

  const { data, isLoading } = useSyllabus(syllabusId)

  const updateMutation = useUpdateSyllabus()

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div>Loading...</div>
  }

  if (!data) {
    return <div>Syllabus not found</div>
  }

  const initialData: CreateSyllabusRequest = {
  courseId: data.courseId,
  versionNumber: data.versionNumber,
  versionLabel: data.versionLabel,
  academicYear: data.academicYear,

  courseDesignation: data.courseDesignation ?? "",
  courseTypes: data.courseTypes ?? "",
  semester: data.semester ?? "",
  language: data.language ?? "",
  relation: data.relation ?? "",
  teachingMethods: data.teachingMethods ?? "",
  workloadTotal: data.workloadTotal ?? "",
  workloadContact: data.workloadContact ?? "",
  workloadPrivate: data.workloadPrivate ?? "",
  prerequisites: data.prerequisites ?? "",
  objectives: data.objectives ?? "",
  examForms: data.examForms ?? "",
  examRequirements: data.examRequirements ?? "",
  rubrics: data.rubrics ?? "",
  major: data.major ?? "",

  createdBy: data.createdById,
  changeSummary: data.changeSummary ?? "",
  notes: data.notes ?? "",
  clos: data.clos ?? [],
  topics: data.topics ?? [],
  assessments: data.assessments ?? [],
}

  const handleUpdate = (values: CreateSyllabusRequest) => {
    updateMutation.mutate(
      {
        id: syllabusId,
        data: values,
      },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          alert("Cập nhật đề cương thành công!")
          navigate("/dept-head/syllabus")
        },
        onError: (error: any) => {
          console.error("Update syllabus error:", error)
          alert(
            "Lỗi khi cập nhật đề cương: " +
              (error?.response?.data?.message || error.message)
          )
        },
      }
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-8 pb-20">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
            {data.courseCode} • {data.academicYear} •{" "}
            {data.versionLabel || "v1.0"}
          </p>

          <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            Form Đề cương Học phần
          </h1>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
          className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
        >
          ← QUAY LẠI DANH SÁCH
        </button>
      </div>

      <SyllabusForm
        initialData={initialData}
        onSubmit={handleUpdate}
        loading={updateMutation.isPending}
      />
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/pages/syllabus/SyllabusListPage.tsx">
import { useMemo } from "react"
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select"
import { Search, Plus, Filter, Copy, Edit2, Trash2, Map } from "lucide-react"
import { useSyllabuses } from "@/hooks/useSyllabuses"
import { useDeleteSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useDeleteSyllabus"
import { useCloneSyllabus } from "@/hooks/useCloneSyllabus"
import { useAuthStore } from "@/store/authStore"
import type { Syllabus } from "@/types/syllabus"

const getNotesData = (item: Syllabus) => {
  if (!item.notes) return {}

  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(item.notes)
    return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" ? parsed : {}
  } catch {
    return {}
  }
}

const getMajor = (item: Syllabus) => {
  return item.major || "N/A"
}

const getSemester = (item: Syllabus) => {
  return item.semester || "N/A"
}

export default function SyllabusListPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError } = useSyllabuses()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const user = useAuthStore((state) => state.user)
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams()

  const activeCohort = searchParams.get("cohort") || "all"
  const searchVal = searchParams.get("q") || ""
  const majorVal = searchParams.get("major") || "all"
  const semesterVal = searchParams.get("semester") || "all"

  const updateFilter = (key: string, value: string) => {
    const newParams = new URLSearchParams(searchParams)

    if (value && value !== "all") {
      newParams.set(key, value)
    } else {
      newParams.delete(key)
    }

    setSearchParams(newParams)
  }

  const deleteMutation = useDeleteSyllabus()
  const cloneMutation = useCloneSyllabus()

  const filteredData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data) return []

    return data.filter((item) => {
      const itemMajor = getMajor(item)
      const itemSemester = getSemester(item)

      if (searchVal) {
        const query = searchVal.toLowerCase().trim()
        const codeMatch = (item.courseCode || "")
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query)
        const nameMatch = (item.courseName || "")
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query)
        const nameVnMatch = ((item as any).courseNameVn || "")
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query)
        const instructorMatch = (item.createdByUsername || "")
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query)

        if (!codeMatch && !nameMatch && !nameVnMatch && !instructorMatch) {
          return false
        }
      }

      if (majorVal !== "all" && itemMajor !== majorVal) return false
      if (semesterVal !== "all" && itemSemester !== semesterVal) return false

      if (
        activeCohort !== "all" &&
        (item.academicYear || "").toLowerCase().trim() !==
        activeCohort.toLowerCase()
      ) {
        return false
      }

      return true
    })
  }, [data, searchVal, majorVal, semesterVal, activeCohort])

  const dynamicCohorts = useMemo(() => {
    const baseCohorts = ["K20", "K21", "K22", "K23", "K24", "K25"]

    if (!data) return baseCohorts

    const all = new Set(baseCohorts)

    data.forEach((item) => {
      if (item.academicYear) {
        all.add(item.academicYear.trim())
      }
    })

    return Array.from(all).sort()
  }, [data])

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-64 items-center justify-center">
        <div className="text-lg font-medium text-slate-400 animate-pulse">
          Đang tải danh mục đề cương...
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (isError) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/5 p-6 text-center text-destructive">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-2">Đã xảy ra lỗi</h3>
        <p>Không thể tải dữ liệu đề cương môn học. Vui lòng thử lại sau.</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const handleDelete = (id: number) => {
    if (window.confirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn xóa đề cương này không?")) {
      deleteMutation.mutate(id, {
        onSuccess: () => alert("Đã xóa đề cương thành công!"),
        onError: () => alert("Xóa đề cương thất bại."),
      })
    }
  }
  const handleViewCurriculumMap = () => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams()

    if (activeCohort !== "all") {
      params.set("academicYear", activeCohort)
    }

    if (majorVal !== "all") {
      params.set("major", majorVal)
    }

    navigate(`/curriculum-map?${params.toString()}`)
  }
  const handleClone = (id: number) => {
    if (
      window.confirm(
        "Bạn muốn nhân bản đề cương này thành một phiên bản mới (Nháp)?"
      )
    ) {
      cloneMutation.mutate(
        {
          id,
          userId: user?.userId || 1,
        },
        {
          onSuccess: (newSyllabus) =>
            navigate(`/dept-head/syllabus/${newSyllabus.id}/edit`),
          onError: () => alert("Nhân bản thất bại."),
        }
      )
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#FDFDF9] min-h-screen -m-6 p-6 md:-m-10 md:p-10 font-sans text-slate-900">
      <div className="max-w-[1400px] mx-auto space-y-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-slate-400">SCSE</span> / QUẢN LÝ ĐỀ CƯƠNG
            </p>

            <h1 className="text-3xl font-light tracking-tight text-slate-800">
              Danh mục{" "}
              <span className="font-bold text-amber-600">
                Đề cương Học phần
              </span>
            </h1>
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <Button
              onClick={handleViewCurriculumMap}
              className="bg-amber-600 text-white hover:bg-amber-700 font-semibold shadow-sm h-10 px-5 rounded-lg transition-all"
            >
              <Map className="mr-2 size-4" /> Xem bản đồ đào tạo
            </Button>

            <Link to="/dept-head/syllabus/create">
              <Button className="bg-slate-900 text-white hover:bg-slate-800 font-semibold shadow-sm h-10 px-5 rounded-lg transition-all">
                <Plus className="mr-2 size-4" /> Tạo đề cương mới
              </Button>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/60 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-600 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
            <Filter className="size-4" /> BỘ LỌC
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-4 items-center">
            <div className="relative w-full lg:flex-1">
              <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 size-4 text-slate-400" />

              <Input
                className="pl-9 bg-slate-50/50 border-slate-200/80 h-10 rounded-lg"
                placeholder="Tìm theo mã, tên môn, giảng viên..."
                value={searchVal}
                onChange={(e) => updateFilter("q", e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex w-full lg:w-auto items-center gap-3">
              <Select
                value={activeCohort}
                onValueChange={(v) => updateFilter("cohort", v)}
              >
                <SelectTrigger className="w-full lg:w-[160px] h-10 rounded-lg">
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Khóa" />
                </SelectTrigger>

                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="all">Khóa: Tất cả</SelectItem>
                  {dynamicCohorts.map((c) => (
                    <SelectItem key={c} value={c}>
                      {c}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>

              <Select
                value={majorVal}
                onValueChange={(v) => updateFilter("major", v)}
              >
                <SelectTrigger className="w-full lg:w-[200px] h-10 rounded-lg">
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Ngành" />
                </SelectTrigger>

                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="all">Tất cả ngành</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="Khoa học Máy tính">
                    Khoa học Máy tính
                  </SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="Kỹ thuật phần mềm">
                    Kỹ thuật phần mềm
                  </SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="Khoa học Dữ liệu">
                    Khoa học Dữ liệu
                  </SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>

              <Select
                value={semesterVal}
                onValueChange={(v) => updateFilter("semester", v)}
              >
                <SelectTrigger className="w-full lg:w-[140px] h-10 rounded-lg">
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Học kỳ" />
                </SelectTrigger>

                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="all">Tất cả HK</SelectItem>
                  {[
                    "Semester 1",
                    "Semester 2",
                    "Semester 3",
                    "Semester 4",
                    "Semester 5",
                    "Semester 6",
                    "Semester 7",
                    "Semester 8",
                  ].map((sem) => (
                    <SelectItem key={sem} value={sem}>
                      {sem}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200/60 rounded-xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-500 uppercase text-[11px] font-bold tracking-widest">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-left">Mã môn</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-left">Tên học phần</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-left">Khóa</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-left">Ngành</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-left">Học kỳ</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-left">Trạng thái</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-left">Người tạo</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 text-right">Thao tác</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filteredData.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td
                      colSpan={8}
                      className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-slate-400"
                    >
                      Không có đề cương nào phù hợp với bộ lọc.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredData.map((item) => (
                    <tr
                      key={item.id}
                      className="hover:bg-amber-50/30 transition-colors"
                    >
                      <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold text-slate-800">
                        {item.courseCode}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4">
                        <div className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
                          {item.courseName}
                        </div>

                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                          Version: {item.versionLabel || "N/A"}
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                        {item.academicYear || "N/A"}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                        {getMajor(item)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                        {getSemester(item)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4">
                        <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">
                          {item.status}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                        {item.createdByUsername || "N/A"}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4">
                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => handleClone(item.id)}
                            className="h-8 w-8 p-0"
                          >
                            <Copy className="size-4" />
                          </Button>

                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() =>
                              navigate(`/dept-head/syllabus/${item.id}/edit`)
                            }
                            className="h-8 w-8 p-0"
                          >
                            <Edit2 className="size-4" />
                          </Button>

                          <Button
                            variant="ghost"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => handleDelete(item.id)}
                            className="h-8 w-8 p-0 text-red-500 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

// Dashboard Placeholder

interface Props {

export default function DashboardPlaceholder({ title }: Props) {

<file path="src/pages/LoginPage.tsx">
import { useState } from "react"
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { AlertCircle, GraduationCap, Mail, Lock, Eye, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react"

import { loginRequest } from "@/api/authApi"
import { useAuthStore } from "@/store/authStore"
import type { UserRole } from "@/types/auth"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label"
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from "@/components/ui/alert"

import logoImg from "@/assets/logo.jpg"

// Role-based redirect map
const ROLE_HOME: Record<UserRole, string> = {
  ADMIN: "/admin",
  DEAN: "/dean",
  PROGRAM_COORDINATOR: "/coordinator",
  DEPT_HEAD: "/dept-head",
  INSTRUCTOR: "/instructor",
  STUDENT: "/student",
}

export default function LoginPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const setAuth = useAuthStore((state) => state.setAuth)

  const [username, setUsername] = useState("")
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("")
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false)

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: loginRequest,
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      setAuth(data)
      navigate(ROLE_HOME[data.role] ?? "/")
    },
  })

  function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    mutation.mutate({ username, password })
  }

  const errorMessage =
    (mutation.error as any)?.response?.data?.message ||
    (mutation.error ? "Sai tài khoản hoặc mật khẩu." : null)

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen bg-[#f0f6fc] font-sans">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <header className="bg-[#004b87] text-white py-4 px-8 shadow-md">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="bg-white/10 p-2 rounded-lg backdrop-blur-sm border border-white/20">
            <GraduationCap className="h-8 w-8 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">SCSE Curriculum Management System</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-blue-100 font-medium">Manage Curriculum • Syllabus • CLO-PLO Matrix</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-6">
        <div className="w-full max-w-5xl bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] flex flex-col md:flex-row overflow-hidden border border-slate-100">
          
          {/* Left Pane - Illustration & Info */}
          <div className="hidden md:flex flex-col w-1/2 bg-[#e8f4fc] p-10 justify-between">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 tracking-wider uppercase">Curriculum Mapping</span>
              <h2 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#003b6b] leading-tight">
                From Course Outcomes to Program Outcomes
              </h2>
              <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm leading-relaxed">
                Centralized platform for curriculum management, syllabus approval and accreditation reporting.
              </p>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center mt-8">
              <img 
                src={logoImg} 
                alt="Logo" 
                className="max-w-full h-auto drop-shadow-lg mix-blend-multiply rounded-xl"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right Pane - Login Form */}
          <div className="w-full md:w-1/2 p-8 md:p-12 lg:p-16 flex flex-col justify-center">
            <div className="mb-8">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#003b6b] mb-2">Sign In to Your Account</h2>
              <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Use your institutional credentials to continue.</p>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
              <div className="space-y-2 relative">
                <Label htmlFor="username" className="text-slate-700 font-semibold text-sm">Email Address</Label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <Input
                    id="username"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="lecturer@hcmiu.edu.vn"
                    className="pl-10 h-11 border-slate-200 focus-visible:ring-[#004b87]"
                    value={username}
                    onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                    required
                    autoFocus
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2 relative">
                <Label htmlFor="password" className="text-slate-700 font-semibold text-sm">Password</Label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <Input
                    id="password"
                    type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                    placeholder="Enter your password"
                    className="pl-10 pr-10 h-11 border-slate-200 focus-visible:ring-[#004b87]"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    required
                  />
                  <button 
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                    className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-slate-600">
                  <input type="checkbox" className="rounded border-slate-300 text-[#004b87] focus:ring-[#004b87] w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer" />
                  <span>Remember Me</span>
                </label>
                <a href="#" className="text-[#004b87] font-semibold hover:underline">
                  Forgot Password?
                </a>
              </div>

              {errorMessage && (
                <Alert variant="destructive" className="bg-red-50 border-red-200 text-red-800">
                  <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" color="#991b1b" />
                  <AlertTitle className="text-red-800 font-semibold">Đăng nhập thất bại</AlertTitle>
                  <AlertDescription className="text-red-700">{errorMessage}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              )}

              <Button
                type="submit"
                className="w-full h-11 bg-[#004b87] hover:bg-[#003b6b] text-white font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all shadow-md hover:shadow-lg"
                disabled={mutation.isPending}
              >
                {mutation.isPending ? "Signing in..." : (
                  <>
                    Sign In <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </form>

            <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                By signing in you agree to the SCSE academic systems acceptable use policy.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="py-6 text-center bg-white/50 backdrop-blur-sm border-t border-slate-200">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#004b87]">School of Computer Science and Engineering</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">International University — VNU HCM - Version 1.0</p>
      </footer>
    </div>
  )
}
</file>

<file path="src/routes/ProtectedRoute.tsx">
import { Navigate, Outlet } from "react-router-dom"
import { useAuthStore } from "@/store/authStore"
import type { UserRole } from "@/types/auth"

interface ProtectedRouteProps {
  allowedRoles?: UserRole[]
}

export default function ProtectedRoute({ allowedRoles }: ProtectedRouteProps) {
  const { isAuthenticated, user } = useAuthStore()

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  }

  if (allowedRoles && user && !allowedRoles.includes(user.role)) {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />
  }

  return <Outlet />
}
</file>

<file path="src/store/authStore.ts">
import { create } from "zustand"
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware"
import type { AuthUser, LoginResponse } from "@/types/auth"

interface AuthState {
  accessToken: string | null
  user: AuthUser | null
  isAuthenticated: boolean
  setAuth: (data: LoginResponse) => void
  logout: () => void
}

export const useAuthStore = create<AuthState>()(
  persist(
    (set) => ({
      accessToken: null,
      user: null,
      isAuthenticated: false,

      setAuth: (data) =>
        set({
          accessToken: data.accessToken,
          isAuthenticated: true,
          user: {
            userId: data.userId,
            username: data.username,
            email: data.email,
            role: data.role,
            instructorId: data.instructorId,
          },
        }),

      logout: () =>
        set({
          accessToken: null,
          user: null,
          isAuthenticated: false,
        }),
    }),
    {
      name: "auth-storage", // key trong localStorage
    }
  )
)
</file>

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from "react-router-dom"
import type { ReactElement } from "react"
import LoginPage from "@/pages/LoginPage"
import DashboardPlaceholder from "@/pages/DashboardPlaceholder"

import CurriculumMapPage from "@/pages/program/CurriculumMapPage"

import DeptHeadApprovalPage from "@/pages/admin/DeptHeadApprovalPage"
import FacultyDashboardPage from "@/pages/admin/FacultyDashboardPage"

const ROLE_HOME: Record<UserRole, string> = {
  ADMIN: "/admin",
  DEAN: "/dean",
  PROGRAM_COORDINATOR: "/coordinator",
  DEPT_HEAD: "/dept-head",
  INSTRUCTOR: "/instructor",
  STUDENT: "/student",
}

function RootRedirect() {
  const { isAuthenticated, user } = useAuthStore()
  if (!isAuthenticated || !user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />
  return <Navigate to={ROLE_HOME[user.role] ?? "/login"} replace />
}

const PAGE_OVERRIDES: Record<string, React.ComponentType> = {
  "/admin": AdminDashboardPage,
  "/admin/users": UserManagementPage,
  "/admin/departments": DepartmentManagementPage,
  "/admin/programs": ProgramManagementPage,
  "/admin/courses": CourseManagementPage,
  "/admin/class-sections": ClassSectionManagementPage,
  "/admin/instructors": InstructorManagementPage,
  "/admin/reports": ReportManagementPage,
  "/admin/audit-log": AuditLogPage,
  "/admin/settings": SystemSettingsPage,
  "/admin/syllabus": SyllabusListPage,
  "/admin/syllabus/diff": CohortSyllabusDiffPage,
  "/admin/syllabus/curriculum-map": SyllabusCurriculumMapPage,

  "/dean": DeanDashboardPage,
  "/dean/reports": ReportManagementPage,
  "/dept-head": DeptHeadDashboardPage,
  "/instructor": FacultyDashboardPage,
  "/coordinator/curriculum-map": CurriculumMapPage,

  "/dept-head/courses": CourseManagementPage,
  "/dept-head/approvals": DeptHeadApprovalPage,
  "/dept-head/syllabus": SyllabusListPage,
  "/dept-head/syllabus/diff": CohortSyllabusDiffPage,
  "/dept-head/syllabus/curriculum-map": SyllabusCurriculumMapPage,
  "/dept-head/instructors": InstructorManagementPage,
  "/instructor/syllabus": SyllabusListPage,
}

function buildRoleRoutes(role: UserRole) {
  const routes = []

  NAV_CONFIG[role].forEach((item) => {
    // Add parent route
    const Component = PAGE_OVERRIDES[item.path]
    routes.push(
      <Route
        key={item.path}
        path={item.path}
        element={
          Component ? (
            <Component />
          ) : (
            <DashboardPlaceholder title={item.label} />
          )
        }
      />
    )

    // Add children routes
    if (item.children) {
      item.children.forEach((child) => {
        // Skip if child path is same as parent (already added)
        if (child.path === item.path) return

        const ChildComponent = PAGE_OVERRIDES[child.path]
        routes.push(
          <Route
            key={child.path}
            path={child.path}
            element={
              ChildComponent ? (
                <ChildComponent />
              ) : (
                <DashboardPlaceholder title={child.label} />
              )
            }
          />
        )
      })
    }
  })

  return routes
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<RootRedirect />} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />

        <Route element={<ProtectedRoute />}>
          <Route element={<DashboardLayout />}>
            <Route
              path="/curriculum-map"
              element={<SyllabusCurriculumMapPage />}
            />
            <Route element={<ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["ADMIN"]} />}>
              {buildRoleRoutes("ADMIN")}
            </Route>
            <Route element={<ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["DEAN"]} />}>
              {buildRoleRoutes("DEAN")}
            </Route>
            <Route
              element={
                <ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["PROGRAM_COORDINATOR"]} />
              }
            >
              {buildRoleRoutes("PROGRAM_COORDINATOR")}
            </Route>
            <Route element={<ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["DEPT_HEAD"]} />}>
              {buildRoleRoutes("DEPT_HEAD")}
            </Route>
            <Route element={<ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["INSTRUCTOR"]} />}>
              {buildRoleRoutes("INSTRUCTOR")}
            </Route>
            <Route element={<ProtectedRoute allowedRoles={["STUDENT"]} />}>
              {buildRoleRoutes("STUDENT")}
            </Route>
            <Route
              path="/dept-head/syllabus/:id"
              element={<SyllabusDetailPage />}
            />

            <Route
              path="/dept-head/syllabus/:id/edit"
              element={<SyllabusEditPage />}
            />
            <Route
              path="/dept-head/syllabus/:id/diff"
              element={<SyllabusDiffPage />}
            />
            <Route
              path="/dept-head/syllabus/create"
              element={<SyllabusCreatePage />}
            />

            {/* Admin Syllabus Routes */}
            <Route
              path="/admin/syllabus/:id"
              element={<SyllabusDetailPage />}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/syllabus/:id/edit"
              element={<SyllabusEditPage />}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/syllabus/:id/diff"
              element={<SyllabusDiffPage />}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/syllabus/create"
              element={<SyllabusCreatePage />}
            />
            <Route
              path="/admin/programs/:id/diff"
              element={<ProgramDiffPage />}
            />
          </Route>
        </Route>

        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />


      </Routes>
    </BrowserRouter>
  )
}

content = content.replace('const errors: string[] = [];', 'const errors: {id: string, name: string}[] = [];')

for old, new_s in replacements:
    content = content.replace(old, new_s)

# Update scroll logic
old_scroll = """    if (errors.length > 0) {
      setValidationErrors(errors);
      alert("Vui lòng điền đầy đủ thông tin! Kéo lên đầu trang để xem danh sách các trường còn thiếu.");
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      return;
    }"""

new_scroll = """    if (errors.length > 0) {
      setValidationErrors(errors);
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        const firstErrorEl = document.getElementById(errors[0].id);
        if (firstErrorEl) {
          firstErrorEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
          firstErrorEl.focus();
        }
      }, 100);
      return;
    }"""

content = content.replace(old_scroll, new_scroll)

# Render validation alert
old_render_alert = """          <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-sm text-red-600">
            {validationErrors.map((err, idx) => (
              <li key={idx}>{err}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>"""

new_render_alert = """          <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-sm text-red-600">
            {validationErrors.map((err, idx) => (
              <li key={idx} className="cursor-pointer hover:underline" onClick={() => {
                const el = document.getElementById(err.id);
                if (el) {
                  el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
                  el.focus();
                }
              }}>{err.name}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>"""

content = content.replace(old_render_alert, new_render_alert)

# Now, add IDs and error classes to the inputs!
field_mapping = {
    'value={courseNameEn}': 'id="courseNameEn"',
    'value={courseNameVn}': 'id="courseNameVn"',
    'value={courseCode}': 'id="courseCode"',
    'value={instructor}': 'id="instructor"',
    'value={creditsTheory}': 'id="creditsTheory"',
    'value={creditsPractice}': 'id="creditsPractice"',
    'value={ects}': 'id="ects"',
    'value={periodsTheory}': 'id="periodsTheory"',
    'value={periodsPractice}': 'id="periodsPractice"',
    'value={courseDesignation}': 'id="courseDesignation"',
    'value={semester}': 'id="semester"',
    'value={language}': 'id="language"',
    'value={relation}': 'id="relation"',
    'value={teachingMethods}': 'id="teachingMethods"',
    'value={workloadTotal}': 'id="workloadTotal"',
    'value={workloadContact}': 'id="workloadContact"',
    'value={workloadPrivate}': 'id="workloadPrivate"',
    'value={prerequisites}': 'id="prerequisites"',
    'value={objectives}': 'id="objectives"',
    'value={examForms}': 'id="examForms"',
    'value={examReqs}': 'id="examReqs"',
    'value={rubrics}': 'id="rubrics"',
}

# Add helper function for dynamic class
helper_func = """  const getErrorClass = (id: string) => {
    return validationErrors.some(e => e.id === id) ? "border-red-500 ring-red-500 bg-red-50/50" : "";
  }
  
  return ("""

content = content.replace('  return (', helper_func)

for search_str, id_str in field_mapping.items():
    lines = content.split('\n')
    for i in range(len(lines)):
        if search_str in lines[i] and 'className={inputClasses}' in lines[i]:
            field_id = id_str.split('"')[1]
            lines[i] = lines[i].replace('className={inputClasses}', f'id="{field_id}" className={{cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("{field_id}"))}}')
    content = '\n'.join(lines)

# For Textareas (inputClasses -> textareaClasses if there's any, else skip)
# For Selects: major, academicYear
content = content.replace(
    '<SelectTrigger className={inputClasses}>\n                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn khóa" />',
    '<SelectTrigger id="academicYear" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("academicYear"))}>\n                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn khóa" />'
)
content = content.replace(
    '<SelectTrigger className={inputClasses}>\n                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn chuyên ngành" />',
    '<SelectTrigger id="major" className={cn(inputClasses, getErrorClass("major"))}>\n                    <SelectValue placeholder="Chọn chuyên ngành" />'
)

# For CourseTypes (Checkbox group)
content = content.replace(
    '<div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">',
    '<div id="courseTypes" className={cn("grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3 p-2 rounded-lg transition-all", getErrorClass("courseTypes"))}>'
)

# For CLOs table
content = content.replace(
    '<table className="w-full text-sm text-left border-collapse">',
    '<table id="clos" className={cn("w-full text-sm text-left border-collapse transition-all", getErrorClass("clos") && "ring-2 ring-red-500")}>'
)

# For Topics table
content = content.replace(
    '<table className="w-full text-sm text-left">',
    '<table id="topics" className={cn("w-full text-sm text-left transition-all", getErrorClass("topics") && "ring-2 ring-red-500")}>'
)

# For Assessments table
content = content.replace(
    '<table className="w-full text-sm text-center border-collapse">',
    '<table id="assessments" className={cn("w-full text-sm text-center border-collapse transition-all", getErrorClass("assessments") && "ring-2 ring-red-500")}>'
)


with open('E:/Luận Văn Tốt Nghiệp/Code/frontend/src/components/syllabus/SyllabusForm.tsx', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    f.write(content)

print("Done")
</file>

<file path="README.md">
# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:

```js
export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...

      // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
      tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
      // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
      tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
      // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
      tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,

      // Other configs...
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])
```

You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://github.com/Rel1cx/eslint-react/tree/main/packages/plugins/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://github.com/Rel1cx/eslint-react/tree/main/packages/plugins/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:

```js
// eslint.config.js
import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...
      // Enable lint rules for React
      reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
      // Enable lint rules for React DOM
      reactDom.configs.recommended,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])
```
</file>

import path from "path"

import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react"
import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/vite"

// https://vite.dev/config/
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": path.resolve(__dirname, "./src"),
    },
  },
})
